eṉa virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mutaleḻuttāntaṉmi avaṟṟiṟkiṉmiyāṉum cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟutalāṉum ippattuñ cārpākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭu meṉpatu eṉa mayilinātarum, uyirmey uyirum meyyuṅ kūṭip piṟattalāṉum, āytam uyirpōla alaku peṟṟum meypōlap peṟātum puṭiyottu avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē cārntu varutalāṉum, ēṉiya tattam mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripu vikārattāṟ piṟattalāṉum cārpeḻut tāyiṉa veṉak koḷka eṉac caṅkara namaccivāyap pulavarum kāraṇaṅ kūṟic ceṉṟaṉar.
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟi yalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟiyum cārpeḻuttiṉuḷ aṭak kiyatu, ivi yātāṉum orucollic cārntu variṉallatu taṉiyē olittu niṟkum iyalpiṉṟi mutaleḻuttukkaḷic cārntu varum iyalpupaṟṟiyēyām. āciriyar cārntu variṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta eṉimūṉṟum eṉa (piṟap-19-l) viḷakkip pōtalāṉum ivaṟṟiyalpu taṉiyē vara viyalātu oṉṟaṉic cārntu varutalē yeṉpatu pulaṉām. eṉavē taṉṉiyalpiṉ niṟṟalāṟṟātaṉavāy moḻiyic cārntu varum iyalpuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷē cārpeḻuttātal tollāciriyar tuṇipeṉpatu tēṟṟam.
īṇṭu naṉṉūlārāṟ cārpil cērkkappaṭum uyir meyyeḻuttukkaḷ, uyirum meyyumeṉa vēṟu niṉṟa viṭattum taṉiyē niṟṟaṟkuriya mutaleḻuttāy niṟṟalāṉum, uyirumeyyuṅ kūṭi niṟṟal poruḷuṇarttum poruṭṭaṉṟit taṉiyē niṟṟalāṟṟāt taṉmi paṟṟi yaṉṟākalāṉum, uyirumeyyumākiya mutaleḻuttukkaḷiraṇṭum oṉṟupaṭṭa niliyil akkūṭṭat tiṉiyuṇarttiya peyarē uyirmey eṉpataṉṟi, muppateḻuttiṉum vēṟupaṭṭa eḻuttolikaḷi appeyar kuṟittateṉṟal vaḻakkaṉmiyāṉum, meyyum uyiruṅ kūṭutalākiya kūṭṭampaṟṟi vēṟupaṭṭateṉiṉ `mey yōṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu vitikaḷ vēṇṭappaṭāmiyāṉum mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ vēṟākavittu uyirmeyyi (uyirēṟiya meyyi)c cārpeḻutteṉṟal poruntāteṉka. iṉi uyiraḷapeṭiyiṉi mūvaḷapicikkum vēṟeḻuttākakkoṇṭu cārpeḻuttuḷ aṭakkutalum poruttamaṟṟatām. eṉṉi? āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār aḷapeṭiyiṉit taṉiyē ōreḻutteṉak koṇṭila reṉpatu,
mūvaḷapicitta lōreḻuttiṉṟē
nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷapuṭiya
kūṭṭiyeḻūuta leṉmaṉār pulavar          		    (naṉ. nūṉmarapu 5,6)
eṉa varum cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ naṉku pulappaṭum. ōreḻuttu mūṉṟu māttiriyāka icittaliṉṟu, oli nīṭci vēṇṭuvōr ataṟ kēṟṟa iyalpuṭiya eḻuttukkūṭṭi iciyiṉi eḻuppikkoḷka eṉpatē mēṟkāṭṭiya cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷ. itaṉāl ōreḻuttiṉ oli nīṭciyi vēṇṭuvōr avvoliyit tarutaṟkuriya oṟṟumiyuṭiya eḻuttolikaḷik kūṭṭikkōṭalē aḷapeṭiyāmeṉap peṟap paṭutaliṉ, aḷapeṭi iraṇṭu mutaliya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kūṭṭoliyēyaṉṟit taṉiyē ōreḻuttaṉ ṟeṉpatu āciriyar karuttātaluṇarka. oṟṟaḷapeṭikkum iḵtokkum.
eṉavē mutaleḻuttukkaḷil iṉamuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vēṟākāta aḷapeṭikaḷic cārpeḻuttiṉuḷaṭakkuvatu iyipuṭittaṉṟu. ākavē uyiraḷapeṭi oṟṟaḷapeṭiyām ivaṟṟiyum, eḻutteṉappaṭum mutaleḻuttukkaḷ puṇarcci yiṟ paṭṭaviṭattu uṇṭām vikāraṅkaḷākiya aikārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum pavaṇantiyār cārpeḻut teṉac cārttiyurittal cārpeḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇattiṟku muṟṟum māṟātal orutali.
iṉi im mūṉṟumēyaṉṟi uyirmey mutaliyavaṟṟiyuñ cārpeḻutteṉpārum uḷarālō veṉiṉ eṉa viṉā veḻuppikkoṇṭu, āl eṉpuḻi uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉāṟpōla, lā eṉpuḻi meymuṉṉum uyirpiṉṉum niṉṟu mayaṅkiṉavēyallātu, uyirum meyyumākiya tantaṉmi tirintu vēṟākāmikku mey yōṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyaltiriyā eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattuc cūttiraṅkaḷē cāṉṟākalāṉ, uyir meyyākiya kālattum, kuṟiṉmi, neṭiṉmi eṉṉum uyirt taṉmiyum, vaṉmi, meṉmi, iṭimi eṉṉum meyt taṉmiyum taṉṉiyal piṟṟiripu paṭāmiyāṉum, uṭalmēl uyir vantoṉṟutal  poṉmaṇipōla iyalpu puṇarcci yeṉpavāka lāṉum....... tuṇaṅkiyeṉpatu meymutal uyirīṟu meym mayakka meṉavum, varaku eṉpatu uyirttoṭar  moḻik kuṟṟiyalukarameṉavum koḷvataṉṟi, uyirmey mutal uyirmeyyīṟu, uyirmeymayakkam, uyirmeyt toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉak koḷḷāmiyiṉ oṟṟumi nayampaṟṟi oṉṟeṉpataṉāl oru payaṉiṉmi yāṉum..... uyirōṭu kūṭiyaviṭattu varivaṭivu vēṟupaṭutaliṉ atupaṟṟi puḷḷiyillā ellā meyyum eṉa meymmēl vittuc cūttirañceytu vaṭiveḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāraṉṟi oliyeḻuttilakkaṇam vēṟupaṭak kūṟāmiyāṉum, aḷapeṭi cārpeḻutteṉa vēṟākāmi muṉṉark kāṭṭappaṭṭatākalāṉum, aikārak kuṟukkam mutaliyaṉa oru kāraṇam paṟṟik kuṟukiya vākaliṟ ciṟumaram peruttuḻiyum, perumaram ciṟuttuḻiyum vēṟoru maramākātavāṟu pōla vēṟeḻutteṉap paṭāvākalāṉum....... atu poruntāteṉa maṟukka. vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu varuvaḻik kāl māttiriyāyk kuṟukutaliṉ atupaṟṟi ataṉik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ vēṟeṉṉāmiyiṉ avarkkum (pavaṇanti yārkkum) atu karuttaṉṟu pōlumeṉka, eṉa āciriyar civañāṉa muṉivarum cūttira viruttiyuḷ kūṟi maṟuttami īṇṭu vittuṇaraṟ pā ṟṟu.
		avaṟṟuḷ, a i u
e o eṉṉum appālintum
ōraḷa picikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻutteṉpa.           			  (tol. 3)
itu, mēl eḻutteṉappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ oru cāraṉavaṟṟiṟkuriya māttiriyaḷavum kāraṇap peyarum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟiya muppateḻuttiṉuḷ a, i, u, e, o eṉpaṉa aintum ōrōvoṉṟu oru māttiriyāka olikkum kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṉum peyaruṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṟṟeḻuttu - kuṟitākiya eḻuttu eṉap paṇputtoki; iḵtu oru māttiriyākiya kuṟumiyōciyāṟ peṟṟa kāraṇappeyar. ōriṉapporuḷkaḷiṉuḷḷēyē kuṟumi. neṭumi koḷḷappaṭumākaliṉ, ik kuṟṟeḻuttiṉuṅ kuṟintu meykaḷ ari māttiriyāka olippaṉavāyiṉum avvari māttiriyiṉum mikkolippaṉavaṟṟit tammuṭ peṟāmiyiṉ kuṟṟeḻut teṉappaṭāvāyiṉa eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		ā ī ū ē ai
ō au eṉṉum appālēḻum
īraḷapicikku neṭṭeḻutteṉpa.                 		   	(tol. 4)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, au eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa akkūṟṟu ēḻum iraṇṭu māttiriyaḷavāka olikkum neṭṭeḻut teṉṉum peyariṉa eṉṟu colvar āciriyar.
aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ kuṟiya eḻuttiṉ neṭiyavātaṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttākiya iṉan tamakkilliyeṉiṉum māttiriyop pumiyāṉ avi neṭṭeḻutteṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉpar urikārar.
ivviru cūttiraṅkaḷilum āciriyar a, i, u, e, o eṉṉum aintum kuṟṟeḻutteṉap peyar peṟumeṉṟum, ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, au eṉṉum ēḻum neṭṭeḻutteṉap peyar peṟumeṉṟum kūṟiṉār, ivaṟṟip piṉvaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷil eṭuttāḷutaṟ poruṭṭu, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟa ivaṟṟi ippeyarkaḷāṟ kūṟutalēyaṉṟik kuṟṟeḻuttik `kuṟiyatu (tol 38,226, 234) eṉṟum neṭṭeḻutti `neṭiyatu (tol. 060,401) eṉṟum cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
iṉi naṉṉūlār
		avaṟṟuḷ,
a, i, u, e, o k kuṟilintē.
ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, au neṭil.                 			(naṉ. 64, 65)
eṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟavaṟṟi muṟiyē kuṟil, neṭil, eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
ivar kuṟil neṭil ākiya peyarkaḷi uyireḻut tiṟkum, uyir meyyeḻuttiṟkum, uriyaṉavākak koṇṭu, uyirkkuṟil. uyirneṭil ākiya iraṇṭaṉiyum kuṟṟuyir, neṭṭuyir eṉac ciṟappuppeyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar. (naṉ. 126) mēl āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu eṉap peyar kūṟavanta viṭattu appeyarkkāraṇam viḷaṅka ōraḷapicikkum kuṟṟeḻuttu, īraḷapicikkum neṭṭeḻuttu eṉa avaṟṟiṉ māttirikaḷiyum uṭampoṭu puṇarttuk kūṟiṉār. ellā eḻuttukkaḷukkum māttiri kūṟum pakutiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ māttiri ilakkaṇam kūṟumvaḻi naṉṉūlār iyittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
		mūvaḷapicittal ōreḻuttiṉṟē.               			   (tol. 5)
mēlē iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷilum oru māttiriyāka icikku meḻuttukkaḷ kuṟṟeḻutteṉavum, iraṇṭu māttiriyāka icikku meḻuttukkaḷ neṭṭeḻutteṉavum kūṟiyavaḻi, mūṉṟu māttiriyaḷavāka olikkum ōreḻuttu muṇṭō eṉṟu aiya nikaḻumākaliṉ ataṉiyakaṟṟa eḻuntatu iccūttirameṉka.
(i-ḷ) ōreḻuttē niṉṟu mūṉṟu māttiriyaḷavāka olittalilli (eṉṟavāṟu)
eṉavē palaveḻuttuk kūṭiyaviṭattu mūṉṟu māttiri yum nāṉku māttiriyum icikkum eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		nīṭṭamvēṇṭiṉ avvaḷapuṭiya
kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.                 			  (tol. 6)
mēl ōreḻuttu mūṉṟu māttiriyaḷavāka olitta lilli yeṉavē iraṇṭu māttiri peṟṟa eḻuttu, ōciyum poruḷum kāraṇamāka nīṇṭolittali vēṇṭiṉ, eṉ ceyvateṉṟārkku itaṉāl māttiri nīḷumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) iraṇṭu māttiri peṟṟa eḻuttu am māttiri yiṉ mikkolittali virumpuvarāyiṉ am māttiriyiṉit tarutaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷik kūṭṭiyeḻuppuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar - eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṉāl neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ tāmē iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉ mikku mūṉṟu māttiriyaḷavāka ōcimikku nillā eṉpatum, aṅṅaṉam avi mūṉṟu māttiri mutalāka nīṇṭolittali vēṇṭuvōr avaṟṟiṟku iṉamotta māttiriyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷikkūṭṭi icittal vēṇṭu meṉpatum āciriyar karuttātal peṟappaṭum. aṅṅaṉam kūṭṭiyeḻuppumāṟu kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ ai au veṉṉum (moḻi marapu 8,9) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ kūṟappaṭum.
avvaḷapuṭiya eṉap paṉmi kūṟiyavataṉāṉ otta ōreḻuttēyaṉṟi `ceṟāaay vāḻiya eṉṟāṅku iraṇṭeḻut tiṉik kūṭṭi nāṉku māttiri kōṭalum taḻuvik koḷḷap paṭṭatu. ivvāṟu nāṉku māttiriyāka īraḷapu peṟutal ceyyuṭkaḷōci citiyuṅkāl īraḷapum aiyappāṭiṉṟi yaṇiyumām eṉa māpurāṇattuṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
kūṭṭiyeḻuutal eṉpataṟku kūṭṭiyeḻuppuka eṉa muṉṉiyōruritta vaḻiyē poruḷkoḷḷātu, `kūṭṭi yeḻutuka eṉap poruḷ koḷvārumuḷar. eḻu eṉṟa taṉviṉippakuti iṟutiyukaram nīṇṭu aḷapupeṟṟu niṉṟatākaliṉ, `eḻutuka eṉa `eḻutu eṉpataṉip pakutiyāy vitturittal poruntātu. aṉṟiyum iraṇṭu māttiri peṟṟa eḻuttu ōci kuṉṟuvatāṉa coṟkaṇ mūṉṟu māttiriyāy niṉṟu ōci peṟavēṇṭiṉ ataṉōṭu otta kuṟṟeḻuttu ataṉ piṉṉar niṉṟu ataṉ ōciyi niṟivikkum eṉṟa aḷapeṭic cūttirattōṭu itu māṟupaṭṭu, varivaṭivil iṉaveḻutti aṟikuṟi aḷavākak kūṭṭi eḻututa loṉṟē ōci nīṭaṟkuriya vaḻiyām eṉap poruḷ tantu āciriyar karutti mayaṅka vippatākum.
kaṇṇimi noṭiyeṉa avvē māttiri
nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē.               		   (tol. 7)
itu, mēl eḻutteṉappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu eṉappeyar kūṟiyavaḻi uṭampoṭu puṇartta lāṉ avaṟṟaḷapāka māttiriyum kūṟappaṭṭatākaliṉ iyipu paṭṭami karuti māttiri iṉṉateṉa ataṉ aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kaṇṇimittalum, kinnoṭiyum, ākiya aviyē oru māttirikku aḷavākum. itu nuṇṇitāka ōci yiyalpiṉi yuṇarnta āciriyar kaṇṭaneṟi eṉṟavāṟu.
īṇṭu imiyeṉṟatu imittaṟṟoḻili yeṉavum noṭiyeṉṟatu noṭiyiṟ piṟanta ōciyi yeṉavum uriyāciriyar kūṟuvar.
kaṇṇimittalum koṭiyōciyum iṅku iyaṟṟamiḻā ciriyar eḻuttōciyiṉ elliyāka amittuk koṇṭa ōraḷapiṟku (oru māttirikku) aḷavātal kūṟappaṭṭatu.
piṉṉar, mey mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku ariyaḷapu eṉak kūṟap paṭum aḷavu, itaṉiṟ pātiyātalum, makarak kuṟukkam mutaliya vaṟṟikkuk kūṟappaṭum aḷavu ippātiyiṟ pātiyātalumuṇarka.
cārntu varutaliṉṟit taṉiyēvarum akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuti yākavuḷḷa muppateḻuttiṉ tokiyiṉi mutaṟ cūttirattuk kūṟi, 2-l cārpeḻuttiṉ peyarum muṟiyuṅ kūṟiya āciriyar 3,4 -l kuṟṟeḻut tiviyeṉavum neṭṭeḻut tivi yeṉavum avaṟṟiṉ māttiri yivvaḷaveṉavuṅ kūṟippōntu, 5-l ōreḻuttiṉ māttiri variyaṟiyiyum, 6-l oci kuṟintāl ataṉi nīṭṭi niṟivikkum muṟiyiṉiyum uṇartti, ic cūttirattāl ammāttiri yilakkaṇamum kūṟip pōntār.
ic cūttirattu varum `eṉa eṉpatu eṇṇiṟ pirintu kaṇṇimiyeṉa noṭiyeṉa eṉṟu iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭiṟṟu. avvē eṉpataṉ ēkāram, aḷavu karuvikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum iviyē oru māttiri yiyalpiṉi viḷaṅka uṇarttuvaṉa eṉap pirinili kuṟittatu.
ivvāṟē naṉṉūlārum eḻuttoli yeḻucci palavaṟṟiyum aḷantu kōṭaṟkuriya kāla aḷaviṉi,
		iyalpeḻu mānta rimi noṭi māttiri  		   (naṉ. 100)
eṉpataṉāl viḷaṅka urittār. iyalpāka eḻum māntaratu imippoḻutum noṭippoḻutum oru māttirikku aḷavākum eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
imittaṟṟoḻilum noṭiyōciyumākiya ivi yiraṇṭum ākupeyarāyk kālatti yuṇartti niṉṟaṉa eṉpar caṅkara namaccivāyap pulavar. eṉavē imi noṭi yeṉpaṉa vaṟṟiṟku imippoḻutum noṭip poḻutum eṉap poruḷ koṇṭu appoḻutiṉi māttirikku aḷavākki yurippatē poruntuvatām.
		aukāra viṟuvāyp
paṉṉīreḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa.          			   (tol. 8)
itu muṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭa kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻut tākiyavaṟṟit tokuttu vēṟōr kuṟiyiṭukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) akara mutalāka aukāramīṟākak kiṭanta paṉṉi reṇṭeḻuttum uyireṉṉum peyariṉiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
akara mutalāṉavaṟṟi muṉṉar eṭutturittārātaliṉ muṉṉarp peṟappaṭṭataṉiyē aukāra viṟuvāy eṉa īṟoṉṟē kūṟi vaḻimoḻintār. 
mey  patiṉeṭṭaṉiyum iyakkit tāṉ aruvāy vaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyirāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ṉakaraviṟuvāyp
patiṉeṇṇeḻuttum meyyeṉa moḻipa.           		  (tol. 9)
iḵtu uyirallaṉavaṟṟit tokuttu vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kakāramutal ṉakāramīṟākak kiṭanta patiṉeṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷum meyyeṉṉum peyariṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉṟavāṟu.
paṉṉīruyirukkun tāṉ iṭaṅkoṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkun taṉmi peṟṟa uṭampāy niṟṟaliṉ meyyeṉpatu uvama ākupeyar.
mēṟ cūttirattu aukāra viṟuvāy eṉak kūṟip pōntā rātaliṉ īṇṭu `ṉakara viṟuvāy eṉṟē kūṟiṉum aukārattiṟ kaṭutta kakaram mutal eṉpatu tāṉē peṟappaṭumātaliṟ kūṟā toḻintār.
akaramutal aukāramīṟām paṉṉiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷum ulakattu uṭali yiyakkum uyirpōla ēṉip patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷiyum iyakkit tām aviyiṉṟiyum iyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭimi kaṇṭu, avi paṉṉiraṇṭiṉiyum uvama ākupeyarāṉ uyir eṉak kuṟiyiṭṭum, uyiriṉṟi yiyaṅkā ulakattu ēṉiyuṭalkaḷpōla iviyiṉṟi yiyaṅkāk kakaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāyp patiṉeṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiyum av ākupeyarāṉ meyyeṉak kuṟiyiṭṭum vaḻaṅkiya paṇṭit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāciriyariṉ oli nūlaṟivu ivvulakattu em moḻiyiṉuṅ kāṇappaṭāta taṉic ciṟappuṭiyateṉap pērāciriyar palar kūṟupa.
ivvāṟu taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭiyavāy avvāṟṟa lillāta patiṉeṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi yiyakkiyum varum paṉṉiraṇ ṭeḻuttiṉiyum uyireṉavum, av vāṟṟalillātu avaṟṟāṉiyakkappaṭum patiṉeṭṭeḻuttiṉiyum meyyeṉa vum muṉṉiyōr moḻipa eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār vēṟupirittuk kūṟutalāl avarkku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ uṭaliyiyakkum āṟṟaloṉṟuṇṭeṉak koṇṭu ataṉi uyireṉavum, av uyirāl iyakkappaṭum uṭali meyyeṉavuṅ kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatum, eṉavē uyir vēṟu, uṭal vēṟu eṉṉuṅ koḷki avar tamakku uṭaṉpāṭeṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
uyir āvi yeṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa. mey uṭampu eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
ammuta līrā ṟāvi kammutal
meym mūvāṟeṉa viḷampiṉar pulavar. 		    (naṉ. 63)
eṉac cūttirañceytār. itaṉāl akara mutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭum āvi (uyir) eṉavum, kakara mutaliya patiṉeṭṭum meyyeṉavum peyarpeṟumeṉa viḷakkiṉār. ippeyar, yāṉiṭṭataṉṟu muṉṉiyōriṭṭa muṟimitteṉpār viḷampiṉar pulavar eṉṟār.
āviyum meyyum pōṟaliṉ ivviruvaki yeḻuttuk kaḷum uvamavāku peyarāyk kāraṇap potup peyarāyiṉa veṉpar caṅkara namaccivāyappulavar.
meyyō ṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā.       		 (tol. 10)
iḵtu uyirmeykku aḷavu kūṟutal nutaliṟ ṟeṉpar uri yāciriyar.
(i-ḷ) paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meyyōṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟaṉavāyiṉum tam aḷavum, kuṟiyum, eṇṇum tirintu nillā eṉṟavāṟu.
uyirum meyyum kūṭukiṉṟa uyirmeyk kūṭṭattiṉi meyyōṭiyiyiṉum eṉa uyirmēl vittuk kūṟiyatu, avvuyiriṉ māttiriyē itaṟku māttiriyākak kūṟu kiṉṟami nōkkippōlum eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
uyir mey eṉṉum peyar, uyirum meyyum kūṭi niṉṟa niliyiṟ peṟṟatāyiṉum akkūṭṭattu, maṟṟataṉōṭu iyitalākiya viṉiyiṉi uyiriṉ taṉi viṉiyākkik kūṟiyatu, taṉṉiliyil taṉittiyaṅkun taṉmi uyirkkēyaṉṟit taṉi meykku iṉmiyiṉ avvāṟṟalillā meyyōṭu ceṉṟu poruntun taṉicciṟappu uyirkkē urittātal karutiyeṉka. iṅṅaṉam meyyum uyirum kūṭiya niliyiṉa tākiya uyirmey yeḻuttukkaḷ, meyyiṉaḷapum uyiraḷapum peṟṟu oṉṟari māttiriyum iraṇṭari māttiriyumāka niṟkavēṇṭiyaṉa, oru māttiriyāyum iraṇṭu māttiriyāyum olittu niṟṟaṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkūṟap pukunta nacciṉārk kiṉiyar, āyiṉ oṉṟari māttiriyum iraṇṭari māttiriyumuṭiyaṉa orumāttiriyum iraṇṭu māttiriyum āyavāṟu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, nīr taṉittu aḷantuḻiyum nāḻiyāy, ari nāḻi yuppiṟ kalantuḻiyum kūṭi oṉṟari nāḻiyāy mikāta vāṟu pōlvatōr poruṭpeṟṟi yeṉṟu koḷvatallatu kāraṇaṅ kūṟalākāmiyuṇarka eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. oru nāḻi nīrilē arināḻi uppikkalantāl aḵtu oṉṟari nāḻiyāy mikātavāṟu pōlvatōr poruṭ peṟṟiyi uyirmey yaḷaviṟku uvami kūṟippōnta itaṉkaṇ, nāḻi nīriṟ kalakkappaṭṭa ari nāḻiyuppu nīrōṭu kalantu oṉṟari nāḻiyāy mikātu oru nāḻiyaḷavuṭpaṭumāyiṉum avvari nāḻiyuppiṉ parumi, oru nāḻi nīriṟ kalantu, muṉ avvaḷavu niṟiyillāta nīr, muṉṉilum niṟiyiṉāṟperukutalpōla, meyyiṉ māttiri uyiraḷaviṟpaṭṭu aṭaṅkiṉum, akkūṭṭattup poruṭpeṟṟiyāl meyyiṉatu ōciyum eṇṇum ataṉōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭu aḻiyātu niṟkumeṉpatu koḷḷakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. ulakattu uyirkaḷiṉ uṭampāṉatu, uyirvaḻiyā yaṭaṅki ataṉ virivu peṟṟu niṟṟal pōla, immeyyeḻuttum uyireḻuttaḷaviṟṟāy aṭaṅki ataṉ oliyōṭu ottolikkumeṉpatu, uyirmey eṉṟa niṟiyuvamap peyarāl naṉku viḷaṅkum.
		meyyi ṉaḷapē ariyeṉamoḻipa.           			  (tol. 11)
itu taṉi meykku aḷapu (māttiri) kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) meyyiṉatu aḷapuari māttiriyeṉac colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
īṇṭu ariyeṉakkūṟiyatu muṉṉark kaṇṇimi kinnoṭi yaḷavākak kūṟappaṭṭa ōraḷapiṟ pātiyiṉiyē. nāc ciṟitu puṭipeyaruntaṉmiyāy niṟṟaliṉ av varimāt tiriyun taṉittuk kūṟikkāṭṭalākātu eṉpar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.
		avviyaṉiliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē.            			  (tol. 12)
itu cārpiṟṟēṟaṟṟattu mūṉṟaṟkum aḷapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oḻinta cārpiṟṟōṟṟattuvarum kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷum muṟkūṟiya ari māttiriyākiya avviyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṟpaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.
		ariyaḷapu kuṟukal makaramuṭittē
iciyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅkāli.                		  	 (tol. 13)
itu meykaḷuḷ makaram taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟintu cila viṭattu varumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) taṉ arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟuki varutali makara meyyuṭittu; ārāyuṅ kālattu atu vēṟeḻuttiṉatu ōciyiṉ kaṇ ciṟupāṉmiyāki varum. eṉṟavāṟu.
āciriyar iṅṅaṉam makaramey taṉ ari māttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukivarumeṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiṉār. uri yāciriyar iḷampūraṇar ataṉ māttiric curukkattiṉi variyaṟuttu elli kūṟutal vēṇṭi ariyaḷapu kuṟukal makaramuṭittē	eṉpataṟku ariyaḷapākiya elliyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttiri yātali makaramey yuṭittu eṉa uriyiṟ kōṭalāl  kālmāttiri peṟumeṉa variyaṟi kūṟiyuḷḷār. itaṉit taḻuviyē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum naṉṉūlār mutaliya piṉṉūlārum makarak kuṟukkattiṟkuk kāl māttiri yelliyātali variyaṟuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
`kālkuṟaṇmaḵkāṉ	                		        (eḻuttiyal -4)
eṉpatu naṉṉūl cūttirattoṭar.
u-m pōṉm, maruṇm, tarumvaḷavaṉ, eṉavarum.
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvākummē.             			  (tol. 14)
itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceykiṟa teṉakkoṇṭu uriyāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum `puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉap poruḷurippar. itaṉkaṇ pakaravaṭiviyum, makaravaṭiviyum cērttaṟkuriya iṉṟiyamiyāmi yum, ic cūttirattiṉkaṇ aṅṅaṉam cērtturittār āciriyar eṉpataṟ kuriya coṟkiṭak kiyum kāṇappaṭāmiyāṉ ic cūttirattāṟ cuṭṭappaṭṭa uṇmipporuḷ atuveṉak koḷḷaṟkilli. pa.ma. mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ varivaṭivu iṉṉateṉak kiḷantu kūṟāmalē āciriyar avaṟṟiṉ varivaṭivu vēṟu pāṭṭik kūṟiṉāreṉṟal poruntātatām. aṉṟiyum pa ma ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭurikkap pōnta āciriyar tām evveḻuttukkaḷukku varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟārō avveḻuttukkaḷ iviyeṉa eṭutturittaṉṟik kūṟa māṭṭār. makaram mēlic cūttirattāṟ peṟappaṭiṉum, ataṉōṭu vēṟupāṭu kūṟa eṭuttukkoṇṭa pakarattiṉi ic cūttirattu eṭutturiyāmalē avviraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭu kūṟiṉāreṉṟāl eṅṅaṉam poruntum?
iṉi iccūttiravari taṉiyē oru cūttiramāka iruntirut taliyalātu. eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, itaṉuṭ kūṟappaṭum viti iṉṉataṟ keṉpatu peṟappaṭāta niliyil itaṉi oru cūttiramāka āciriyar viyār. piṉ varum meyyiṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal eṉṟa cūttiram meykaḷiṉ potuviyal purittalāl ivvari ataṉit toṭarntatākātu. eṉavē ittoṭar, mēlic cūttirattiṉiyē cērntatātal vēṇṭum.  ākaliṉ itu makarattōṭu makarak kuṟukkattiṟku varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceykiṉṟatu eṉpatē poruttamuṭittu.
(i-ḷ) uṭpeṟu puḷḷi - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum - makarak kuṟukkattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.
mēlic cūttirattu ari māttiriyiṉiṉṟum kuṟuki iciyiṭaṉaruki varumeṉappaṭṭa makarak kuṟukkattiṉi, avvāṟu kuṟukāta ari māttiri peṟṟu iyalpāṉa makarattōṭu pirittaṟintu koḷḷutaṟku aṟikuṟiyāka varivaṭivil ellā meykaḷukkumuriya puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyoṭu, ikkuṟukiya makara meykku varivaṭivil uḷḷē peṟum puḷḷiyē poruntiya vaṭivām eṉpatik kuṟikkavē iccūttiram mēlic cūttirattōṭu iyittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. ic cūttiram makarak kuṟukkattip paṟṟiyatāyiṉum varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi kūṟutal kāraṇamāka vēṟē pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu; oru poruḷ nutaliyatē cūttiramātaliṉ  eṉka.
meykaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya makarattiṉ kuṟukiya niliyi aṟintu koḷvataṟkeṉavē akkālattār, ellā meykaḷum peṟutaṟ kuriyatāya talippuḷḷiyōṭu kuṟukiya makarattiṉ uḷḷēyum oru puḷḷiyi iṭṭu vari vaṭivil ataṉik kuṟittuppōntārkaḷ eṉpatu iccūttirattāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. ōci kuṟukiya eḻuttiṉip puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭum muṟi uṇṭeṉpatu meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal eṉṟataṉ vitiyik kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipeṉa aṭutta cūttirattil māṭṭeṟintu `īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum meypōlap puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkum eṉak kūṟutalāṟ peṟappaṭum. ivvāṟu taṉṉiyalpām oru māttiriyiṉiṉṟum kuṟuki ari māttiriyiyalpil niṟkum kuṟṟiyalukaram īṟṟil puḷḷipeṟutal pōlat taṉṉari māttiriyiṟ kuṟinta makaramum puḷḷi peṟutal iyalpum muṟiyum ākum.
ivvāṟē makarak kuṟukkam iyalpākiya puḷḷiyoṭu uḷḷēyoru puḷḷiyiyum peṟumeṉpataṉi vīracōḻiyam cantippaṭalam 19-m ceyyuḷ muṉ vayiṟ kālvavvariṉ varumoḻi mutalil vakaram vantu puṇarntāl anta makaramāṉatu kuṟukik kālmāttiriyāy uṭpuḷḷipeṟum eṉap perun tēvaṉār uri kūṟip pōtalāṉum naṉkaṟiyalām.
		meypi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal           		  (tol. 15)
mēl makarattiṟkum makarak kuṟukkattiṟkum vari vaṭivu vēṟṟumikku aṟikuṟiyāka makarak kuṟukkattiṟku uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvākum eṉṟār. āṇṭup puḷḷi peṟutal atikārap paṭṭamiyiṉ, ellā meykaḷukkum potuvākiya puḷḷi peṟutali itaṉāṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. iḵtu uyirmeyyōṭu taṉimeyyiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceytal nutaliṟṟu eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
(i-ḷ) meykaḷiṉ taṉmiyāvatu puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟṟalām.
		ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē.             			 (tol. 16)
ituvum varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉatu niliyum meypōlap puḷḷipeṟum iyalpiṟṟu.
paṇṭik kālattu e,ē ākiya kuṟil neṭil iraṇ ṭiṟkum e, eṉṟa vaṭivum o,ō ākiya kuṟil neṭiliraṇṭiṟkum o eṉṟa vaṭivum varivaṭivil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ kuṟilitu neṭilitu eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu uṇartaṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttik kuṟikkumiṭattu avviraṇṭaṉmēlum puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutiṉār kaḷeṉpatum ivvitiyāṟ pulaṉām. ivvāṟu puḷḷiyiṭutal ekara okaraṅkaḷukkēyaṉṟi meyyūrnta ekara okaraṅkaḷukkum uṇṭeṉpatu tantivarmaṉ āṭciyil 4-m āṇṭil  (ki.pi. 800-840) tiruveḷḷaṟikkiṇaṟoṉṟil veṭṭappaṭṭa cācaṉattu nāṉ kāvateṭuttu, peruṅkiṇaṟu, ceytu eṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ meyyūrnta ekaraṅkaḷ puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutappeṟṟiruttalāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mēliyiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷilēyum kuṟikkappaṭṭa meykaḷum ekara okaramum puḷḷi peṟutalākiya vitiyi, eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ uruvam eṉṟa pakutiyil
tolli vaṭiviṉa vellāveḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytu mekara okara meypuḷḷi.                    			    (naṉ. 98)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟippōntār naṉṉūlār.
ekaramum okaramum puḷḷi peṟutalip piṉ varum māt tiric curukka aṇiyaminta ceyyuṭkaḷālum naṉkaṟiyalām.
nēriḻiyār kūntaliṉōr puḷḷipeṟa nīṇmaramām
 nīrniliyōr puḷḷipeṟa neruppām.
mayirniṟuvi maṟṟataṟkōr puḷḷi koṭuppiṉ
 ceyirtīrmaramākum ceṉṟu                          (naṉ. cū. 268, mēṟ)
itaṉkaṇ nēriḻiyār kūntal, mayir eṉpaṉa ōti eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. ataṉkaṇ oti eṉappuḷḷi koṭuttāl oti eṉṟa marattik kuṟikkum. avvāṟē nīrniliyeṉpatu ēri. ataṉ ēkārattum puḷḷiyiṭṭāl eri eṉa neruppi yuṇarttum.
eḻuttiṉatu uruvattik kuṟikka vanta naṉṉūlār, makarak kuṟukkam, kuṟṟiyalukaram, ivi puḷḷi peṟumeṉṟu tollāciriyar kūṟiya varivaṭivi iṟantatu vilakkal eṉṉum, uttiyāl kūṟātu vilakkiṉār pōlum.
ikkālattu ekara okaram eṉpaṉa, makarakkuṟukkam kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpaṉapōlap puḷḷi peṟutali yoḻintaṉa. ekara okaraṅkaḷōṭu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceyya eṇṇiya piṟkālattār kuṟilkaḷiṉ taliyiṟ puḷḷiyiṭum paḻiya muṟiyiṉi māṟṟi neṭilkaḷiṉ kōṭṭiṉik kīḻvilaṅkupōlac ciṟitu vaḷitteḻututali vaḻakkamākak koḷvārāyiṉar.
puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum
uruvuru vāki yakaramo ṭuyirttalum
ēṉi yuruvō ṭuruvutirin tuyirttalum
āyī riyala vuyirtta lāṟē.                  				  (tol. 17)
iḵtu uyirumeyyuṅ kūṭumāṟuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ellā meykaḷum puḷḷiyilli yāmpaṭiyāka, tattam muṉṉi vaṭivē piṉṉum vaṭivākavē akarattōṭu kūṭiyolittalum, oḻinta uyirkaḷōṭu vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭu olittalum ākiya avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya; avi yolikku muṟimikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
1. puḷḷiyilliyātal - tattam muṉṉi vaṭivē vaṭivāka akarattōṭuyirttal. ka,ṅa,ya eṉa varutal.
2. ēṉiyuyirōṭu uruvu tirintu varutalāvatu, mēluṅkīḻum, puḷḷipeṟṟum, puḷḷiyuṅkōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟum uyirttalām. ki,kī mutaliyaṉa mēl vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ku, kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. kā,ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyi ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. ko, kō, ṅo, ṅō eṉpaṉa puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟaṉa eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iṅṅaṉan tirintolippavē uyirmey paṉṉiru patiṉeṭṭu iru nūṟṟorupattāṟu āyiṉa.
uyirmey eṉpataṉi oṟṟumi koḷvaḻi ummit tokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyeṉavum, vēṟṟumi koḷvaḻi ummit tokiyeṉavuṅ koḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. āciriyar, uyirmeyyeṉpataṉi vēṟṟumi nayaṅ karutiyē kūṟukiṉṟārākaliṉ, ummit tokiyeṉak koṇṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. cūttirattil `illāka eṉpatu `illā eṉa niṉṟatu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
iṅṅaṉam mey uyirōṭu kūṭi olikkum niliyil uyiraḷavāy aṭaṅki niṟṟal kāraṇamāka meyyōci āṇṭut tōṉṟātu eṉpārumuḷar. meyyōṭiyiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā eṉpataṉāl uyirmeyk kūṭṭattut tiriyā uyiriyalpiṉi viḷakkiya āciriyar, akkūṭṭattu meykaḷ uyiroli niṟiyap peṟṟu ataṉōṭu ataṉ aḷavāy ataṉmuṉ olikkumiyalpiṉa eṉpataṉi iccūttirattāl teḷivākak kuṟittuḷḷār.
		meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu niliyē.         		 (tol. 18)
uyirum meyyum kalantu uyiraḷavāy olikkum poḻutu avviraṇṭaṉuḷ muṉṉolippatetu piṉṉolippatetu eṉpataṉi itaṉuṭ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) meyyum uyiruṅ kalantu uyiraḷavāy olikkum poḻutu meyyoliyiṉ piṉṉarē uyiroli tōṉṟi niṟku meṉpatām.
itaṉāl uyirmeyk kūṭṭattu mey muṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāka olittu niṟṟal kūṟappaṭṭatu.
iṅṅaṉam āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār vēṟṟumi nayaṅkaruti uyirmeyyilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉārāka, naṉṉūlāciriyar pavaṇantiyār, uyirmeyyi oṟṟumi nayaṅ karuti oreḻuttākak koṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvar.
		puḷḷiviṭ ṭavvoṭu muṉṉuru vākiyum
ēṉi yuyirō ṭuruvu tirintum
uyiraḷa vāyataṉ vaṭivoḻit tiruvayiṟ
peyaroṭu moṟṟumuṉ ṉāyvarum uyirmey.       	    (naṉ. 89)
mey puḷḷiyi viṭṭu akarattōṭu kūṭiyavaḻi viṭṭavuruvē uruvākiyum, oḻinta uyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiyavaḻi uruvu vēṟupaṭṭum, taṉmāttiri tōṉṟātu uyir māttiriyē māttiriyāy, ataṉ vari vaṭiviṉatu vikāravaṭivē vaṭivāy uyir vaṭivi yoḻittu, meyyuyi reṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum piṟanta uyir meyyeṉṉum peyaruṭaṉē, oṟṟoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉumāy varum uyirmeyyeḻuttu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. itaṉkaṇ uyirmeyyi ōreḻutteṉak koṇṭu viti kūṟuvatum ataṉic cārpiṟṟōṟṟattu ōreḻutteṉa aṭakkiyurippatum uyirmeyyi ōreḻut tākak koṇṭaṉar pavaṇantiyār eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttum. ivarē piṉṉar eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mutalīṟurikkap pōntaviṭattu.
		niṉṟa neṟiyē uyirmey mutalīṟē.         			   (naṉ. 109)
eṉa vēṟṟumi nayaṅkaruti viti kūṟic cellutalum nōkkat takkatu.
		valleḻutteṉpa, 	ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa, ṟa.		
		melleḻutteṉpa, 	ṅa ña ṇa, na, ma, ṉa.	
		iṭiyeḻutteṉpa, 	ya, ra, la, va, ḻa, ḷa.
(tol. 19,20,21)
immūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷālum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa meykaḷi vaṉmi, meṉmi, iṭimiyākiya piṟappiyaṟ pirivāṉ vēṟupaṭuttu kiṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
valleṉṟicittalāṉum valleṉṟa talivaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum valleḻutteṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉavum, melleṉ ṟicittalāṉum, melleṉa mūkku vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum melleḻutteṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉavum, iṭi nikaraṉavāki olittalāṉum iṭi nikarttākiya miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum iṭiyeḻutteṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉavum uriyāciriyar kūṟuvar.
vaṉmiyu meṉmiyum uṇarntaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ iṭip paṭṭa nilimi yuṇaralākāmiyiṉ, iṭi yeḻutteṉap piṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
valliṉattuk ka, ca, ta, pa nāṉkum, melliṉattu ña, na, ma mūṉṟum, iṭiyiṉattu ya, va iraṇṭum moḻikku mutalātal nōkki immuṟiyē vikkappaṭṭaṉaveṉavuṅ kūṟuvar.
valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṟa peyarāl vakippaṭuttiya ivaṟṟi
		valliṉam 	ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa, ṟa veṉavāṟē.		
		melliṉam 	ṅa, ña, ṇa, na, ma, ṉa, veṉavāṟē.
		iṭiyiṉam 	ya, ra, la, va, ḻa, ḷa. veṉavāṟē.
(naṉ. 68,69,70)
eṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷāl muṟiyē valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar naṉṉūlār.
		am mūvāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
meymmayak kuṭaṉili teriyuṅ kāli.            		 	 (tol. 22)
itu taṉimey mayakkattiṟkup peyar kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu mūṉṟu kūṟākap pakukkappaṭṭa patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷum tammi moḻippaṭuttu vaḻaṅkumiyal puḷatākumiṭattu meymayakkeṉṟum uṭaṉiliyeṉṟum iru vakiyām ārāyumiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūṉṟiṉiyum uṟaḻcci vakiyāṉ uṟaḻa oṉpatu uḷavāmaṉṟē. avaṟṟuḷ taṉimeyyōṭu taṉimey mayakkam oṉṟē kūṟiyatu eṉṉeṉil, maṟṟavaṟṟiṟku variyaṟiyiṉmiyiṉ variyaṟiyuṭiya taṉimey mayakkamē kūṟi yoḻintāreṉa vuṇarka. meyyeṉṟataṉāl taṉimeyyōṭu uyirmey mayakkamaṉṟi, taṉimeyyōṭu taṉimey mayakkamātal koḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
uṭaṉiliyi uṭaṉili mayakkameṉak kūṟuvar iḷampūraṇar. meymmayakkuṭaṉili eṉṟa ivar pāṭatti viṭuttu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar meymmayaṅkuṭaṉili eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu meymayaṅkum nili, uṭaṉ mayaṅkum nili eṉa īriṭattum mayaṅkunili eṉṟa coṟkaḷikkūṭṭi mey mayaṅku nili = taṉimeytaṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa piṟameyyōṭum taṉ meyyōṭum mayaṅkuniliyum, uṭaṉ mayaṅkunili = ap patiṉeṭṭum uyiruṭaṉē niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭum taṉi meyyōṭum mayaṅkum niliyum eṉa iraṇṭām, eṉap poruḷkūṟi, ic cūttiram taṉimey piṟameyyōṭum taṉ meyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum, uyirmey uyirmeyyōṭum taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉak karutturippar. ivarkku muṉṉavarāya iḷampūraṇar koṇṭa meymayakkuṭaṉili eṉṟa pāṭamē toṉmi karuti ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭuva tākalāṉum, uyirmey, uyirmeyyōṭum taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkattiṟku variyaṟiyiṉmiyiṉ ataṟku viti kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟākalāṉum avar kūṟiyavāṟu meym mayaṅku nili eṉpataṉuḷ mey piṟameyyōṭum taṉ meyyōṭum kalantu niṟkunili eṉpatu viḷaṅkāmi yāṉum, uyirmeyyi uyirum meyyum eṉa īreḻuttākap pirittu viti kūṟuvār īṇṭu ōreḻuttākak koṇṭu uriyārākalāṉum, uṭaṉili yeṉpataṟku uyiruṭaṉ niṟṟal eṉak kōṭaṟkuriya colliṉmiyāṉum iḷampūraṇar pāṭamē āciriyar karuttātal peṟappaṭum.
āciriyar taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkuniliyi meymmayakku eṉavum, taṉimey taṉ meyyōṭu mayaṅki niṟkum niliyi uṭaṉili yeṉavum ic cūttirattiṟ pakutturittal kāṇalām.
moḻiyiṭi niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mayakkam uṇarttap pōnta naṉṉūlār, uyiruṭaṉ uyirkku mayaṅkun taṉmi yiṉmiyiṉ ataṉiyoḻittu meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkutalum, uyirum meyyum māṟi mayaṅkalumākiya ivviraṇṭiṉiyum iṭinili mayakkattil uṇartta eṭuttuk koṇṭār.
		ka, ca, ta, pa, voḻinta īrēḻaṉ kūṭṭam
meymmayak kuṭaṉili raḻavoḻit tīreṭ
ṭākum ivvirupāṉ mayakkum moḻiyiṭi
mēvum uyirmey mayakkaḷa viṉṟē.          			   (naṉ. 110)
(i-ḷ) mey patiṉeṭṭaṉuḷ ka, ca, ta, pa, eṉṉum nāṉkum oḻittoḻinta patiṉāṉku meyyum, piṟa meykaḷōṭu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam vēṟṟunili meym mayakkamām. ra ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭu moḻittoḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammoṭu tām kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam uṭaṉili mayakkamām. ivviraṇṭu pakuti mayakkamum moḻiyiṭi varum. uyiruṭaṉ meyyum meyyuṭaṉuyirum māṟi uyirum meyyumāka mayaṅkum mayakkattiṟku variyaṟiyilli; vēṇṭiyavāṟē mayaṅkumeṉpatām.
itaṉkaṇ pavaṇantiyār, uyirum meyyum, meyyum uyirum māṟi mayaṅku mayakkattiṟku aḷaviṉṟeṉakkūṟi, iṭi niliyāka meykaḷi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyavāṟē meymayakku, uṭaṉilimayakkam eṉa iruvakippaṭuttup peyar kūṟiṉār.
iccūttirattāṉ mey mayaṅkuṅkāṟ ka, ca, ta, pa eṉṉum nāṉkum tammoṭu tāmē mayaṅku meṉavum, ra, ḻa eṉṉum iraṇṭum tammoṭu piṟavē mayaṅkumeṉavum, oḻinta paṉṉi raṇṭum tammoṭu tāmum piṟavum mayaṅkumeṉavum peṟṟām.
ivviruvaruṅ kūṟiya meymmayakkattiṉip piṟ kālattu uriyāciriyarkaḷ vēṟṟunili meymmayakkeṉavum, uṭaṉili yiṉi uṭaṉili meymmayakkameṉavum peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar. meymayakku - taṉimey muṉṉarp piṟamey vantu mayaṅkum (vēṟṟunili) mey mayakkam. itaṉi ēḻu cūttiraṅkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār virittuk kūṟuvar.
		ṭa, ṟa, la, ḷa, veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
ka, ca, pa veṉṉu mūveḻut turiya.
(tol. 23)
(i-ḷ) ṭa, ṟa, la, ḷa eṉṟa nāṉku meykaḷiṉ muṉṉark ka, ca, ta, pa eṉṉum mūṉṟu eḻuttukkaḷum taṉittaṉi vantu mayaṅku vaṉavām : eṉṟavāṟu.
		avaṟṟuḷ,
la, ḷa, ḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum.
(tol. 24)
(i-ḷ) muṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷ lakāra ḷakāramākiya meykaḷiṉ muṉṉark ka, ca, pa - kkaḷēyaṉṟi yakaravakaraṅkaḷum vantu mayaṅkum.
ivviru cūttira vitiyiyum naṉṉūlār,
		ṭa, ṟa, muṉ ka, ca, pa, meyyuṭaṉ mayaṅkum.
la, ḷa, muṉ ka, ca, pa, va, ya, voṉṟummē.            	           	           (naṉ. 113, 117)
eṉṟa iru cūttiraṅkaḷāl kūṟiṉār.
		ṅa, ña, ṇa, na, ma, ṉa, veṉum puḷḷi muṉṉart 
tatta micika ḷottaṉa niliyē.                              (tol. 25)
(i-ḷ) melliṉa meykaḷ  āṟaṉ muṉṉarum muṟiyē avaṟṟavaṟṟiṟku iṉamotta valliṉa meykaḷ vantu mayaṅkum.
		avaṟṟuḷ
ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
ka, ca, ña, pa, ma, ya, vav vēḻumuriya.                			 (tol. 26)
(i-ḷ) ṇakāra ṉakārameykaḷiṉ muṉ avaṟṟiṟ kiṉamākiya valliṉameyyē yaṉṟik ka, ca, ña, pa, ma, ya, va ākiya ēḻu meykaḷum vantu mayaṅkuvaṉavām.
		ña, na, ma, va veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
yaḵkā ṉiṟṟaṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.                  			 (tol. 27)
(i-ḷ)ña, na, ma, va eṉṉum nāṉku meykaḷiṉ muṉṉar yakaramey vantu mayaṅkum.
	 	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvuntōṉṟum.         		 	 (tol. 28)
(i-ḷ) makaramākiya meyyiṉ muṉṉarp pakara yakaraṅkaḷē yaṉṟi vakaramum vantu mayaṅkum.
innāṉku cūttira vitikaḷiyum naṉṉūlār,
		ṅa mmuṉkavvām vammuṉ yavvē.
ñanamuṉ ṟammiṉam yakaramo ṭākum.
ṇaṉamuṉ ṉiṉaṅ kaca ñapamaya vavvarum.
ma mmuṉ payava mayaṅku meṉpa.                		  (naṉ. 111, 112, 114, 115)
eṉṟa nāṉku cūttiraṅkaḷāl kuṟippiṭuvar.
		ya, ra, ḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalākeḻuttu ñakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.          		 	 (tol. 29)
(i-ḷ) ya, ra, ḻa eṉṉum meykaḷiṉ muṉṉar moḻikku mutalākum ka, ca, ta, pa, ma, va, ya ña, eṉṉum  oṉpatu meykaḷum, mutalākāta ñakara meyyum vantu mayaṅkum.
naṉṉūlār mutalākāta ṅakaratti avviyoṭṭi mutalākumeṉak koṇṭārātaliṉ ivvitiyi,
		ya, ra, ḻa muṉṉar moḻimutaṉ meyvarum.		    (naṉ. 116)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ cuṭṭiṉār.
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār piṉṉiṟkum meykaḷit tokuttu ivi ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ mayaṅkumeṉac cūttiram ceytalum, naṉṉūlār muṉṉiṟku meykaḷit tokuttu avikaḷōṭu iṉṉiṉṉa meykaḷ vantu mayaṅkumeṉak kūṟalum ivviruvar ceyta cūttiraṅkaḷiyum nōkkiṟ pulaṉām.
		meynnilic cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttum
tammuṉ tām varūum ra ḻa va la ṅ kaṭiyē.		 (tol. 30)
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē uṭaṉili mayakkamāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) poruḷ nilimiyik karutiṉ ellā meykaḷum tammuṉ tām vantu mayaṅkum; rakara ḻakaraṅkaḷ allā viṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
meynniliccuṭṭiṉ eṉṟataṉāl taṉimeymuṉṉar uyirēṟiya mey varumeṉakkoḷka. ellāmeṉṟatu rakara ḻakara moḻintavaṟṟi. ivvuṭaṉili mayakkatti naṉṉūlār,
		ra ḻa va l la ṉa tammuṟ ṟāmuṭa ṉiliyum.		    (naṉ. 118)
eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār.
		a, i, u immūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu			 (tol. 31)
itu kuṟṟeḻutteṉṟavaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) a, i, u, eṉṟu kūṟiya ammūṉṟuñ cuṭ ṭeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum itaṟkaṭutta cūttiramum eḻuttām taṉmiyiṉṟi moḻinilimippaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ moḻi marapiṉiccāra vittār eṉpar urikārar.
cuṭṭiyaṟiyappaṭum poruḷi yuṇarttaliṉ cuṭṭeṉpatu kāraṇappeyar.
akkoṟṟaṉ, ikkoṟṟaṉ, ukkoṟṟaṉ eṉavarum. a, i, u eṉṟa mūṉṟeḻuttum moḻi mutalilē taṉiyē niṉṟu cuṭṭup poruḷi yuṇartti niṟṟal mēṟkāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅ kaḷāṟ pulaṉām. āciriyar taṉiyē cuṭṭu eṉṟa peyarik kuṟippiṭum pōtellām immūṉṟeḻuttit tavira vēṟoṉ ṟiyuñ cuṭṭāmiyum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum.
ivvāṟu naṉṉūlārum a, i, u, im mūṉṟeḻuttum moḻimutaṟkaṇ puṟattē taṉittuc cuṭṭup poruḷuṇartta variṟ cuṭṭeḻuttāmeṉpataṉi,
		a, i, um mutaṟ ṟaṉivariṟ cuṭṭē.			    (naṉ. 66)
eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār.
iṅṅaṉam āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum, urikārarum, pavaṇantiyārum, mayilinātarum moḻikku mutalil taṉittuc cuṭṭup poruḷuṇarttivarum a, i, u eṉṟa im mūṉṟa ṉiyumē īṇṭuc cuṭṭeṉap peyar kūṟiṉārākap piṉvanta naṉṉūluriyāciriyar caṅkara namaccivāyar mutaleṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamiyāṟ puṟattum akattum varutal peṟṟām eṉak koṇṭu, āciriyar kūṟiya `taṉivariṉ eṉṟataṉiyum nōkkātu, avaṉ eṉpataṉkaṇ akaram, aṟam eṉpataṉ kaṇ akaram pōlap piṉṉeḻuttukkaḷōṭu toṭarntu niṉṟa oru poruḷi yuṇarttātu, maliyaṉ eṉpataṉkaṭ pakutipōla vēṟu niṉṟu cuṭṭup poruḷuṇart taliṉ akattuvarum itaṉiyum taṉivariṉ eṉṟār eṉa amiti kūṟi, avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉṟa peyariyum utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiṉar. avarkaḷ akattu varuvatākak koṇṭa ivvutāra ṇattiṉkaṇ cuṭṭeḻuttum cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷum ak koṟṟaṉ eṉṟaviṭattup pōla vēṟu nillātu oru peyarppaṭṭu niṟṟalāṉ a, i, u mutaliya eḻuttukkaḷ itaṉ mutaṟkaṇ maliyaṉ eṉpataṉkaṭ pakutipōla piḷavupaṭṭut taṉiyē niṉṟaṉa eṉak koḷḷal poruntātu. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār cuṭṭu eṉṟa peyariṉi a, i, u, eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷukkē iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉār. avi yaṭiyākap piṟanta peyarkaḷic `cuṭṭup peyar (col - kiḷavi cū 37, 38) eṉak kuṟippiṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar.

(i-ḷ) ā, ē, ō eṉṟa mūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉum peyariṉiyuṭiyaṉa. eṉṟavāṟu.
viṉāpporuḷuṇarttaliṉ viṉāvāyiṟṟu. uṇkā uṇkē, uṇkō eṉa varum. āciriyar īṇṭu ā, ē, ō, eṉa eḻuttām niliyil niṉṟu viṉāp poruḷ uṇarttivarum mūṉṟaṉiyum viṉāveṉak kuṟiyiṭṭurittār. collām nilimiyil niṉṟu yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar eṉa uyar tiṇik kaṇṇum, yātu, yā, yāvi, yeṉa aḵṟiṇikkaṇṇum peyarākavum, uyartiṇik kaṇ yār eṉavum aḵṟiṇikkaṇ evaṉ eṉavum viṉāviṉik kuṟippākavum varuvaṉavaṟṟi muṟiyē collatikārattup peyariyal viṉiyiyal ivaṟṟiṟ kūṟippōntār. evvayiṉ etōḷi eṉa ekara mutalāka vayiṉ eṉpatum ikaravikutiyum oṭṭi yorucol nīrmippaṭṭu iṭapporuṇartti iṅṅaṉam viṉāvāy niṟkum iṭiccoṟkaḷi viṉāveṉat taḻuviṉār (eḻuttu 159, 334). ivi coṉṉilimippaṭṭu viṉāp poruḷuṇartti niṟṟaliṉ eḻuttām nilimiyil niṉṟu īṇṭup puṇarccikkap paṭum īṟṟu viṉāvākiya ā, ē, ō, eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ iyit turikkappaṭāvāyiṉa.

eṉa ivvatikārattum viṉāveṉakkoṇṭu, yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yā, yāvi, eṉap peyariyaṟkaṇṇum,

attiṇi maruṅkiṉ muppāṟku murittē.

okkumeṉpa evaṉeṉ viṉāvē. 		         (tol. 13, 22)
eṉa viṉiyiyaṟkaṇṇum eṭutturittamiyāl viṉāc colli viṉāppeyar, viṉā viṉikkuṟippu eṉa iruvakippaṭuttiṉā reṉpataṟiyalām. īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭṭa ā, ē, ō mūṉṟum irutiṇiyimpāṟ peyariṟutikaḷōṭuñ cērntu viṉāp poruḷuṇartti niṟṟalum, coṉṉilip paṭṭuvarum yāvaṉ mutaliya peyarum, viṉāviṉik kuṟippum, avvirutiṇiyuḷ oṉṟāy orupāṟ poruḷiṉkaṇ niṟṟaluṅ koṇṭu, ivi poruḷāṉum vēṟupāṭuṭiya eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. iṉi evvayiṉ etōḷi mutaliyavi ekaratti mutalākak koṇṭu variṉum vayiṉ eṉṉum collum ikara vikutiyum peṟṟu oru collāy niṉṟu iṭapporuḷuṇartti viṉāvāy varutaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṉa.
iṉi iḷampūraṇar ic cūttirattiṟ taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl ekaramum yakara ākāramum viṉāp peṟumeṉak koḷka. eṉat taḻuvikkoṇṭār. ikkaruttēpaṟṟi naṉṉūlārum,
	 	e, yā, mutalum ā, ō īṟṟum
ēyiruvaḻiyum viṉāvākummē.			    (naṉ. 67) 
eṉac cūttirañ ceytaṉar.
(ic cūttirattil āciriyar pavaṇantiyār viṉāvāka varumeṉakkūṟiya ā, ē, ō ākiya īṟṟu viṉāvil ē mutaliṉum varumeṉak kuṟippiṭṭatu, ētu eṉpatu mutalāka varum piṟkālattu vaḻakku nōkkiyātal vēṇṭum.)
pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyavāṟu ekaram viṉāvāy varumeṉ ṟāl, a, i, u eṉṟavaṟṟic cuṭṭeṉak kuṟippiṭṭa āciriyar ekarattiyum viṉāveṉa aṭakkik kūṟiyiruppar. uyir mayaṅki yalil a, i, u eṉṟavaṟṟiṟku avvavvīṟṟiṉkaṇ puṇarcci viti kūṟiya āciriyar, ekaram taṉi niṉṟu viṉāpporuḷuṇarttu māyiṉ ataṉiyum ekara vīṟṟuḷ eṭuttuk kūṟip puṇart tiruppar. avvāṟeṭuttō tāmiyāṉum cuṭṭumutal vayiṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla `ekara mutal vayiṉ eṉa eḻuttē paṟṟik kūṟi ekaram ikaram mutaliyavikaḷōṭu kūṭi oru collākiya niliyilēyē, avviṭic colli viṉāveṉak kuṟippiṭṭuc cēṟalāṉum, ekaramoṉṟē moḻi mutaṟkaṇ taṉittu niṉṟu viṉāp poruḷuṇarttiṟṟeṉṟal tol kāppiyaṉārkkuṭaṉ pāṭaṉṟu eṉka.
iṉi aḵṟiṇippaṉmip peyarāy niṉṟu viṉāp poruḷuṇarttum yā eṉṉum viṉāp peyariṉi moḻi mutaliṉiṉṟum viṉāpporuḷuṇarttum viṉā eḻuttākak koṇṭu, iru tiṇiyimpāṟ peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum piṉṉiṉṟu viṉāpporuḷuṇarttum eḻuttukkaḷōṭiyit  turittal poruntātu. aṉṟiyum yāvoṉṟaṉi viṉā veḻuttākak koṇṭu ataṉōṭu tuvvikutiyum vivikutiyum cērntu yātu, yāvi eṉa āyiṟṟeṉak koḷḷiṉ yā eṉpataṉi aḵṟiṇip paṉmip peyarākak koṇṭa tolkāppiyaṉār karuttōṭu muraṇi, yā eṉpatu aimpāṟku muritteṉap poruḷ paṭutalāṉum, aimpāṟkumuriya yāveṉpatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum vaḻakkilum, aḵṟiṇip paṉmip poruḷuṇartti niṟṟalāṉum āciriyar karuttu aḵtaṉṟeṉka.
iṉi, e, eṉpataṉit taṉiyē varum viṉāveḻuttākak koṇṭu epporuḷ eṉṟum, uyartiṇik kaṇ aṉ vikuti peṟṟu evaṉ eṉṟu āṇpāluṇarttiyum niṟkumeṉak koḷvar piṟkālattu uriyāciriyar.
epporuḷāyiṉum eṉṟa tolkāppiyac cūttirat toṭarkku ekarattit taṉiyē vanta viṉāveḻuttākap pirittu entap poruḷāyiṉum eṉap piṟkālattārpōlap poruḷuriyātu yātāṉu moru poruḷiyāyiṉum eṉac cēṉā variyar kūṟippōtalāl yātuporuḷāyiṉum eṉpatē epporuḷāyiṉumeṉa oru moḻiyāyt tirintu niṉṟateṉpatu avar karuttātalaṟiyalām.
evaṉ eṉṟa viṉā uyartiṇikku urittaṉṟeṉpati aḵtēl numakkivaṉ evaṉām eṉa uyar tiṇikkaṇṇum varumāleṉiṉ, āṇṭu atu muṟi paṟṟi niṟṟaliṉ aḵṟiṇik kaṇ vantateṉavē paṭumeṉpatu; aḵtēl numakkivaṉ eṉṉa muṟiyaṉām eṉpatallatu, eṉṉa muṟiyām eṉpatu poruntā teṉiṉ, eṉṉa muṟiyeṉpatu āṇṭu muṟimēl nillātu oṟṟumi nayattāṉ muṟiyuṭiyōṉ mēl niṟṟaliṉ amiyu meṉka eṉa viṉāviṭikaḷāṉ vaṟpuṟuttiṉār. īṇṭu evaṉ eṉpatu aḵṟiṇiyiru pāṟkumuriya viṉāviṉik kuṟippām. aḵṟiṇi viṉāvāy varum evaṉ eṉṉum viṉikkuṟippuc collē attiṇik kaṭ peyarākavum āciriyar kālattiṟkup piṉ vaḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiyatu. ip peyarccol iṉ coliṉi tīṉṟal kāṇpāṉevaṉkolō - vaṉcol vaḻaṅkuvatu eṉat  tirukkuṟaḷilum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. itippaṟṟi evaṉeṉpatōr peyarumuṇṭu; aḵtik kālattu eṉṉeṉṟum eṉṉi yeṉṟum niṟkum eṉac cēṉāvariyar kūṟutalāl, evaṉ eṉṉum aḵṟiṇi viṉāppeyar piṟkālattu eṉ, eṉṉi, eṉṟu tirintu vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu eṉpatum peṟṟām. cēṉā variyar evaṉ eṉpataṉi āciriyar karuttāka viṉik kuṟippeṉakkoṇṭu accol uyartiṇi kuṟippataṉṟu eṉa vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, yāvaṉ eṉpatu mutalāka uyartiṇi viṉāppeyarik kuṟippiṭṭuc ceṉṟa tolkāppiyaṉār, taṅkālattu evaṉ eṉṟa viṉā uyartiṇi āṇpālic cuṭṭi niṟkumāyiṉ ataṉik kūṟātirār ākalāṉum avarkālattu evaṉ eṉṟa col uyartiṇi viṉāp peyarāy niṉṟa taṉṟeṉpatu pulaṉām.
āciriyar kālattup piṉṉar yātu yāvi yeṉpaṉa muṟiyē etu, evi eṉavum, yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar eṉpaṉa muṟiyē evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, eṉavum tirintu vaḻaṅkiyatātalāl uriyāciriyarum naṉṉūlārum, ekaratti viṉāveḻuttākat taḻuvik koṇṭaṉar  pōlum. iṅṅaṉam yātu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ yā eṉṉum uyirmey ekaramākattiriyum muṟimit teṉpati yāṉ yām eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mutal yakara mey keṭṭu ākāram ekaramākattirintu eṉ, em, eṉa niṟkum eṉakkiḷanta avaṟṟiṉ iyalpāṉ uṇarntu koḷḷa vittār āciriyar. ātalāṉ, avviyalpu muṟipaṟṟiyē ac coṟkaḷiṉ yakara ākāram ekaramākat tirintateṉak koḷḷal porutta muṭittu.
		aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.            			 (tol. 33)
iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiriyil nīṇṭu niṟkumiṭam ituveṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) eḻuttukkaḷ tamakkuc coṉṉa aḷapiṉikkaṭan tolittalum, oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ ari māttiriyiṉ nīṇ ṭolittalum uḷaveṉpar āciriyar. aṅṅaṉam uḷavātalum kural mutaliya ēḻiciyōṭu poruntiya narampiṉiyuṭiya yāḻiṉitu ici nūliṭattaṉa eṉac colluvar pulavar. eṉṟavāṟu.
oṟṟici nīṭalumeṉavē muṉṉar aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum eṉṟa viti oṟṟallāta uyireḻuttukkaḷukkeṉpatu peṟutum. oṟṟici nīṭalum `uḷa eṉṟatu, an nīṭṭippu orutali yaṉṟeṉpatu viḷakkiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. iciyiṉ aḷaviṟan ticikkuṅ kāl uyir paṉṉiraṇṭu māttiri yīṟākavum, oṟṟu (mey) patiṉoru māttiri yīṟākavum icikkumeṉpar ici nūlār. avar koḷkiyi iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyarākiya ivar piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭam paṭutal eṉṉum uttiyāṟ ṟaḻuviṉār. naṉṉūlār ici nūliṭattu eḻuttukkaḷ tattam māttiriyiṉik kaṭantolittalōṭu viḷi paṇṭamāṟṟutal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum avi aṅṅaṉamicikku meṉak koṇṭu,
		āviyu moṟṟu maḷaviṟan ticittalum
mēvu miciviḷi paṇṭamāṟ ṟātiyiṉ.		    	   (naṉ. 99)
eṉac cūttiram ceytaṉar.
iṉi iccūttirattu ātiyeṉṟamiyāṉ neṟpōr teḻikkum pakaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷit turakkuñ collākiya `nāval eṉpataṉ kaṇṇum kuṟippici muṟiyīṭu pulampal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum uyirum oṟṟum aḷapiṟanticikkum meṉak koṇṭār piṉ vanta urikārar.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar `aḷapiṟantuyirttalum eṉṉuñ cūttirattil `iciyoṭu civaṇiya eṉpataṟku icittalōṭu poruntiya nālvakic ceyyuṭkaṇ eṉap poruḷ kūṟi, uyirum oṟṟum ceyyuṭkaṇ aḷa piṟanticikkumeṉak koṇṭār. iciyoṭu civaṇiya eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar narampiṉ maṟiya eṉpataṉ aṭimoḻiyākalāṉum, taṉṉūlē paṟṟākappiṟa nūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟutaṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākap pērāciriyar itaṉik kuṟippiṭutalāṉum atu karut taṉṟeṉka.

2. moḻimarapu
moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉ varum marapu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻi marapeṉappaṭṭatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, eḻuttāṉāl moḻiyatu marapu uṇarttukiṉṟamiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻi marapeṉak kāraṇap peyarttāyiṟṟeṉa nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum kūṟuvar. eḻuttukkaḷukku moḻiyiṭiyuḷatām aṭippaṭṭa iyalpiṉi āciriyar ivviyalāṉ uṇarttu kiṉṟāreṉpatu koḷḷattakum. ivviyaluṭ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam taṉi niṉṟa eḻuttiṟkaṉṟi moḻiyiṭi yeḻuttiṟkeṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟuvar.
ivviyalāṟ kūṟappaṭuvaṉa kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṉum cārpeḻuttilakkaṇam, uyiraḷapeṭi, moḻiyākkam, meykaḷiṉiyakkam, īroṟṟuṭaṉili, makarak kuṟukkam, pōli, aikārakkuṟukkam, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ, moḻikkīṟāmeḻuttukkaḷ, ākiya ivikaḷām. ivi yellām moḻiyiṭi vittuṇarattakkaṉavātaliṉ īṇṭu ṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.
moḻiyiṭiccārtti yuṇarappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum, oruvāṟṟāṉ eḻutteṉak koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum ivi nūṉmarapiyaṭuttu moḻimarapiṉ mutaṟ kaṇ viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. cārntu varutali marapākavuṭiya mūṉṟaṟkum avitām, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉa muṉṉaruritta muṟiyē īṇṭu ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār. ammūṉṟum orumoḻi, puṇarmoḻi ākiya iraṇṭiṭattum varumeṉak koṇṭu avaṟṟi orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, orumoḻikkuṟṟiyalukaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, orumoḻiyāytam, puṇarmoḻiyāytam, eṉa āṟākappirittu muṟiyē 34 mutal 39 vari uḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil uṇartti 40-l avvoru moḻiyāytat tiṟkōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
		kuṟṟiya likaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum
yāveṉ ciṉimici yuriyacik kiḷavik
kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē.	      		 (tol. 34)
iḵtu orumoḻikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oru moḻikkuṟṟiyalikaram tām kūṟum poruḷik kōṭaṟku oruvaṉi etirmukamākkum coṟkup poruntavarum uriyacic collākiya `miyā eṉṉum colliṉ ciṉiyākiya `yā veṉṉum uṟuppiṉ mēlatāy makaravoṟṟiṉi yūrntu niṟṟali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) kēṇmiyā eṉa varum.
miyāveṉṉuñ col iṭam, makaram paṟṟukkōṭu, `yā eṉpatum ikaram ari māttiriyātaṟkuc cārpu.
		puṇariyal niliyiṭik kuṟukalu murittē
yuṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.			 (tol. 35)
itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumeṉ kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) akkuṟṟiyalikaram orumoḻikkaṇṇaṉṟi irumoḻi tammuṭ puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇṇum kuṟukalurittu. ataṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarak kūṟattoṭaṅkiṉ avi kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷḷē kūṟappaṭum. eṉṟavāṟu.
`kuṟukalum eṉpuḻi ummi, puṇariyal niliyiṭiyum eṉa māṟṟiyurikkappaṭṭatu.
yakaram varumvaḻi yikaraṅkuṟukum, ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu (400) eṉṟa cūttirattu aṟuvakik kuṟṟiya lukara vīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu nilimoḻik kuṟṟukaraveḻuttu muṟṟat tōṉṟātu; āṇṭōr ikaram vantu arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum eṉakkūṟiya vitiyi īṇṭuc cuṭṭiṉār.
(u-m) nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokkiyātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉavarum. yakaram iṭam, ukarañcārnta valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
ivviruvakik kuṟṟiyalikarattiyum naṉṉūlār,
		yakaram varakkuṟa ḷuttiri yikaramum
aciccoṉ miyāvi ṉikaramuṅ kuṟiya.		   	    (naṉ. 93)
eṉpataṉāl tokuttōtiṉār.
āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum yakaram varumvaḻi yikaramākat tiriyumeṉpar pavaṇantiyār. yakaramvarak kuṟaḷu tiriyikaram eṉpatu kāṇka. kuṟṟukaraṅkeṭṭu ikaran tōṉṟumeṉpatē tolkāppiyaṉār karuttākum.
		neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.			 (tol. 36)
iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti yilum varum valleḻuttu āṟiṉiyum ūrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟṟali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ.
niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉpatu īṇṭuṅ kūṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉpar urikārar.
`neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟum iṭam. valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu. ivvāṟu iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟavē, moḻikku īṟātalum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
neṭṭeḻuttimpark kuṟṟiyalukaratti īreḻuttoru moḻiyeṉap peyar tantum, toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟiṉi uyirttoṭar, iṭittoṭar, āytattoṭar, vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar eṉa aivakiyākkiyum piṉṉark kūṟuvar āciriyar (cū. 406) ivvāṟē naṉṉūlārum,
		neṭilō ṭāyta muyirvali meliyiṭi
toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti vaṉmiyū rukaram
aḵkum piṟamēṟ ṟoṭaravum peṟumē.			    (naṉ. 94)
eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟṟukaratti aṟuvakiyākkik kūṟuvar. toṭar moḻiyeṉpataṉi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iraṇṭiṟan ticikkun toṭarmoḻi yeṉakkoṇṭatu pōlaṉṟip pavaṇantiyār īreḻuttu oru moḻiyiyum toṭarmoḻiyākak koṇṭārātalāṉ piṟamēṟṟoṭartal neṭilukkilli yeṉpatu paṭa neṭili oṭukkoṭuttup pirital iṉṟiyamiyātāyiṟṟu.
		iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.			 (tol. 37)
itu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷum varumeṉpatu uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) akkuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻi yiṭip paṭiṉ kuṟukumiṭamum uṇṭu; ataṉ puṇarcci muṟimiyaṟiyum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ eṉpatām.
iṭippaṭiṉuṅ kuṟukumeṉa ummiyi māṟṟi yurip pār iḷampūraṇar. iṭaṉ eṉṟataṉāl ik kuṟukkam ciṟu pāṉmi yeṉka.
iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukumiṭattik kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇari yaluḷ valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻit, tolliyiyaṟki niliyalumurittē eṉpataṉāṟ cuṭṭiṉār āciriyar. iccūttiram kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻikkaṇ taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟuki varumeṉkiṉṟatu eṉak karut turittu, avvukaram oru moḻiyuḷaṉṟip puṇarmoḻi yiṭippaṭiṉ taṉ ari māttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukumiṭaṉumuṇṭu; ataṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭup peṟuvateṉiṉ ataṉ puṇarcci muṟimiyaṟiyuṅ kuṟṟilukarap puṇariyaluḷ, eṉap poruḷ kūṟiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iṅṅaṉaṅkoṇṭā laṉṟi iccūttiram puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaratti yuṇarttiṟ ṟeṉṟal poruntātu. ukaraṅ kuṟukumiṭam īreḻuttoru moḻi mutal aṟuvakiyiṭam eṉak kūṟippōnta āciriyar, atu puṇarmoḻiyiṭippaṭiṉ taṉ arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟintu olikku miṭattip puṇar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramāka vurittuc cellutalē porutta muṭittu.
allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum, ellā viṟutiyum ukaram niṟiyum eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, āṟu toṭariṉ kaṇṇum kuṟṟiyalukaram varumeṉap poruḷ koḷḷātu, oru māttiriyil kuṟukātu niṟkumeṉap poruḷ koṇṭu, av vāṟṟāṉ ellā iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum taṉ ari māttiriyiṉ mikkolikkumeṉavum valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya arimāttiriyākiya paḻiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟṟalum urittu eṉavum kūṟi, iccūttiram taṉṉiyalpākiya ari māttiri peṟṟuc ciṟupāṉmiyāy varumiṭattiyē cuṭṭiṟṟeṉavum koḷvar iḷampūraṇar. orumoḻik kaṇṇun tam ari māttiri yiyalpiṉavāy varuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷi arimāttiriyiṉ mikkolippaṉa eṉakkōṭalum, valloṟṟuttoṭar moḻi valleḻuttu varumiṭattu ari māttiriyiṟ ciṟitu kuṟintolippaṉavaṟṟi ari māttiriyiyalpāka olikkumeṉakkōṭalum vaḻakkiṟkum vitikkum poruntāvām. āciriyar `avviyal niliyu mēṉi mūṉṟē eṉpataṉāl kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku ari māttiriyiyal peṉak kūṟiṉār. valloṟṟuttoṭar moḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻiyallatu arimāttiri peṟātu mikkolippaṉa vāyiṉ āciriyar arimāttiri peṟumeṉa vitippataṟku iṉṟiyamiyāmi etuvumilli. ataṉāl āciriyar karuttup paṭi āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum taṉ ari māttiriyiṉ mikātu icikku meṉpatē poruttamuṭittu. iccūttirattil iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukumiṭattic ciṟuvaraviṟṟeṉac cuṭṭiṉami yālum oru moḻiyiṭattē ari māttiri peṟṟu niṟpaṉa valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuvaḻik kuṟukumeṉak kuṟṟiya lukarap puṇariyaluḷ kūṟiṉamiyāṉ av varimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukumeṉpatē accūttirak karuttākalāṉum nacciṉārk kiṉiyar uritta uriyē poruntumeṉka.
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya itaṉippaṟṟi naṉṉūlāciriyar kuṟippiṭavilli.
	38.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittē.		 (tol. 38)
iḵtu oru moḻiyāytam varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) puḷḷi vaṭiviṉatākiya āytam kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyirōṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttāṟaṉ mēlatāy varum.
(u-m) eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, aḵtu, kaḵpu, paḵṟi eṉavarum.
kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉarum uyirōṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttiṉ mēlumāka ivvāytavoli varumeṉṟu tolkāppiyar variyaṟi kūṟiyataṉāl avar kālattu avaṟṟiṭiyēyallatu āytam payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkāmi teḷiyappaṭum; ivvāytavoli valleḻuttāṟaṉ mēlatāy niṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ vallōciyi melivittu niṟpatākum. ivvāṟu valleḻuttukkaḷukku muṉṉar niṉṟu avaṟṟiṉōciyi meṉmippaṭutti niṟkum iyalpiṟṟu āytam eṉpataṉiyuṇarnta kālañceṉṟa uyartiru māṇikka nāyakaravarkaḷ ivveḻuttiṉ tuṇiyāl piṟamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum ellā volikaḷiyum tamiḻ moḻik kaṇ kuṟittu vaḻaṅkutal kūṭumeṉṟu ārāyntu viḷakkiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. iv veḻuttiṉ tuṇiyāl kāḵpi mutaliya piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷit tamiḻ oli eḻuttukkaḷāl vaḻaṅka vaḻi kāṭṭiyavarum avarēyāvar. it tolkāppiyac cūttiratti naṉṉūlār muṟṟāytam eṉṟa pakutiyil tāṉeṭuttu moḻital eṉṉum uttiyāl eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.
		īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉu micimi tōṉṟum.		 	 (tol. 39)
iḵtu ivvāytam puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkumiṭattum vēṟōreḻuttiṉ ōciyiṉkaṇ āytam tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
āytam tōṉṟumāṟu;- lakara ḷakara vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ takara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu āytamākat tirintu muṭiyum (369, 399) eṉavum vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu av vakaramey āytamākat tirintu muṭiyum (379) eṉavum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaliṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. avvāṟu la, ḷa, vīṟṟu moḻikaḷ al vaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyōṭu puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa am meykaḷ āytamākat tirintu muṭitalāṉ, āytan tōṉṟu meṉṉātu vēṟōreḻuttiṉ ōcikkaṇ āytavoli tirintu tōṉṟumeṉpaṭa icimi tōṉṟum eṉṟār.
(u-m) kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu, aḵkaṭiya eṉavarum.
		uruviṉu miciyiṉu marukit tōṉṟu
moḻikkuṟip pellā meḻutti ṉiyalā
āyta maḵkāk kāli yāṉa.	       		 (tol. 40)
iḵtu orumoḻi yāytattiṟku ōrilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
oru poruḷiṉatu uruvattiṉ kaṇṇum ōciyiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmiyāyt tōṉṟum kuṟippumoḻi kaḷellām āyta eḻuttāṉiṭṭu eḻutappaṭṭu naṭavā. avvāytam taṉ ari māttiriyaḷavāyc curuṅki nillātu nīṇṭakālattu annīṭcikku eṉap poruḷ kūṟik kaḵṟeṉṟatu eṉpatu uruvu, cuḵṟeṉṟatu eṉpatu ici eṉa iḷampūraṇar utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiṉar.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyāciriyari maṟuttu `iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttiyāl ceyyuḷiyali nōkki āytattiṟku eytiyatōr ilakkaṇa muṇarttukiṉṟatu eṉak karutturittu, `niṟattiṉ kaṇṇum oliyiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi āytan tōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittaliyuṭiya collum aviyoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum oṟṟeḻut tukkaḷ pōla ari māttiriyiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi mikkum naṭantu āytam curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām eṉap poruḷ kūṟi, `kaṇṇṭaṇṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum eṉa oṟṟaḷapeṭuppuḻik kaṇṇ eṉpatu cīr niliyey tiṉāṟpōla kaḵḵṟeṉṉuṅ kallatarattam eṉa niṟattiṉ kaṇṇum, cuḵḵṟeṉṉun taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇi yeṉa iciyiṉ kaṇṇum vanta āytam oru māttiripeṟṟuc cīr niliyeytuṅkāl āṇṭup peṟukiṉṟa oru māttirikku, āytam atikārappaṭṭami kaṇṭu īṇṭu etiratu pōṟṟi vitikūṟiṉār āciriyar eṉac ciṟappuriyuṅ kūṟiṉār.
(i-ḷ) oru poruḷiṉatu niṟattiṉ kaṇṇum iciyiṉ kaṇṇum māttiri perukit tōṉṟuṅ kuṟippu moḻi kaḷellām annīṭciyik kuṟittaṟ keṉat taṉiyē eḻuttāl eḻutappaṭṭu naṭavā; āytam curuṅkā iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām.
eṉavē āytam curuṅkiṉ immoḻik kuṟippup pulappaṭā teṉpatu karuttu.
		(u-m) `kaḵṟeṉṉuṅ kallatarattam
			`cuḵṟeṉṉun taṇtōṭṭup peṇṇi
eṉa muṟiyē niṟattiṉum iciyiṉum āytam nīṇṭu olitta tāyiṉum annīṭcikku maṟṟumō reḻuttu vēṇṭap paṭāmi yaṟika. ic cūttirattil vanta aruki eṉṉuñ collukkup peruki yeṉavē takkayākapparaṇi yuriyāciriyar poruḷ kūṟiṉār. (tāḻici - 37)
ivaṟṟi āytam curuṅkāta coṟkaḷeṉavē āyta voli curuṅkum coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉpatu peṟutum. naṉṉū lārum lakara ḷakara vīṟṟup puṇarmoḻik kaṇ varum āytam taṉ ; ari māttiriyiṟ kuṟukum eṉpataṉi,
		 laḷavīṟ ṟiyipiṉām āytam aḵkum		     	     (naṉ. 97)
eṉṟa cūttirattil kuṟippiṭṭu eḻuttiṉ eṇ eṉṟa pakutiyil āytak kuṟukkam eṉattaṉiyē ōreḻuttāka eṇṇiṉār.
uyiraḷapeṭi
		kuṉṟici moḻivayi ṉiṉṟici niṟikkum
neṭṭeḻut timpa rottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.		  	  (tol. 41)
itu nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ eṉa mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa aḷa peṭi, moḻiyiṭivittuṇarum peṟṟiyatākaliṉ ataṉi yuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōciyiyuṭiya coṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉākat tamakku iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ avvōciyi niṟivikkum eṉpatām.
mēl neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ tāmē mūṉṟu māttiri mutalāka ōcimikku nillā eṉavum iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉu mikuttu mūṉṟu mutalāka neṭṭeḻuttiṉi nīṭṭa virumpu vōr, am māttiri yiṉit tarutaṟkuriya eḻuttiṉik kūṭṭi eḻupputal vēṇṭum eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār. ici kuṉṟuvatāṉa moḻikkaṇ neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉar ōciyi niṟittu niṟpaṉa avaṟṟiṉ iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē eṉpataṉi iccūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār. itaṉāl aḷapeṭikkaṇ neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuriya  iraṇṭu māttirikku mēṟpaṭṭa vōciyiṉi niṟippaṉa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉark kūṭṭiya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē eṉpatu āciriyar karuttātal viḷaṅkum.
		(u-m) āaṭi, īiki eṉavarum.
ceyyuṭkaṇ ici kuṉṟiṉ moḻikku mutaliṉumiṭi yiṉuṅ kaṭiyiṉum niṉṟa neṭṭeḻut tēḻum avvici niṟikkat tattam māttiriyiṉ mikkolikkum eṉavum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ varivaṭiviṟ kāṇappaṭum iṉamāya kuṟṟeḻut tukkaḷ avvāṟu neṭilaḷapeṭuttami yaṟitaṟkeṉa varinta aṟikuṟikaḷē eṉavum karutiya naṉṉūlār,
		icikeṭiṉ moḻimuta liṭi kaṭi nilineṭil
aḷapeḻu mavaṟṟavaṟ ṟiṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē.		     (naṉ. 91)
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār. iṅṅaṉam neṭiloṉṟē aḷapeḻuntu mūṉṟu māttiriyāy mikkicikku meṉavum neṭil aḷapeṭuttati yaṟitaṟku varum veṟum aṟikuṟiyē neṭiliṉpiṉ niṉṟa kuṟil eṉavum oru tali tuṇitalākki ivar kūṟiyatu, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttoṭu māṟu paṭukiṉṟatu.
naṉṉūlār kūṟiyavāṟu neṭṭeḻuttē yaḷapeṭuttu niṟka, ataṉ piṉṉar varuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttu aṟikuṟiyāy vantatāyiṉ,
		ōōtal vēṇṭum oḷimāḻkuñ ceyviṉi
yāatu meṉṉu mavar.
		uṟāark kuṟunō yurippāy kaṭalic
ceṟāay vāḻiya neñcu.
aṉiccappūk kālkaḷiyāḷ peytāḷ nucuppiṟku
nalla paṭāa paṟi.
eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkat tilakkiyaṅkaḷil iru māttiri yuṭiya neṭṭeḻuttu ōraciyākavum, oru māttiriyuṭiya kuṟṟeḻuttu ōraciyākavum pirintu, acikoḷḷappaṭutal poruntātām. ceyyuḷil varum uyiraḷapeṭi neṭilōci yum kuṟilōciyum eṉa vēṟu pirittu acikoḷḷap paṭutalāṉum, neṭilē mūṉṟu māttiriyiṉ mikkolikku meṉṟal, iraṇṭu māttirikku mēl mūṉṟu māttiri mutalāka ōreḻuttolittalilli eṉpatupaṭa mūvaḷa picittal ōreḻuttiṉṟē  eṉa āciriyar kūṟiya cūttirak karuttōṭu muraṇutalāṉum, `kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ neṭṭeḻut timpar otta kuṟṟeḻuttu niṉṟu ici niṟikkum eṉpuḻi, ici niṟikkum eṉṉum payaṉilikku viṉimutalākiya kuṟṟeḻut tiṉi veṟum aṟikuṟiyeṉṟal poruntāmiyāṉum tollāciriyariṉ vitiyē aḷapeṭikku amiyu meṉka.
		ai au veṉṉu māyī reḻuttiṟ
kikara vukara miciniṟi vākum.			 (tol. 42)
iḵtu otta kuṟṟeḻuttillātaṉa aḷapeṭukkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) tamakku iṉamillāta aikāra aukāra meṉṟu kūṟappaṭum avviraṇṭeḻuttiṟkum muṟiyē īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷuk kiṉamāṉa ikara ukaraṅkaḷ kuṉṟici moḻikkaṇ niṉṟu ōciyi niṟippaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.
aḷapeṭikkaṇ iciniṟippaṉa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē yeṉpati īṇṭum ikara vukaram ici niṟivākum - eṉpataṉāl āciriyar viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
`neṭil aḷapeḻum avaṟṟavaṟ ṟiṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē eṉa vuritta pavaṇantiyārum, tam koḷkikku māṟākak kuṟṟuyir aḷapiṉīṟām eṉak kūṟip pōntār. ociyāl moḻi mutal moḻiyīṟu collum ivviṭattil aḷapeṭi yiṟ kuṟṟeḻuttu īṟāmeṉak kūṟutal veṟuṅ kuṟiyaḷaviṟ keṉṟē koḷḷaṟkilli. itaṉāl aḷapeṭiyiṟ kuṟṟeḻuttu olittu niṟṟali naṉṉūlārum tam koḷkiyiṉi maṟantu uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉareṉavē koḷḷa vēṇṭiyuḷatu.
ituvariyum collappaṭṭa vitikaḷ muṉṉi iyalākiya nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyaṉa vātaliṉ, it tuṇiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu eṉpar iḷam pūraṇar.
moḻiyākkam
		neṭṭeḻut tēḻē yōreḻut torumoḻi.   		 (tol. 43)
eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu kūṟattoṭaṅki ōreḻuttoru moḻi yāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷākiya uyirkaḷēḻum ōreḻut tāṉākum oru moḻiyām.
(u-m) ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō, eṉavarum. aukāram uyirmeyk kaṇṇallatu vārātu. (ū - taci). iv viti uyirkkum uyir meykkum potu.
ivi tammiyuṇara niṉṟavaḻi eḻuttām. iṭi niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttiya vaḻi collām.
		kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻiniṟi pilavē.
(tol. 44)
(i-ḷ) kuṟṟeḻuttintum ōreḻuttāy niṉṟu oru moḻiyāy niṟitalilli.
aintum niṟitalilli yeṉavē, avaṟṟuṭ cila meyyōṭu kūṭi niṟintu niṉṟu moḻiyām eṉa ummi eccappaṭṭa teṉpār iḷampūraṇar.
(u-m) tu, no eṉavarum.
a, i, u eṉat taṉiniṉṟu cuṭṭup poruḷuṇarttuvaṉa vaṟṟi moḻiyeṉṉātatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ avi tamakkeṉa vēṟu poruḷiṉṟip poruḷā tiyāṟaṉ poruḷiyē cuṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ moḻiyāmēṉum taṉiyē ōreḻuttāka niṉṟu oru moḻiyāka niṟitalilavākaliṉ moḻiniṟipu ila eṉṟār. moḻi niṟipu - moḻiyiyalpil niṟintu muḻupporuḷ taral.
ic cūttiramum uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.
		ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticikkun toṭar moḻi yuḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.		 (tol. 45)
iḵtu eḻuttiṉāl ākum moḻikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ōreḻuttāṉākum oru moḻiyum īreḻut tāṉākum oru moḻiyum, iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttuk kaḷāl icikkum toṭarmoḻiyuṭaṉē kūṭa moḻikaḷil eḻuttukkaḷ olittu niṟkum nilimi mūṉṟēyām. avi tōṉṟiya neṟikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
taṉiyē moḻiyāy varum ōreḻutti ōreḻuttoru moḻi eṉavum, eḻuttu iraṇṭāy iṇintu poruḷ taruvaṉa vaṟṟi īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉavum, mūṉṟu mutalākat toṭarn ticittup poruḷ taruvaṉavaṟṟit toṭarmoḻi eṉavum āciriyar kūṟippōntār. ōreḻut torumoḻiyum toṭar moḻiyum eṉṉātu īreḻut torumoḻiyum ōtiṉār; cila pala eṉṉun tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki eṉa viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar. pavaṇantiyārum eḻuttē taṉittum toṭarntum poruḷtariṟ patamām (128) eṉa eḻuttukkaḷ collākum niliyi iraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉār.
īreḻut torumoḻi yuyirttoṭa riṭit toṭar (kuṟṟiya -1)
īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum (kuṟṟiya -6) eṉṟāṅkup piṉṉar eṭuttāḷutaṟporuṭṭum iraṇṭi ṇintu niṟpaṉavaṟṟit toṭareṉṉātu iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭaṉa vaṟṟit toṭar eṉa vaḻaṅkum tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkkiyum mūvakippaṭuttuk kūṟiṉār. immoḻi vakikaḷit tāmē vakuttuk kūṟuvatākak kūṟātu. avi tōṉṟiya neṟiyē eṉa āciriyar kūṟutalāl, moḻittōṟṟaṅkarutip paṇṭiyōr vakutta vaḻakku neṟi aḵteṉpatu pulappaṭuta lāṉum, immoḻi vakikaḷik kūṟumuṉṉēyum neṭ ṭeḻuttimparum toṭar moḻiyīṟṟum eṉa iraṇṭiṟan ticippaṉavaṟṟiyē toṭar moḻiyākak koṇṭu āciriyar viti kūṟutalāṉum, pala, cila eṉṉum īreḻuttoru moḻi kaḷit toṭaral iṟuti eṉak kuṟittup pōtalāl avi toṭar moḻiyākā eṉpatu āciriyar karuttu ātalāṉum, aṅṅaṉam īreḻuttoru moḻiyiṉiyum toṭarmoḻi eṉakkōṭal paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlār karuttaṉṟeṉpatu teḷiya viḷaṅkum.
eḻuttukkaḷ moḻiyāṅkāl muṉ piṉ toṭarpiṉṟi oṉṟāy niṉṟu taṉittolittalum, aṉpuṭiyāriruvar oruvar oruvari iṉṟiyamiyātu nōkki niṟkumāṟupōla iraṇ ṭeḻuttukkaḷ tammuṉ iṇintolittalum, ivvāṟaṉṟi mutaleḻuttiṉi iraṇṭāvatum iraṇṭāmeḻuttiṉi mūṉṟāvatum iṅṅaṉamē piṉvarum eḻuttukkaḷum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākat toṭarntu niṉṟolittalum eṉa im mūvaki oli niliyum moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟattuṭaṉ oruṅkutōṉṟiya tamiḻ neṟiyākum. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya mūvaki moḻi niliyiṉit teḷiya uṇarnta vittiyanāta nāvalar tāmiyaṟṟiya ilakkaṇa viḷakka nūlil,
eḻuttē taṉittum iṇintun toṭarntum
patamām poruḷtariṉ atupakāp patampaku
patameṉa āyiru pakutit teṉpa
ittalic cūttiram moḻiyākkamum ataṉ pakutiyum uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi eytiniṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ tāmē taṉittu ōreḻuttākiyum, iṇintu īreḻuttākiyum, iraṇṭaṉiyiṟantu toṭarntu pala eḻuttākiyum niṉṟu poruḷi viḷakkumeṉiṉ avveḻut tukkaḷ moḻi eṉappaṭum eṉac cūttiramuṅ karuttum poruḷum kūṟi viḷakkiyuḷḷār. tolkāppiyar oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, pala eṉa vaṭanūl vaḻakkiṉip piṉpaṟṟi iṅṅaṉam moḻikaḷi mūvakippaṭuttiṉār eṉak kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉārumuḷar. oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, pala eṉṉum eṇṇu nilikkum taṉit tolittal, iṇintolittal, toṭarn tolittal eṉṉum eḻuttiṉ oli nilikkum vēṟupāṭuṇmiyi aṉṉōr aṟintilar. aṉṟiyum tamiḻ nūliṟpōṉṟu vaṭanūl ilakkaṇa marapiṉ ēkākṣara patam, tuvikṣarapatam pakuvakṣara patam eṉa vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉmiyum karutaṟkuriyatu.
āciriyar ceyyuḷiyalil uyiril leḻuttu meṇṇap paṭā eṉpataṉāl oṟṟumutaliyaṉa eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāveṉa āṇṭukkūṟiya vitiyi vaḻakkiṉi ṭattum mēṟkoṇṭu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar oṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṭṭi eḻuttākak kōṭal āciriyar karuttaṉṟeṉak kūṟiṉār. nāku mutaliya coṟkaḷil kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum eḻuttākak koṇṭu avaṟṟi īreḻuttoru moḻi eṉavum, vaṇṭu, pattu, mutaliya kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷil iṭiyē varum meyyeḻuttukkaḷiyum eḻuttākaveṇṇi avaṟṟi iraṇṭiṟanticikkun toṭar moḻiyākak koṇṭu vaṉ ṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar eṉavum peyarkūṟippōtalāl eḻuttati kārattu oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramuṅ kūṭṭi eṇṇi moḻiyākak kōṭal āciriyarkku uṭaṉpāṭātal peṟappaṭum.
(u-m) ā, maṇi, varaku, eṉavarum.
		meyyi ṉiyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum.		 	 (tol. 46)
itu taṉi meykaḷ iyaṅkum muṟi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) taṉi meykaḷiṉ naṭappu akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum (eṉṟavāṟu) meykaḷ nāvāl uruvāka iyakkum iyakkamē yaṉṟik kiyāl vaṭivāka iyakkum nilimik kaṇṇum akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkumeṉpatu muṉṉar akaraṅ kalanta vaṭivē eḻutip piṉṉar mey nilimi kāṭṭap puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutalāṟ pulaṉām.
iṅṅaṉam meykkaṇ akaraṅ kalantu niṟkumāṟu pōlap patiṉōruyirk kaṇṇum akaraṅkalantu niṟkumeṉpataṉip piṟarkku uṇarttutalarumi karuti, āciriyar kūṟātoḻintār; `iṟivaṉ iyaṅkutiṇik kaṇṇum nilittiṇikkaṇṇum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyāy niṟkumāṟu ellārkkum oppa muṭintāṟpōla, akaramum uyirkkaṇṇum taṉi meyk kaṇṇum kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyākiyē niṟku meṉpatu cāṉṟōrkkellām oppa muṭintatu. akaramutala eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāṉ akaramākiya mutaliyuṭiya eḻuttukka ḷellām, atupōla, iṟivaṉākiya mutaliyuṭittu ulakam eṉa vaḷḷuvaṉār uvami kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, kaṇṇaṉ `eḻuttukkaḷil akaramākiṉṟēṉ yāṉē eṉak kūṟiyavāṟ ṟāṉum piṟa nūlkaḷāṉum uṇarka veṉat teḷivuri kūṟiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
taṉṉiyuṇarttātu vēṟu poruḷuṇarttuñ col pōla ṭaṟalaḷa (cūt. 23) veṉpaṉa uyir meyyiyuṇart tātu taṉimeyyi yuṇarttalāṉum oṟṟiṉi yuyirmey pōlac collukiṉṟa vaḻuvamitiyilakkaṇattāṉum, ic cūttiram moḻimarapiṉ kaṇṇatāyiṟṟu.
ivviti iṉṟiyamiyātatākavum curukka nūlātaliṉ naṉṉūlār itaṉik kūṟiṟṟilar.
		tammiyal kiḷappiṉ ellāveḻuttum
meynnili mayakka māṉa milli			 (tol. 47)
itu meymayakkattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ellā meyyeḻuttum moḻiyiṭiyiṉṟit tam vaṭiviṉiyalpic collumiṭattu mey mayakka niliyil mayaṅki varutal kuṟṟamaṉṟu (eṉṟavāṟu)
(u-m) valleḻut tiyipiṉ ṭakaramākum. (puḷḷi 
mayaṅku. 7)
iṅṅaṉam mayaṅkivarutal moḻiyāyt toṭarumiṭat tākalāṉ itu moḻimarapiṉkaṇ vikkappaṭṭatu.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyāciriyar karutti maṟuttu itu muṉṉar meykkaṇ uyir niṉṟavāṟu kūṟi, avvuyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy niṉṟa kālattu ammeyyāṟ peyarpeṟu māṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉak karutturittu, `paṉṉīruyirum vaṉmi, meṉmi iṭimiyākiya meyyiṉ taṉmiyilē tammuyirt taṉmi mayaṅkiṟṟākap peyar kūṟiṉ kuṟṟamilli eṉap poruḷ kūṟi, eṉṟatu, valleḻuttu melleḻuttu iṭi yeḻuttu eṉa uyirmeykkum peyariṭṭāḷutal kūṟiṟṟu, eṉa viḷakkamuṅ kūṟiṉar. uyir meyyi vēṟṟumi nayaṅkaruti iraṇṭeḻuttākak kūṟum āciriyar, valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē `melleḻuttiyiyi ṉiṟutiyo ṭuṟaḻum, iṭiyeḻutteṉpa yaralavaḻaḷa veṉṟaviṭattu ōreḻuttākak kūṟiṉāreṉṟal poruntātākalāṉum, valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē yeṉpuḻi valleḻut teṉpataṉi uyirmeyyeḻuttākak koḷḷiṉ avveḻuttu mikum eṉpatu avvuyirmeyyeḻuttu mikumeṉap poruḷpaṭṭu māṟukoḷḷumātalāṉum, vaṉmi, meṉmi, iṭimiyākiya taṉmi meykkēyaṉṟi uyirk killāmi yāṉum, valleḻuttu melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu eṉṟa peyarkaḷ uyir meykkaṉṟi meykkē yuriyaṉaveṉpatu avaṟṟit tokuttu ammūvāṟum eṉac cuṭṭi mey mayakkaṅ kūṟutalāṟ pulaṉāmātalāṉum uriyāciriyar kūṟiya vāṟu iccūttiram mey mayakkattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi yeṉpatē poruntumeṉka.
mey mayakkattiṟku avaṟṟuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ eṉpataṉik kāṭṭil aḵtirumoḻik kaṇṇateṉa maṟukka eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. āciriyar ivvōttiṉ kaṇ oru moḻi toṭarmoḻi yeṉṉum vēṟupāṭiṉṟip potuvāka moḻi yiṭi yeḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟāreṉpatu, orumoḻi puṇarmoḻikaḷil varum kuṟṟiyalukara kuṟṟiyalikaraṅ kaḷiyum āytattiyun kūṟutalāṟ peṟappaṭumātaliṉ, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟu āciriyar karuttaṉṟeṉka. im meymayakkap puṟaṉaṭiyi naṉṉūlār mutaṉili yeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyōṭu cērttuk kūṟuvar.
īroṟṟuṭaṉili
		yaraḻaveṉṉu mūṉṟu moṟṟak
ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟṟākum.			 (tol. 48)
mēṟkūṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ moḻiyāmiṭattu vēṟupaṭṭa mey kaḷiraṇṭum oṟṟāy uṭaṉiṟkum niliyi ic cūttirattāṟ kūṟukiṉṟār āciriyar.
(i-ḷ) yaraḻa veṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu oṟṟāy niṟpa, avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē ka, ca, ta, pa kkaḷil oṉṟātal, ṅa, ña, na, ma kkaḷil oṉṟātal oṟṟāy vara avi īroṟṟāy niṟkum. eṉṟavāṟu.

		avaṟṟuḷ,
rakāra, ḻakāraṅ kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.
(tol. 49)
iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum rakāramum, ḻakāramum kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy vārā. eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē avi neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyir meyyām.
(u-m) tār, tāḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy niṉṟaṉa. karu, maḻu, eṉak kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyāy niṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu iraṇṭiyum vilakkiṉamiyiṉ oḻinta yakaram, poy, nōy eṉa īriṭattum varumeṉpatām. `kuṟṟoṟṟu = kuṟitākiya oṟṟu eṉap paṇput toki kuṟiṟkīḻ niṟṟalāṉ kuṟiyateṉap paṭṭatu eṉpar uriyāciriyar. kīḻ eṉṉum urupu tokuttuk kūṟiṉāreṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. eṉavē avar kuṟṟoṟ ṟeṉpataṉi vēṟṟumit tokiyākak koṇṭār eṉpatu pulaṉām.
ivviraṇṭu cūttirattālum kūṟappaṭṭa vitiyi naṉṉūlār uṭaṉili mayakkattiṉ kīḻvarum,
		ya, ra, ḻa, voṟṟiṉmuṉ ka, ca, ta, pa, ṅa, ña, na, ma,
īroṟṟām; īḻattaṉik kuṟilaṇiyā.		    	    (naṉ. 119)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl tokuttuk kūṟiṉār.
		kuṟumiyum neṭumiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṟ
ṟoṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻuttiyala.		 (tol. 50)
itu mēlic cūttirattiṉ mēleḻunta aiyam akaṟṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) uyireḻuttiṟkuk kuṟumiyum, neṭumiyum māttiriyeṉṉum aḷavupaṟṟik koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ toṭar moḻi yiṟutiyil niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāra mellām neṭiṟ kīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpi yuṭiyaṉavām. eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉṟatu, mēlic cūttirattu ra, ḻa, eṉpaṉa kuṟiṟ kīḻ oṟṟākā eṉṟu kūṟiyatu koṇṭu kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉatāy yāṇṭum rakāra, ḻakāram vārā eṉpatu karutip pukar, pukaḻ eṉpaṉa yāṇṭaṭaṅku meṉpāri nōkkip pukaḻ, pulavar eṉṟāṟpōla akkuṟilkaḷ iṇintu niṟka avaṟṟit toṭarntuvariṉ ak kuṟiliṇikaḷum neṭiliṉ taṉmiyi yuṭiyaṉa vātalāṉ, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ varum rakara ḻakaraṅkaḷum neṭṭeḻuttiṉpiṉ niṉṟaṉavākavē koḷḷappaṭu meṉṟavāṟām.
ivvāṟaṉṟi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, iccūttiram aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyali nōkkiya nūṉmarapiṟ cūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭiyāy ataṉkaṇ uyirum uyir meyyum aḷapiṟantu icikkuṅkāṟ kuṟilō neṭilō icippateṉa māṇākkarkku nikaḻvatōr aiyamakaṟṟukiṉṟa teṉak karutturittu, iccūttirattu neṭṭeḻuttiyal eṉṟa toṭarkku neṭiṟkīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpi yuṭiyavām eṉa uriyāciriyar uritta vaḻiyē uriyātu, `iyala eṉpataṉic ceyaveṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyecca mākak koṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟiya taṉmiyum neṭiya taṉmiyum māttiri yeṉṉum uṟuppiṉic cevikaruviyāka aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭkuk koḷḷap paṭutaliṉ am māttiri tammuḷ toṭarntu niṉṟa col lellām neṭṭeḻuttu māttiri mikku naṭakkum paṭiyākat toṭarnta collām - eṉap poruḷ kūṟiṉār.
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum eṉṟa cūttiram ici nūlil uyirum oṟṟum taṉ māttiriyil nīṇṭolittalik kūṟiya tātal, accūttirattiṟku iḷampūraṇareḻutiya uriyālum ataṉip piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉṉum uttikku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākap pērāciriyar kuṟittalāṉum pulaṉāmātaliṉ, ici nūṟkuk kūṟum ivvilakkaṇam ceyyuḷiyalukkumuṇṭeṉac cūttirac coṟṟoṭarip pirittuk kūṟutal poruntātu. aṉṟiyum ceyyuḷiyal vitiyi itumāṭṭeṟintateṉpataṟ kuriya coṟṟoṭar itaṉuṭkāṇappaṭāmiyāṉum āṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum viti aḷapeṭikkaṇ aṭaṅku mātalāṉum, mēlic cūttirattil kuṟṟoṟṟu eṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiyata ṉāl varum aiyamakaṟṟutal vēṇṭumātalāṉum nacciṉārkkiṉi yaruri ic cūttirattiṉ karuttaṉ ṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aṉṟiyum neṭṭeḻuttāvatu, kuṟṟeḻuttiraṇṭu niṉṟu nīṇṭicippa tākaliṉ kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ iraṇṭu mutaliyaṉa vākat toṭarntaṉavellām neṭṭeḻuttiṉiyalpuṭiya veṉpatu poruntum neṭṭeḻuttāvatu nīrum nīrum cērntāṟ pōlak kuṟṟeḻuttu iraṇṭottu niṉṟu nīṇṭicippa toṉṟākaliṉ eṉavarum civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟṟāṉumkuṟaliṇi mutaliyaṉavākat toṭarnta kuṟṟeḻut tukkaḷ neṭṭeḻuttiṉ iyalpuṭiya eṉpatu pōtarum. ikkaruttē paṟṟi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ākāram kuṟukutalik kūṟa vantaviṭattuk kuṟiṟkīḻ ākāram akarameṉak kuṟukumeṉṟuvari yātu av ākārattiṉ uṟuppākiya orumāttiri yaḷaviṉi yuṭiya akaram keṭumeṉpatupaṭa,
kuṟiyata ṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭa vukaram
aṟiyavarutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē 			(tol. 156)
eṉac cūttirañ ceytalāṉum - viraltītu eṉpatu mutalāka varum kuṟaliṇi yoṟṟi neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟeṉak karutik keṭuttaṟ kēṟpa, piṉṉar 160-l neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutalum eṉa viti kūṟippōtalāṉum, piṉṉarum pala iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟiliṇi eḻuttukkaḷi neṭiliyalpiṉavākak koṇṭu viti kūṟutalāṉum iḷampūraṇa ruriyē āciriyar karuttōṭu oṭṭiyatātal naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum.
		ceyyu ṉiṟutip pōlu moḻivayiṉ
ṉakāra makāra mīroṟṟākum.				  (tol. 51)
itu ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyamāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ceyyuṭkaṇ pōlum eṉṉum colliṉiṟuti yil ṉākaramum, makāramum, vantu īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyāy niṟku meṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) `annūli - muṉṉūlākak koḷvāṉum pōṉm eṉa varum. pōli moḻivayiṉ eṉṟapāṭam nacciṉārk kiṉiyaratu aṉṟeṉpatu, `pōlum eṉṉuñ ceyyumeṉṉu muṟṟu īṟṟumiciyukaram meyyoḻittuk keṭṭu lakāram tirintu niṉṟatu eṉavarum avaruriyāṟ pulaṉām.
		ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.			  (tol. 52)
itu mēl īroṟṟāy varum eṉṟa makaram taṉmāt tiriyiṟ kuṟukumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta makaram taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟuki niṟkum.
iṉit taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl ṇakāra voṟṟiṉ muṉṉum makaraṅ kuṟukutal koḷka. `maruḷiṉum ellām maruṇm eṉavarum, eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar orōvaḻi ṇakārat tiṉ muṉ makaram īroṟṟāy niṉṟu ari māttiriyiṟ kuṟuki niṟṟalit taḻīiyiṉār. ivvāṟu ceyyuḷakattu lakāra ḷakāra voṟṟut tirintami kāraṇamāka ṉakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ varum makaravoṟṟu muṉṉark kūṟiyavāṟē taṉ ari māttiriyiṟ kuṟuki avaṟṟuṭaṉ īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyāy varutali naṉṉūlār,
		laḷamey tirinta ṉaṇamuṉ makāram
nintī roṟṟām ceyyu ḷuḷḷē.			   (naṉ. 120)
eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟippiṭuvar.
		moḻippaṭut ticippiṉun terintuvēṟicippiṉum eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar.				 (tol. 53)
iḵtu eḻuttukkaṭku moḻikkaṇ māttiri kāraṇamākap piṟappatōr aiyan tīrkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) moḻikkaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum terintu koṇṭu vēṟu colliṉum uyirum meyyumākiya eḻut tukkaḷ tattam māttiri mutaliya iyalpil tirintu nillā eṉṟavāṟu.
mēl `pōlum eṉṟa moḻiyiṉ makaram taṉ ari yaḷapiṟ kuṟivatu pōṉṟu ēṉiyeḻuttukkaḷ tammiyalpu vēṟupaṭumiyal piṉavō eṉṟu aiyuṟuvāri nōkki, moḻik kaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum terintu koṇṭu vēṟē col liṉum eḻuttukkaḷ tattam māttiriyiyalpil tiriyāveṉak kūṟit teḷivittār.
īṇṭu eḻuttiyal tiriyāveṉṟatu moḻikkaṭpaṭṭut terintu vēṟicikkappaṭum muppateḻuttukkaḷiyumē. terintu vēṟicikkappaṭu miyalpiṉṟi moḻiyic cārntu varuvaṉa mūṉṟum, cārntuvarutalākiya tammiyalpilaṉṟi vēṟu vakiyāṟ ṟiripupaṭa vicippaṉa vākāvātaliṉ ivviti avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭuvataṉṟām. moḻikkaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum terintu vēṟu colliṉum eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉavē ivaṟṟāṟ poruḷ tiriyumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu. vēṟu icittal eṉṟataṉāl eṭuttal paṭuttal mutaliya ōci vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉpatu koḷka. eṭuttal paṭuttalāl māttiri vēṟupaṭṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭumeṉpati kātu, kaṭṭu mutaliya kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ itaḻkuvittu muṟṟakkūṟiyaviṭattum itaḻ kuviyāmal kuṟiyak kūṟiyaviṭattum poruḷ vēṟupaṭumeṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatu koṇṭu aṟiyalām,  oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum īṇṭu eḻutteṉak koṇṭār āciriyar eṉpatu muṟ kūṟappaṭṭa tātaliṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruri āciriyar karuttaṉṟām. ivvāṟē eḻuttukkaḷ taṉittuc collumiṭattup pōla moḻiyāyt toṭarumiṭattum tammiyalpiṟ ṟiriyātu niṟkumeṉpataṉi naṉṉūlār.
		moḻiyāyt toṭariṉum muṉṉaṉit teḻuttē.       	    (naṉ. 127)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār. caṅkara namaccivāyarum eḻuttu, cuṇṇat tiṉkaṇ aricaṉa mutaliya pōlātu māliyiṉkaṇ malarpōl avaṟṟil niṟṟaliṉ muṉṉaṉit teṉṟār eṉa viḷakki yurittār.

3. pōli
aḵtāvatu ōreḻuttiṉip pōṉṟu piṟaveḻuttukkaḷ niṉṟolittal.
		akara vikaram aikāramākum.				 (tol. 54)
(i-ḷ) akaramum ikaramum kūṭṭic colla aikāram pōl vicikkum.
(u-m) aivaṉam, aivaṉam - eṉavarum.
		akara vukaram aukāramākum.				 (tol. 55)
(i-ḷ) akaramum ukaramuṅ kūṭi aukāram pōla vicikkum.
(u-m) auvi, auvi eṉavarum.
ākum eṉa ākkac coṟkoṭuttu ōtiyavataṉāl ivvolikaḷ ceyaṟki eṉpatu uṇara vittār.
		akarattimpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
aiye ṉeṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.		 (tol. 56)
(i-ḷ) akarattiṉ piṉ ikaramēyaṉṟi yakara mākiya puḷḷiyum aiyeṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivupeṟat tōṉṟum.
(u-m) aivaṉam, ayvaṉam - eṉavarum. `mey peṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟataṉāl akarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaramēyaṉṟiyum, vakarap puḷḷiyum aukārampōla varumeṉak koḷka 
auvi - avvi eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. ivveḻuttup pōlikaḷi naṉṉūlār.
		amma ṉikaram yakara meṉṟivi
eytiṉ aiyot ticikkum; avvōṭu
uvvum vavvum auvō raṉṉa.			    (naṉ. 125)
eṉpataṉāṟ ṟokutturittār. ic cūttirattiṟku akarattiṉ muṉ ikaramum yakaravoṟṟum varumāyiṉ aikāram pōṉṟolikkum. akarattōṭu ukaramum vakara voṟṟum varumāyiṉ aukāram pōṉṟolikkum eṉap poruḷ kūṟi, (u-m) a. i=ai; ay = ai; ki = ki; kay - ki; a.u = au; ku = ku; eṉa utāraṇamuṅ kāṭṭiṉar caṅkara namaccivāyar.
mēlic cūttirattil tolkāppiyaṉār akarattiṉ muṉ vakaramey vantu aukāram pōla volikku meṉak kūṟātirukkavum naṉṉūlār ataṉi iccūttirattiṟ kūṟiyatu, - meypeṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟataṉāl vakarap puḷḷiyum aukāram pōla varumeṉak koḷka eṉa accūttirattiṟku uriyā ciriyar uritta ciṟappuri karutiyeṉka. ivviti tolkāppiyark kuṭaṉpāṭāyiṉ,
akarat timpar vakarap puḷḷiyum
auve ṉeṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉṟoru cūttiram ceytiruppar.
avvāṟuriyāmi yoṉṟē, ivviti avarkku uṭaṉ pāṭaṉṟeṉpatip pulappaṭuttalāṉum, piṟappiyalil,
u,ū,o,ō au veṉa vicikkum
appālintum itaḻ kuvintiyalum
eṉak kūṟumāṟṟāl itaḻ kuvitalāṟ piṟakkum aukāram vīṟṟōcikkum toḻiṟpāṭṭukkum pallitaḻiyiya vakaram piṟakkum eṉṟataṉāl mēṟpallik kīḻitaḻ iyiyap piṟakkum vakarattiṉ ōcikkum toḻiṟ pāṭṭukkum periyatoru vēṟupāṭṭiṉi āciriyar viritturittalāṉum, akaramum vakaramuṅkūṭi au eṉa olittal tolkāppiyar kālattu vaḻakkaṉ ṟeṉpatu pulaṉākum. aṅṅaṉamākavum va.u. citamparampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ, tām patippitta tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāram iḷampūraṇaruriyil,
		akarattimpar yavakarap puḷḷiyum
 ai au neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
eṉap pāṭattiṉit tirittup putupporuḷ koḷḷutal muṟṟum poruntāta toṉṟām.
civañāṉa muṉivar ammuṉikaram eṉṉum eḻuttup pōli yuṇarttum naṉṉūṟ cūttirattiṉic cantiyakkara muṇart tiṟṟākakkoṇṭu, akaramum yakara meyyum ikaramuṅkūṭi aiyeṉṉum eḻuttāka volikkum eṉavum, avvāṟē akaramum vakarameyyum ukaramuṅ kūṭi auveṉṉum eḻuttāka volikkum eṉavum poruḷkūṟi, ik karuttē paṟṟi āciriyar, akara vikara mikāramākum  akaravukara mukāramākum eṉak kūṟi aiyeṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivu pulappaṭuttaṟku akara ikaraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē yakaramu motticikku meṉpār, akarattimpar yakarappuḷḷiyum - aiyeṉ neṭuñ ciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟum, meypeṟa veṉṟa vilēcāṉē, auveṉṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivu pulap paṭuttaṟku akara vukaraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē vakaramum otticikkum eṉṟum, impar umpar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa kālavaki iṭavaki kaḷāṉ mayaṅku mākaliṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṟpatu yātō iṟutikkaṇ niṟpatu yātōveṉṉum aiya nīkkutaṟku ikara yakara miṟuti viravum eṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār eṉavum cūttira viruttiyuḷ kuṟippiṭuvar.
akara vikaramikāramākum, akaravukaramukāramākum eṉa iru cūttirattum avaṟṟiṉ vitiyi āciriyar muṭittuk kūṟiṉārātalāṉum, akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaramēyaṉṟi yakarappuḷḷi variṉum aikāram pōla vicikkumeṉpataṟkē.
akarattimpar yakarappuḷḷiyum
 aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉa āciriyar ummi koṭuttuk kūṟiṉārātalāṉum ic cūttirattu `meypeṟa eṉṟatu, ilēcāy vantataṉṟi, akaramum yakarappuḷḷiyum kūṭi aikāram pōla volikkumpoḻutu uyirīṟāka olikkum iyaṟki aikāra vīṟupōlaṉṟi ippōliyīṟu meyyōcipeṟat tōṉṟi yicikkumeṉṟa vēṟṟumiyi viḷakkutaṟku vanta aṭimoḻiyākalāṉum avar kūṟiyavāṟu; a, y, i eṉṟa mūṉṟuṅkūṭi aiyeṉavum a, v, u eṉṟa mūṉṟuṅkūṭi auveṉavum olikkumeṉiṉ, avviṭattu ai, au veṉṟa oliyiṉ vēṟāka ayyi, avvu, eṉa māttiri mikku oli vēṟupāṭṭicit talāṉum, ikara yakara miṟutiviravum eṉṟa cūttiram ikaramum yakaramum oru moḻiyiṉ iṟutikkaṇ `nāy `nāi eṉa viravi iṟutip pōliyāy varutalik kuṟitta vēṟu cūttiramākaliṉ 54-m cūttiramākiya itaṟkum 58-m cūttiramākiya ataṟkum iṭiyē aikārak kuṟukkampaṟṟiya cūttiram amintiruttalāl cantiyakkaram koḷḷutaṟkuriya toṭarpiṉmiyāṉum ai, au veṉpaṉa vaṟṟic cantiyakkaramākak kōṭal tolkāppiyaṉār karuttaṉṟātal pulappaṭum.
aṉṟiyum iccūttiraṅkaḷ aikāra ōḷakāraṅkaḷiṉ kūṭṭoliyiṉi uṇarttiṉavāyiṉ āciriyar iyaṟkip poruḷi iṟṟeṉak kūṟātu ceykip poruḷukkuriya ākkaṅ koṭuttuc collutal poruntātu. eṉavē ic cūttiraṅkaḷ ceyaṟki yōciyāyp pōliyeḻuttuṇarttiṉa eṉavē paṭum. naṉṉūlārkkum ituvē karuttātal ammuṉikaram eṉṟa cūttirattiṟkuc caṅkara namaccivāyar uritta uriyāṟ pulaṉām.
		ōraḷapāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
tēruṅ kāli moḻivayi ṉāṉa.			  (tol. 57)
iḵtu uyirkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttiric curukkam kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aikāram ārāyuṅ kāli moḻikkaṇ oru māttiriyāy niṟkumiṭamum uṇṭu.
(u-m) iṭiyaṉ, kuvaḷi eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattil aikāram atikārappaṭṭamiyāl ivviti aikāramoṉṟaṟkē yurittāmeṉak koṇṭa uriyāciriyar, `tēruṅkāli yeṉpataṉāṉ mutaṟkaṇ curuṅkāteṉakkūṟi, ikkuṟukkañ ciṟupāṉmiyeṉavuṅ kūṟip pōntār.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iccūttirattu aukārattiyuṅ cērtturittu aukārakkuṟukkameṉa oṉṟu koṇṭatōṭu amiyātu, uriyiṟ kōṭalāl aikāram mutaliṭikaṭi yeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattuṅ kuṟukumeṉavum aukāram mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukumeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār. ikkaruttēpaṟṟi naṉṉūlārum,
		taṟcuṭ ṭaḷavoḻi aimmū vaḻiyum
niyum, auvum mutalaṟ ṟākum.			    (naṉ. 95)
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār. ikkūṟṟiṉi maṟukkap pōnta civañāṉamuṉivar iṭiyaṉ, maṭiyaṉ, paṉi, maṉiyeṉ puḻik kuṟukutalpōl, vikalum vikal varak kaṇṭum eṉpuḻi aikāram mutaṟkaṭ kuṟukāmi cevikaruviyākap pulap paṭumātalāṉum, aṉṟiyum vikalum vikal eṉpuḻikkuṟuku māyiṉ, vikal eṉpataṉik kuṟiliṇiyoṟṟeṉṟicittal vēṇṭum; vēṇṭavē veṇṭaḷi citittalāṉum, moḻippaṭut ticippiṉum terintu vēṟicippiṉum eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉa ellā eḻuttiṟkum potuppaṭak kūṟiya vitiyōṭu muraṇi, aikāram terintu vēṟicittaṟ kaṇ tiriyāteṉavum moḻippaṭut ticippiṉ yāṇṭu  variṉun tiriyumeṉavum poruḷkūṟutal poru ntāmiyāṉum, ōraḷapākum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē eṉṟavummi yāṉ, ōraḷapākā iṭaṉu muṇṭeṉpatu tāṉē peṟappaṭuta lāṉum, īṇṭu iṭamāvatu moḻimutal iṭikaṭi eṉa mūṉṟēyaṉṟi vēṟiṉmiyāṉum, aikāram moḻimutaṟ kuṟukāteṉavē koḷka. īṇṭuk kāṭṭiya ētukkaḷāṉum āciriyar ōtāmiyāṉum piṟarum kuṟukiya mūvuyir eṉṟē moḻitalāṉum aukārak kuṟukkameṉa voṉṟiṉmi uṇarka; iḵtu uriyāciriyaruriyāṉum aṟika eṉak kūṟumāṟṟāṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōr koḷkiyi maṟuttu uriyāciriyar uriyit taḻuvik kūṟiṉami īṇṭu oppu nōkki uṇarattakkatu. eṉavē aukārak kuṟukkam eṉa oṉṟu iṉṟeṉpatum, aikāram moḻimutaliṟ kuṟukā teṉpatum āciriyar karuttātal viḷaṅkum.
		ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum.				 (tol. 58)
ituvum pōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ikaramum yakara meyyum oru moḻiyiṉ iṟutikkaṇ ōci viravivarum.
(u-m) nāy, nāi eṉavarum. ippōliyiṉi iṟantatu vilakkaleṉṉum uttiyāl naṉṉulār tam nūliṟ kūṟā toḻintār.
moḻimutal eḻuttukkaḷ
		paṉṉīru ruyirum moḻi mutalākum.		uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā.
ka, ta, na, pa, ma veṉu māvin teḻuttum
ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.	     (tol. 59, 60, 61)
eṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷāl moḻikku mutalāmeḻutti uṇarttavanta āciriyar, paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttukkaḷum moḻikku mutalām eṉavum, uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyal lātaṉavākiya taṉi meykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā, uyirōṭu kūṭiṉa meykaḷē mutalāvaṉa eṉavum, aṅṅaṉam uyirōṭu kūṭi mutalāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ ka, ta, na, pa, ma eṉṉum aintu meykaḷum paṉṉīruyirōṭum moḻikku mutalāvaṉaveṉavuṅ kūṟip, piṉṉarvarum 62 mutaliya nāṉku cūttiraṅkaḷāl ca, va, ña, ya eṉṟa nāṉku meykaḷum paṉṉīruyir kaḷil iṉṉiṉṉa eḻuttukkaḷōṭum kūṭi moḻikku mutalāmeṉkiṉṟār.
naṉṉūlāciriyar, mūṉṟu cuṭṭum yā viṉāvum ekara viṉāvumāy iṭic coṟkaḷiṉpiṉ akaratti oṭṭi ṅavvum aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam, eṅṅaṉam eṉa oruvāṟṟāṉ moḻikku mutalāmeṉak koṇṭār. ataṉāl
		cuṭṭiyā vekara viṉāvaḻi yavvi
oṭṭi ṅavvum mutalā kummē.			   (naṉ. 106)
eṉattām cūttirañ ceytataṟkēṟpa ṅakarattiyum tolkāppi yaṉār kuṟitta ka, ca, ta, na, pa, ma, va, ya, ña eṉṉum oṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭi ippattu meyyum moḻikku mutalāmeṉakkoṇṭu,
		paṉṉīru ruyiruṅ kacatana pamavaya
ñaṅa vīrin tuyirmeyyu moḻimutal.		   	   (naṉ. 102)
eṉac cūttirañ ceytaṉar. aṅṅaṉam mutalāya coṟkaḷil ṅaṉameṉpatu, taṉiyē niṉṟu poruḷ tarātu cuṭṭu viṉāvākiya eḻuttukkaḷi yoṭṭi niṉṟu oru collāyp poruḷuṇart taliṉ, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārāl moḻikku mutalā meḻuttukkaḷil ṅakaram cērkkappaṭātāyiṟṟu.
		cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai auveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē.			     (naṉ. 62)
(i-ḷ) cakaramākiya taṉi meyyum a, ai, au, veṉṉu mūṉṟuyirum allātaviṭattu muṟkūṟiyavi pōla avi tavira ēṉi uyirōṭuṅ kūṭi moḻikku mutalām (eṉṟavāṟu). eṉavē a, ai, au, eṉṉum mūṉṟu uyirōṭuṅkūṭic cakaram moḻikku mutalākāteṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭatu.
ciyam curiyam eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu vaṭacoṟ kaḷilum, caṭṭi, camaḻppu eṉpaṉapōlat tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkuppiṉ tōṉṟik kaṭicollilli kālattup paṭiṉē eṉa avarāl taḻuvikkoḷḷappaṭṭa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉum cakaramey a, ai, au, eṉpataṉōṭu mutalāy varutal karutic cakaram paṉṉīruyiroṭuṅ kūṭi moḻi mutalāmeṉak koṇṭār naṉṉūlār.
		u, ū, o, ō, eṉṉu nāṉkuyir
va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varuta lilli.			    (naṉ. 63)
(i-ḷ) u, ū, o, ō, eṉṉum nāṉkuyirum vakaramey yōṭu moḻikku mutalāy varutalilli eṉpatām. eṉavē oḻintaṉa vaṟṟōṭu moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
		u, ū, o, ō valavoṭu vammutal			   (naṉ. 103)
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār naṉṉūlār.
		ā, e,
o veṉu mūvuyir ñakāratturiya.			 (tol. 64)
(i-ḷ) ā, e, o, eṉṟa mūṉṟuyirum ñakarameyyoṭu kūṭi mutalātaṟkuriya.
eṉavē ēṉiyīṟōṭu ñakaram mutalākāteṉpatu peṟṟām. naṉṉūlār ñamali eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattuc coṟkaḷ piṟkālattut tōṉṟiṉami kaṇṭu ñakaram akarattōṭum mutalāmeṉak koṇṭu,
		a, ā, e, ovvoṭāku ñammutal			   (naṉ. 105)
eṉa akarattiyuñ cērttuk kūṟiṉār.
