ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ eṭṭippū. kāvitippū, nampippēṟu eṉa vēṟṟumiyiṉum, nampik kollaṉ, nampic cāṉṟāṉ, nampip piḷḷi eṉa alvaḻiyiṉum mikkaṉa. eṭṭi, kāviti eṉpaṉa tēya vaḻakkākiya ciṟappuppeyarkaḷ eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar iyalpumā ruḷavē.		(tol. 155)
itu viravup poruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) uyartiṇip peyarōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravup peyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉpatām.
ummiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
immūṉṟu cūttira vitiyiṉiyum naṉṉūlār.
		potup peyar uyartiṇip peyarka ḷīṟṟumey
valivariṉ iyalpām, āvi yaramuṉ
vaṉmi mikā, cila vikāramām uyartiṇi.		   (naṉ. 159)
eṉa oru cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār. itaṉkaṇ cila vikāramām uyartiṇi eṉṟatu, cūttiram 154 il kūṟiya ikara vīṟṟut tiripiyum, 153l uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu eṉṟataṉāl uyartiṇip peyaruḷ iyalpaṉṟi muṭivaṉavellām koḷka eṉa uriyāciriyar kāṭṭiya vikāraṅkaḷiyum nōkkiyatākum.
	156.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāl
tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ col muṉvariṉ
meymmi yākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
ammuṟi yiraṇṭum uriyavi uḷavē
vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.		(tol. 156)
itu mēl uyirīṟṟiṟkum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumik kaṭ kūṟum muṭipu peṟātu niṟkum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tiripu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuccollum, valleḻuttiṉatu mikutimēṟcollum muṟimiyāṉ mūṉṟām vēṟṟumikkuriya viṉimutaṟ poruḷāṉ uḷavākiya viṉic coṟkaḷ tam muṉṉarvariṉ iyalpākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟutalu mākiya ammuṟi yiṉiyuṭiya iru ceykiyum peṟutaṟ kuriyaṉavum uḷa. avaṟṟi mēlē vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcci collumiṭattup pōṟṟiyaṟital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nāy kōṭpaṭṭāṉ, puli kōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārap paṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, ivi iyalpu. cūrkōṭ paṭṭāṉ, cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷik kōṭpaṭṭāṉ ivi uṟaḻcci. ivaṟṟuḷ tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ col paṭṭāṉ eṉpatu. kōḷ eṉpatu mutalāka iṭip piṟa varuvaṉa tam toḻilākum.
`puḷḷiyiṟuti uyirīṟu eṉṟataṉāl pēey kōṭ paṭṭāṉ, pēeykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum ekarappēṟum `uriyaviyuḷa eṉṟataṉāl pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pāppuk kōṭppaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilimoḻi yoṟṟut tiriyāmiyum tiritalum koḷka.
im mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tiripiṉi, naṉṉūlār.
		puḷḷiyu muyiru māyiṟu colmuṉ
tammiṉākiya toḻiṉ moḻi variṉē
valliṉam vikaṟpamum iyalpu mākum.			   (naṉ. 256)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.
itaṉkaṇ varum ummiyāṉ cuṟāp pāyap paṭṭāṉ, arāt tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ eṉa vēṟṟumip potuvitiyāṉ mikku muṭivaṉavē perumpālaṉa veṇpar caṅkara namaccivāyar.
		melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum
iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum
uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutalum
cāriyi uḷvaḻic cāriyi keṭutalum
cāriyi uḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyalum
cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum
aḵṟiṇi viravup peyarkku avviyal niliyalum
meypiṟi tākiṭattu iyaṟki yātalum
aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉa
meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarin ticikkum
aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa.			(tol. 157)
iḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tiripu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu valleḻuttum, valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu melleḻuttum tōṉṟutalum, iyalpāy varumiṭattu mikuntu tōṉṟutalum, uyir mikku varavēṇṭiya iṭattuk keṭṭu varutalum, cāriyip pēṟu uḷḷaviṭattut taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum, cāriyiyiṉ iyalpu mikkun tirintum uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟutalum, iyalpāka varum uyar tiṇip peyariṭattu urupu tokātu niṟṟalum, uyartiṇi yōṭu viraviya aḵṟiṇi viravup peyarkku attaṉmiyē urupu tokātē niṟṟalum, mey piṟitātalākiya tiripiṉkaṇ tiriyātu iyaṟkiyāy varutalum, avipōlvaṉa piṟavum, taṉṉiyē nōkkit tiripu naṭakkumiṭattup poruḷ peṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ēṉi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potumuṭiviṉit tāṉ nīkki vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyatu vēṟupaṭṭa puṇarcci eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar.
(u-m) viḷakkuṟittāṉ eṉpatu, melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikkatu; maraṅ kuṟittāṉ eṉpatu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikkatu; tāykkoli eṉpatu, iyalpāy varumiṭattu mikkatu; palāk kuṟittāṉ eṉa uyir mika varumiṭattu uyir keṭṭatu; vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu, cāriyi yuḷvaḻic cāriyi keṭṭatu; vaṇṭiṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyi yuḷvaḻit taṉ urupu niliyiṟṟu; puḷi kuṟittāṉ, puḷikkuṟittāṉ, āl kuṟittāṉ, āṟkuṟittāṉ eṉa ivi, cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟiṉa; nampiyik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ uruputokātu virintuvantatu; koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu, aḵṟiṇi viravup peyarkkaṇ avvāṟu urupu virintu vantatu; maṇ koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu mey piṟitākiṭattu ṇakaram ṭakara māyt tiriyātu iyalpāy vantatu.
aṉṉapiṟavum eṉṟataṉāl kaḻikuṟittāṉ, paṉi piḷantāṉ eṉa iyalpātal koḷka. meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāl, mi koṇarntāṉ - mikkoṇarntāṉ; vilkōḷ - viṟkōḷ eṉa uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum koḷka eṉavum, ivvāṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭivatu perumpāṉmiyum iraṇṭāvatu viṉiyoṭu muṭiyum vaḻippōlum eṉavum, taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl varipāy varuṭi, pulampukkaṉaṉē eṉa ēḻām vēṟṟumi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyum vaḻittiriyum koḷka eṉavum kūṟuvar iḷampūraṇar.
		iyalpiṉ vikāramum vikārattu iyalpum
uyartiṇi yiṭattu virintum tokkum
viravup peyariṉ virintu niṉṟum
aṉṉa piṟavu mākum ai urupē.			   (naṉ. 255)
eṉpataṉāl ittiripiṉik kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
	 	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi i ai yeṉṉum
īṟṟup peyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya
avitām
iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 158)
iḵtu ikara aikāra vīṟṟuppeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) vēṟṟumi yallātaviṭattu i ai eṉṉum iṟutiyi yuṭiya peyarc coṟkaḷ mūvakiyākiya muṭipu niliyi yuṭiya; ammuṭipukaḷtām iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum eṉa iviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar.
(u-m) parutti, kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu ivi iyalpu. mācit tiṅkaḷ, cittirit tiṅkaḷ alikkoṟṟaṉ, pulik koṟṟaṉ ivi mikuti. kiḷi kuṟitu, kiḷik kuṟitu, tiṉi kuṟitu, tiṉik kuṟitu ivi uṟaḻcci.
iyalpu eḻuvāyt toṭar ummit tokikaḷilum, mikuti paṇputtoki uvamit tokikaḷilum, uṟaḻcci cila peyar muṉ eḻuvāyt toṭarilum koḷḷappaṭum.
ikara aikāra vīṟṟu avvaḻikkaṇ uṇṭām. im mūvaki muṭipukaḷiyum.
		avvaḻi iaim muṉṉa rāyiṉ
iyalpum mikalum vikaṟpamum mākum.			   (naṉ. 176)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
		cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum
ekaramutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum
cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum
colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷaveṉa moḻipa.		(tol. 159)
iḵtu ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇartti niṉṟa aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ikara vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, ekaramākiya moḻimutal viṉāviṉi yuṭiya ikara vīṟṟiṭic collum, cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa aikāra vīṟṟu iṭiccollum, yāveṉṉum viṉā viṉi mutaṟkaṇ uṭiya aikāra vīṟṟiṭic collum valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar.
(u-m) atōḷik koṇṭāṉ, itōḷik koṇṭāṉ, āṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭik koṇṭāṉ ivi mikkaṉa. avvaḻi koṇṭāṉ avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ, evvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, evvaḻik koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkavi koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkavik koṇṭāṉ ivi uṟaḻntaṉa.
colliya maruṅku eṉṟataṉāl, paṇṭic cāṉṟōr, oru tiṅkaḷik kuḻavi eṉap piṟa aikāra vīṟu mikku muṭital koḷvar iḷampūraṇar.
		neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutalum
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru piraṭṭalum
aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.			(tol. 160)
itu, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) neṭṭeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭittalum aṟiyumpaṭi vanta muṟimiyiyal eṉpar āciriyar.
īṇṭu `kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ toṭarmoḻi ellām neṭṭeḻuttiyala (tol - eḻuttu 50) eṉṟapaṭi kuṟil iraṇṭu mutalākat toṭarntataṉiyum neṭiyateṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭār āciriyar.
iṅṅaṉam neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvaṉa ṇakāramum, ṉakāramum, makāramum, lakāramum, ḷakāramum eṉa aivakiyām eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
(u-m)kōṇ + nimirntatu = kōṇimirntatu; tāṉ + nallaṉ = tāṉallaṉ; tām + nallar = tānallar; vēl + naṉṟu = vēṉaṉṟu; tōḷ + naṉṟu = tōṇaṉṟu eṉavum, kōl +tītu = kōṟītu; vēl + tītu = vēṟītu eṉavum, nakaramum takaramum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭṭaṉa. maṇ + akal = maṇṇakal; poṉ + akal - poṉṉakal eṉak kuṟiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟut taṉṉurupu iraṭṭiṉa.
aṭutta cūttirattu (161) āṟaṉurupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭutal uyir mutaṉmoḻik kaṇṇateṉpar uriyā ciriyar. ikkaruttē paṟṟi naṉṉūlārum,
		kuṟilaṇi villā ṇaṉakkaḷ vanta
nakaran tirintuḻi naṇṇuṅ kēṭē.			   (naṉ. 210)
eṉavum,
		kuṟilceṟiyālaḷa alvaḻi vanta
takaran tirintapiṟ kēṭum īriṭattum
varunat tirintapiṉ māyvum valivariṉ
iyalpun tiripum āvaṉa vuḷapiṟa.			   (naṉ. 229)
eṉavum varum iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ viḷaṅka urittār.
		āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉuṅ
kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa villi
īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum
neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa			(tol. 161)
iḵtu urupiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟukimuṭiyum aṟuvakip paṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta āṟām vēṟṟumi yurupiṉum nāṉkām vēṟṟumi urupiṉum muṉ nilimoḻikkaṇ iraṭṭi varumeṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttu iraṭṭutalilli; nilimoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkaḷ akaram peṟṟu niṟkum eṉpatām.
aṟuvakippaṭṭa moḻikaḷāvaṉa tām, nām, yām, tāṉ, yāṉ, nī eṉpaṉa. ivi kuvvum atuvum ākiya urupēṟkuṅkāl muṟiyē tam, nam, em, taṉ, eṉ, niṉ eṉa neṭumutal kuṟuki akaram peṟṟu niṟpaṉa.
(u-m) tamatu, namatu, ematu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu, tamakku, namakku, emakku, taṉakku, eṉakku, niṉakku, eṉa varum.
`kuvviṉ avvarum nāṉkāṟu iraṭṭala (247) eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
		numme ṉiṟutiyum annili tiriyātu.			(tol. 162)
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ) neṭumutal kuṟukāta iyaṟkip peyarākiya nummeṉṉum makaravīṟum muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟiraṭṭāmiyum īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum ākiya iyalpiṉip peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) numatu, numakku eṉavarum.
		ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki.			(tol. 163)
itu, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr nili moḻic ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ukarap pēṟṟoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu avvukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉpatām.
avvīṟukaḷāvaṉa piṉ puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ukaram peṟumeṉṟu vitikkum pala īṟukaḷumām.
(u-m) uriñ, yāṉā, aṉantā, porun yāṉā, aṉantā eṉavum varum.
		uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki
aḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
uḷaveṉap paṭṭa ellāc collum
tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ
otta veṉpa ēyeṉ cāriyi.			(tol. 164)
iḵtu aḷavu, niṟi, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ tammuḷ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) uyirum puḷḷiyun tamakku īṟāy aḷaviyum niṟiyiyum eṇṇiyum karuti varuvaṉa vuḷaveṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tattamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tammiṟ kuṟinta aḷavuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ tammuṉṉē varuṅkālan tōṉṟil, ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalip poruntu meṉpar āciriyar.
munti muttiyeṉat tirintu niṉṟatu.
(u-m) nāḻiyē yāḻākku, kalaṉē patakku ivi aḷavup peyar. toṭiyē kaḵcu, koḷḷē yiyavi ivi niṟip peyar. oṉṟē kāl, kālē kāṇi ivi eṇṇup peyar.
`otta veṉpa ēyeṉ cāriyi eṉṟataṉṟi ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟṟē varum eṉak kūṟāmiyiṉ kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi eṉac cāriyi peṟātu varutaluṅ koḷka.
		ariyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikkup
puriva taṉṟāṟ cāriyi iyaṟki.			(tol. 165)
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) aḷavu, niṟi, eṇ eṉṉum mūvakic coṉ muṉṉar varum ari yeṉṟu collavarum poruṭ kūṟṟi yuṇara niṉṟa colliṟku ēyeṉ cāriyi poruntuva taṉṟu.
(u-m) uḻakkari, toṭiyari, oṉṟari eṉac cāriyi peṟātu vantami kāṇka.
		kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli
niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi iyaṟki.		(tol. 166)
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kuṟiyeṉṉuñ col, aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉvaruṅkālattu, mēl vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkuc collum iyalpu niṟiyat tōṉṟum.
vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki yeṉavē ivi vēṟṟumi yaṉṟeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām.
(u-m)urikkuṟi, kalakkuṟi, toṭikkuṟi, koṭkuṟi, kāṇik kuṟi, kāṟ kuṟi eṉa varum.
`muṉ eṉṟataṉāl kalappayaṟu eṉap poruṭ peyarōṭu puṇarum vaḻiyum vēṟṟumi muṭipeytumeṉṟum, niṟiya eṉṟataṉāl kūṟu eṉṟa col varumoḻiyāy varuvaḻiyum nāḻik kūṟu eṉa vēṟṟumi muṭi peytumeṉṟum koḷvar iḷampūraṇar.
		kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē cāriyi.			(tol. 167)
itu kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu aḷavup peyar, niṟippeyar, eṇṇup peyarkaṭku vēṟṟumi muṭipu vilakki iṉ cāriyi vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟu aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku varuñ cāriyi iṉcāriyiyēyām eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) uḻakkiṉ kuṟi, kaḻañciṉ kuṟi, oṉṟiṉ kuṟi, eṉa varum.
		 attiṭi varūum kalameṉ ṉaḷavē.			(tol. 168)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) kalam eṉṉum aḷavup peyar kuṟiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu iṭiyil attuc cāriyi varappeṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) kalam + attu + kuṟi = kalattukkuṟi eṉa varum.
		paṉiyeṉ aḷavuṅ kāveṉ niṟiyum
niṉiyuṅ kāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.			(tol. 169)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) paṉiyeṉṉum aḷavup peyarum kāveṉṉum niṟip peyarum kuṟiyeṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu ārāyuṅ kālattu iṉcāriyiyōṭu poruntum.
(u-m) paṉiyiṉ kuṟi, kāviṉ kuṟi eṉa varum.
`niṉiyuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāl paṉikkuṟi, kākkuṟi eṉa iṉcāriyi peṟātu valleḻuttu mikutaluṅ koḷka eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
		aḷaviṟku niṟiyiṟku moḻi mutalāki
uḷaveṉap paṭṭa oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
avitām
kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā
akara ukaramō ṭaviyeṉa moḻipa.			(tol. 170)
itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ aḷavup peyarkkum niṟip peyarkkum mutalāmeḻuttu iṉit teṉkiṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aḷavup peyarkkum niṟip peyarkkum moḻi mutalāka uḷaveṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa oṉpatu eḻuttām; avitām ka ca ta pa kkaḷum namava kkaḷum akaramum ukaramumākiya avi eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
(u-m) kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal uḻakku eṉavum, kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟi, mā, vari, anti eṉavum aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum vantaṉa. niṟikku ukaramutaṟ peyar kāṇappaṭavilli.
uḷaveṉappaṭṭa veṉṟataṉāṉē uḷaveṉappaṭā, taṉavum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟi, avi immi, ōraṭi, ōrāṭi, iṭā eṉa variyaṟi kūṟātaṉavuṅkoḷka eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
	 	īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ iviyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
meyttalip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
ottavi yuriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē.			(tol. 171)
iḵtu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāya coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyōṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu immoḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu iviyeṉakkūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉatu vitiyiṉāl, muṭiyātu niṉṟa palavaki muṭipukaḷellām uṇmiyit  talippaṭṭa vaḻakkōṭu kūṭip puṇarum moḻikaḷiṉ nilimik kaṇ poruntiṉaviyuriyavām eṉṟavāṟu.
nil koṟṟā, niṟ koṟṟā eṉpataṉkaṇ nilimoḻi tirinta uṟaḻcciyum, tuk koṟṟā, tuññeḷḷā eṉṉum mikutiyum, maṇṇu koṟṟā maṇṇuk koṟṟā, maṉṉu koṟṟā, maṉṉuk koṟṟā uḷḷu koṟṟā, uḷḷuk koṟṟā, kollu koṟṟā, kolluk koṟṟā eṉavarum puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷavi ukaram peṟṟu valleḻuttōṭu uṟaḻtalum, patakku nāṉāḻi eṉa aḷavup peyar akkuc cāriyipeṟutalum, vāṭṭāṉi tōṟṟaṇṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu, takaram varu vaḻikkeṭātu iyalpātalum, cīrakam ari yeṉpatu puṇarumiṭattuc cīrakam eṉpataṉkaṇ makaravoṟṟum kakara voṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaruyirum keṭṭuc cīrakari eṉattiritalum, orumā + ari ivviraṇṭum puṇaruṅkāl varumoḻi akaraṅkeṭṭu orumāriyeṉa muṭitalum, kalam + ari eṉa irucollum puṇarumiṭattuk kalari eṉattirintu muṭitalum, nākam + aṇi eṉum irucollum puṇaru miṭattu nākam eṉpataṉ īṟṟu makaramum akaramum keṭṭu nākaṇiyeṉa muṭitalum piṟavum ippuṟaṉaṭiyāl ami vaṉavām.
		palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāvar eṉṉum
peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalum ēṉi
oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭi
oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum iraṇṭum
maruviṉ pāttiyiṟ ṟiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.			(tol. 172)
itu marūu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) palari aṟiyum col muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭattuḷḷa vakara uyirmey keṭutalum, ēṉi oṉṟaṉiyaṟiyuñ coṉ muṉṉar varum yātu eṉṉum viṉā moḻiyiṭi vakaravuyirmey varutalum ākiya iraṇṭum marūukkaḷatu muṭipiṉiṭattup payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum.
`oṉṟiyavakaram eṉṟataṉāl ivvakaram uyirmey eṉpatu iṉitu pulaṉām.
payiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāl ic coṟkaḷ yāravar. yāvatatu eṉa varumoḻi nilimoḻiyākiya īriṭattum immarūu muṭipiṉip peṟumeṉak koḷka.
6. urupiyal
vēṟṟumi urupukaḷōṭu puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉa miyiṉ iḵtu urupiyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. peyarum ataṉōṭu puṇarum vēṟṟumi yurupum ākiya avviraṇ ṭiṟkum iṭiyē varuñ cāriyikaḷ ivi eṉpatum, vēṟṟumi urupiṉi ēṟkuṅkāl peyarkaḷ peṟum iyalpum tiripumāvaṉa ivi eṉpatum ivviyalil vakuttu viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
		a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum
appāl āṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku iṉṉē cāriyi.			(tol. 173)
iḵtu akara ākāra ūkāra ēkāra aukāra vīṟukaḷ urupiṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) a, ā, u, ū, ē, au eṉṟa appakutippaṭṭa āṟeḻuttiṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya nilimoḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku iṭiyē varum cāriyi iṉ cāriyiyē eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) viḷaviṉi, palāviṉi, kaṭuviṉi, taḻūuviṉi, cēviṉi, vuviṉi eṉavarum. ivvāṟē ēṉi yurupu kaḷiyum oṭṭuka.
		pallavi nutaliya akara viṟupeyar
vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal ecca miṉṟē.			(tol. 174)
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaram vaṟṟuc cāriyiyōṭu poruntutali oḻital illi eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) palavaṟṟi, cilavaṟṟi, uḷḷavaṟṟi, illavaṟṟi eṉavarum.
`eccamiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāl īṇṭum urupu iṉcāriyi peṟṟē muṭiyumeṉṟum, iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mēl iṉpeṟṟaṉa makatti, nilātti yeṉap piṟa cāriyiyum peṟumeṉṟum koḷvar iḷampūraṇar.
pallavi nutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mūṉṟāmurupu cilavaṟṟōṭu eṉa vaṟṟuppeṟṟē muṭiyumeṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		yāveṉ viṉāvum āyiyal tiriyātu.			(tol. 175)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) yāveṉṟu collappaṭum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāp peyarum urupoṭu puṇaruṅkāl muṟkūṟiya muṟiyil tiriyātu vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉavarum.
		cuṭṭu mutalukaram aṉṉōṭu civaṇi
oṭṭiya meyyoḻit tukaraṅ keṭumē.			(tol. 176)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka uṭiya ukara vīṟṟuc col aṉcāriyiyōṭu poruntit tāṉ poruntiya meyyi niṟutti ukaram keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) ataṉi, itaṉi, utaṉi eṉavarum.
		cuṭṭumuta lākiya aiyeṉ iṟuti
vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittē.			(tol. 177)
iḵtu aikāra vīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟukku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra vīṟṟuc colvaṟṟuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti niṟṟalu murittu eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) aviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟi eṉa varum. iṅṅaṉam aikāram niṟka vaṟṟu vantuḻi vaṟṟiṉ vakāram keṭṭu muṭital `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa (122) eṉpataṉāl kūṟappaṭṭatu.
`niṟṟalum urittē eṉṟa ummiyāl, aikāram nillātu keṭutalum uritteṉpatu peṟappaṭum. aikāram keṭṭavaḻi niṉṟa vakarattiṉi vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu, avaṟṟi ivaṟṟi uvaṟṟi eṉa muṭippar uriyāciriyar. iṅṅaṉam aikāramum meyyum keṭṭa cuṭṭup peyarkaḷi `aiyum meyyum keṭṭa viṟuti (eḻuttu 183) eṉpar āciriyar.
niṟṟalumurittē eṉṉum ummiyāṉ vaṟṟoṭu cilavuru piṉkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu niṟṟalu muritteṉak koṇṭu, aviyaṟṟiṟku aviyaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa nāṉkāvatum, ēḻāvatum iṉ peṟṟu vantaṉa veṉṟum, vaṟṟum iṉṉum uṭaṉpeṟutalākiya ivviti oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāl palavaṟṟiṟku, palavaṟṟiṉ kaṇ eṉap pallavi nutaliya akara vīṟṟiṟkum poruntumeṉṟum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
avvitippaṭi vaṟṟuc cāriyiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu ākiya vakaramey keṭṭa niliyil aṟṟu eṉa niṟṟal paṟṟi ataṉi aṟṟuc cāriyiyeṉak koṇṭār naṉṉūlār. (cūttiram 244)
		yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
āyiyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar
āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭuṅ keṭumē.			(tol. 178)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) yāveṉṉum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya aikāra vīṟṟuc collum muṟkūṟiya cuṭṭu mutal aikāram pōla vaṟṟup peṟum avviyalpil tiriyāteṉṟu kūṟuvar, avvīṟṟiṭattu vakaram aikārattōṭu kūṭak keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.
vakaram vaṟṟiṉ mici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuvataṉi īṇṭuk kēṭu ōtiyavataṉāl kariyavi, ceyyavi, neṭiyavi, kuṟiyavi eṉap paṇpukoḷ peyarāy varum aikāra vīṟṟiṉ vakaram aiyoṭuṅ keṭṭu vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟutal taḻuvappaṭṭatu eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
(u-m) kariyavaṟṟi, ceyyavaṟṟi, neṭiyavaṟṟi, kuṟiyavaṟṟi eṉavarum.
		nīyeṉ orupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum
āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākummē.			(tol. 179)
iḵtu īkāra vīṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nīyeṉṉum orupeyar taṉmēl neṭiyatākiya īkāraṅ kuṟuki ikaramām, avviṭattu ṉakaram oṟṟāy vantu niṟkum.
nī yeṉpatē peyar; niṉ eṉpatu ataṉ tiripeṉpār, `nīyeṉa oru peyar eṉṟār. nī eṉpataṉkaṇ nakara mey mutalāyiṉum ammeyyiṉmēl īkāram ēṟi uyir meyyeṉa ōreḻuttāy niṉṟu oṟṟumi karuti nakarattiṉmēl niṉṟa īkāratti `neṭumutal eṉṟār. peyaroṭu vēṟṟumiyurupu puṇaruṅkāl iṭiyē cāriyi peṟṟu varutalik kūṟiyavarum āciriyar, iccūttirattil ṉakaram peṟutalākiya eḻuttup pēṟṟiṉik kūṟiyatu, mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇ cāriyi peṟṟē vanta atikāratti māṟṟutaṟ poruṭ ṭeṉpar uriyāciriyar.
(u-m) niṉṉi, niṉṉoṭu, niṉakku eṉa varum.
ittiripiṉi,
		tāṉ tām nām mutal kuṟukum, yāṉ yām
nī, nīr, eṉ, em, niṉ, num ām piṟa,
kuvviṉ avvarum, nāṉkāṟu iraṭṭal.	(naṉ. 247)
eṉṟa cūttirattu neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikaḷōṭu cērttuk kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
		ōkāra viṟutik koṉṉē cāriyi			(tol. 180)
iḵtu ōkāra vīṟu iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ōkāra vīṟṟiṟku iṭivaruñcāriyi oṉcāriyi yākum.
(u-m) kōoṉi, kōoṉoṭu eṉa varum.
`oṉṟāka vantakōviṉi aṭarkka vanta eṉavum kōviṉi, kōviṉoṭu, ōviṉi, ōviṉoṭu, cōviṉi cōviṉoṭu, eṉavum ciṟupāṉmi ōkāra viṟuti iṉcāri yiyum peṟumeṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ō - matakunīr tāṅkum palaki. cō - matil.
		a ā veṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavik
kattoṭuñ civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupē.			(tol. 181)
iḵtu akara ākāra vīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) a ā eṉṟu collappaṭum īṟṟiṉiyuṭiya maratti uṇara niṉṟa peyarōṭu puṇaruṅkāl ēḻāmurupu muṉ kūṟiya iṉcāriyiyō ṭaṉṟi attuc cāriyiyōṭum poruntum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) viḷavattukkaṇ, palāvattukkaṇ eṉa varum.
`valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻuttu 144) eṉpataṉāl kaṇṇurupiṉ muṉṉar valleḻuttu mikkatu. `teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē (eḻuttu 133) eṉṟa mikiyāl attiṉ akaram akaramuṉi (125) keṭātāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		ñanaveṉ puḷḷik kiṉṉē cāriyi.			(tol. 182)
itu puḷḷiyīṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ñanaveṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟu kaṭku varuñ cāriyi iṉ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) uriñiṉi, uriñiṉoṭu, poruniṉi, poruniṉoṭu eṉavarum.
		cuṭṭumutal vakaram aiyu meyyuṅ
keṭṭa viṟuti yiyaṟṟiri piṉṟē.			(tol. 183)
itu vakaravīṟu nāṉkaṉuḷ cuṭṭu mutal vakaravīṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya av, iv, uv eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟuc col, aikāramum ataṉāl paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyum keṭṭu vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭinta cuṭṭu mutal aikāra vīṟṟiṉip pōṉṟu vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) avaṟṟi, ivaṟṟi, uvaṟṟi eṉavarum.
		vavviṟu cuṭṭiṟ kaṟṟuṟal vaḻiyē.			   (naṉ. 250)
eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
		ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.			(tol. 184)
itu tev eṉpataṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) vakaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ nāṉkaṉuḷ collātu oḻinta tev eṉṉum vakara vīṟu iṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) tevviṉi, tevviṉoṭu eṉavarum.
tev eṉpatu uriccol eṉṟum, uriccol lāyiṉum īṇṭup paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarākavittu urupoṭupuṇara viti kūṟappaṭṭateṉṟum viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar uriyāciriyar.
		maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attē cāriyi.			(tol. 185)
itu makaravīṟu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) makaramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc col muṉṉar varuñcāriyi attuc cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) maratti, marattoṭu, nukatti, nukattoṭu eṉavarum.
attē vaṟṟē (eḻuttu 133) eṉpataṉāl īṟṟu makaramum, attiṉ akaram akaramuṉiyilli (eḻuttu 125) eṉpataṉāl cāriyiyiṉ akaramum keṭṭup puṇarntaṉa.
		iṉṉiṭi varūu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.			(tol. 186)
iḵtu avvīṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) makaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ attuc cāriyi yēyaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭiyē vantu muṭiyuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) urumiṉi, urumiṉoṭu, tirumiṉi, tirumiṉoṭu eṉavarum. mār - aci.
		nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟki yākum.			(tol. 187)
itu makaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nummeṉṉum makaravīṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyum.
(u-m) nummi, nummoṭu, numakku, nummiṉ, numatu, nuṅkaṇ eṉa varum.
num eṉpatu iyaṟkiyākiya makaravīṟṟuc col leṉpatum, (eḻuttu. 325) aḵtu alvaḻiyil nīyir eṉattiriyum eṉpatu (eḻuttu 326) āciriyar karuttākaliṉ, `nummeṉṉiṟuti eṉa itaṉi iyalpīṟāka vittu ōtiṉār.
		tāmnām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum
yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉō raṉṉa
ā e ākum yāmeṉ iṟuti
āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum
ēṉi yiraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum.			(tol. 188)
itu makara īṟṟuḷ muṟkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉa vaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) tām, nām eṉṉum makaravīṟum, yām eṉṉum makaravīṟum nummeṉṉum makaravīṟu pōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitaliyuṭiya yāmeṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc collil ākāram ekaramām. avviṭattu yakaramākiya mey keṭutal vēṇṭum. tām nām eṉṉum iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukit tam nam eṉa niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) tammi, nammi, emmi eṉavarum.
		ellām eṉṉum iṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum
ummi niliyum iṟuti yāṉa.			(tol. 189)
itu makaravīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ellām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc col muṉṉar attum iṉṉum aṉṟi vaṟṟeṉcāriyi vantu muṭiyum; āṇṭu ummeṉṉum cāriyi iṟutik kaṇ nilipeṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
ellām eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc col muṉṉar attum iṉṉum aṉṟi vaṟṟeṉcāriyi vantu muṭiyum; āṇṭu ummeṉṉum cāriyi iṟutik kaṇ nilipeṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
ellām eṉpataṉ makaram vaṟṟiṉ mici oṟṟeṉṟu keṭukkappaṭum.
(u-m) ellāvaṟṟiyum, ellāvaṟṟiṉum, ellā vaṟṟuk kaṇṇum eṉavarum.
`muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāl ellāvaṟṟoṭum eṉa mūṉṟām urupiṉkaṇṇum, ellāvaṟṟukkum eṉa nāṉkām urupiṉ kaṇṇum, ellāvaṟṟatum eṉa āṟām urupiṉ kaṇṇum muṟṟukarattiṉ muṉ varum um eṉpataṉ ukarakkēṭu koḷḷap paṭum eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
		uyartiṇi yāyiṉ nammiṭi varumē.			(tol. 190)
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ellām eṉa niṉṟa makaravīṟṟu viravup peyar uyartiṇikkaṇ varumāyiṉ nammeṉṉum cāriyi iṭi vantu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) ellā nammiyum, ellā nammoṭum eṉavarum.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟavē, ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar aḵṟiṇiyāy niṉṟu urupoṭu puṇarumiṭattu muṟ kūṟiyapaṭi iṭiyē vaṟṟuccāriyipeṟum eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar aḵṟiṇik kaṇ varumāyiṉ iṭiyē vaṟṟuc cāriyiyum uyar tiṇikkaṇ varumāyiṉ iṭiyē nammuccāriyiyum peṟṟu avvīriṭattum iṟutiyil ummuccāriyi peṟum eṉpatām.
nammuc cāriyi peṟumiṭattu ellām eṉṉum colliṉ īṟṟu makaram, mēṟ cūttirattuc colliya vaṟṟiṉ mici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭutta atikārattāṟ keṭukkappaṭu meṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		ellām eṉpatu iḻitiṇiyāyiṉ
aṟṟo ṭurupiṉ mēlum muṟumē
aṉṟēl nammiṭi yaṭintaṟ ṟākum.			   (naṉ. 245)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl ipporuḷi naṉṉūlār mēṟ koṇṭu kūṟiyuḷḷār. āciriyar vaṟṟuccāriyiyeṉak kūṟiya taṉi naṉṉūlār ataṉ vakaramey cilaviṭattuk keṭṭuvarutal koṇṭu aṟṟuccāriyi yākattirittuk kūṟiṉami kāṇka.
		ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyum
ellīru meṉṉum muṉṉili yiṟutiyum
oṟṟum ukaramum keṭumeṉa moḻipa
niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi
ummi niliyum iṟuti yāṉa
tammiṭi varūum paṭarkki mēṉa
nummiṭi varūum muṉṉili moḻikkē.		(tol. 191)
itu makaravīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ellārum eṉṉum makaravīṟṟu uyartiṇip paṭarkkip peyarum, ellīrum eṉṉum makaravīṟṟu uyartiṇi muṉṉilip peyarum makaravoṟṟum ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ukaramuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar. avvukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi ataṉāl ūrappaṭṭa rakara voṟṟuk keṭātu muṭital vēṇṭum. avviru moḻikkum iṟutikkaṇ ummuc cāriyi nilipeṟum. paṭarkkiyiṭattut tammuc cāriyi nilipeṟum. paṭarkkiyiṭattut tammuc cāriyi iṭivarum. muṉṉili moḻikku nummuccāriyi iṭivarum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) ellār tammiyum, ellār tammoṭum, ellīr nummiyum, ellīr nummoṭum eṉavarum.
`paṭarkki `muṉṉili eṉṟa mikutiyāl kariyē nammiyum, kariyē nammoṭum eṉa makaravīṟṟut taṉmip peyar iṭikkaṇ nammuc cāriyiyum iṟutiyil ummuc cāriyiyum peṟum eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
im makaravīṟṟu uyartiṇi muṭiviṉi,
		ellāru mellīru meṉpavaṟ ṟummi
taḷḷi niralē tamnum cārap
pullu murupiṉ piṉṉa rummē.			   (naṉ. 249)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉar naṉṉūlār.
		tāṉyāṉ eṉṉum āyī riṟutiyum
mēṉmup peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.			(tol. 192)
itu ṉakaravīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu ṉakaravīṟum mēl makaravīṟṟuṭ kūṟiya tām nām yām eṉṉum mūṉṟu peyaroṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tāṉ eṉpatu `taṉ eṉak kuṟukiyum, yāṉ eṉpataṉ kaṇ ākāram ekaramākiya yakaravoṟṟuk keṭṭu `eṉ eṉat tirintum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) taṉṉi, eṉṉi eṉavarum.
iṅṅaṉam tām, tāṉ nām mutal kuṟukutaliyum, yām, yāṉ eṉpatil yakara mey keṭṭu ākāram ekaramāyt tiritaliyum, nī neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakara voṟṟup peṟṟu niṉ eṉa varutaliyum,
		tāṉ tām nām mutal kuṟukum yāṉ yām
nī nīr eṉ em niṉ numām piṟa
kuvviṉ avvarum nāṉkāṟu iraṭṭal.			   (naṉ. 247)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl pavaṇanti muṉivar tokuttuk kūṟiṉami muṉṉarum viḷakkappaṭṭatu.
		aḻaṉē puḻaṉē āyiru moḻikkum
attum iṉṉum uṟaḻat tōṉṟal
otta teṉpa uṇaru mōrē.			(tol. 193)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ ākiya avviru moḻikkum attuc cāriyiyum iṉ cāriyiyum māṟivarattōṉṟutalip poruntiṟṟeṉpar aṟivōr eṉṟavāṟu.
aḻaṉ+attu+ai = aḻatti;	puḻaṉ+attu+ai = puḻatti
	iṉ+ai=aḻaṉiṉi;	iṉ+ai=puḻaṉiṉi
eṉavarum.
aḻaṉ - piṇam. puḻaṉ eṉpataṉ poruḷ naṉku viḷaṅka villi.
attuc cāriyiyoṭu puṇarumiṭattu nilimoḻi yīṟṟu ṉakaram `attēvaṟṟē (eḻuttu 133) eṉṟa vitippaṭi attiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭṭumuṭintatu.
iccūttirattut `tōṉṟal eṉṟataṉāl evaṉ eṉavarum ṉakaravīṟṟu viṉāccolli evaṉ eṉṟum eṉ eṉṟum niṟutti vaṟṟuk koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceykiceytu evaṟṟi, evaṟṟoṭu eṉavum, eṟṟi, eṟṟoṭu eṉavum muṭittal poruntum eṉṟum, `ottatu eṉṟataṉāl ekiṉ eṉa varum ṉakaravīṟṟuc col urupoṭu puṇaruṅkāl attum iṉṉum peṟṟu ekiṉatti, ekiṉiṉi eṉavum, ekiṉattoṭu ekiṉi ṉoṭu eṉavum muṭiyumeṉṟum koḷvar uriyāciriyar.
		aṉṉeṉ cāriyi yēḻaṉ iṟuti
muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkit teṉpa.			(tol. 194)
itu ḻakāravīṟṟu eṇṇup peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aṉ eṉṉum cāriyi ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟumiyalpiṉi yuṭitteṉpar āciriyar.
(u-m) ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṉiṉ, ēḻaṟku eṉa varum.
iccūttirattum `ēḻaṉ iṟuti eṉa aṉ cāriyi peṟṟami kāṇka.
		kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti muṉṉar
muṟṟat tōṉṟum iṉṉeṉ cāriyi.			(tol. 195)
itu kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar iṉ ṉeṉṉuñ cāriyi muṭiyat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nākiṉi, nākiṉoṭu, varakiṉi, varakiṉoṭu eṉa varum. ēṉiyavaṟṟoṭum ivvāṟē iyitturikka.
`muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāl `vaḻakkattāṟ pāṭṭārāyntāṉ kariyataṉi eṉa muṟiyē attum aṉṉumākiya piṟacāriyi peṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
		neṭṭeḻut timpar oṟṟumikat tōṉṟum
appāl moḻika ḷalvaḻi yāṉa.			(tol. 196)
itu mēṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉar varukiṉṟa kuṟṟukaraṅ kaṭku iṉavoṟṟu mikattōṉṟum; aṅṅaṉam oṟṟumikat tōṉṟāta kacatappakkaḷ īṟākiya moḻikaḷ allātaviṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
 eṉavē ṭakāra ṟakāraṅkaḷi yūrnta kuṟṟukara moḻikaḷē oṟṟu iraṭṭippaṉa eṉak koḷka.
ivvāciriyar otta voṟṟi iṉavoṟṟeṉṟum otta voṟṟu iraṭṭutali iṉavoṟṟu mikutaleṉṟum kūṟiṉami kāṇka.
(u-m) yāṭṭi, yāṭṭoṭu, yāṟṟi, yāṟṟoṭu eṉavarum.
nāku, kācu, pōtu, kāpu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa appāl moḻikaḷ eṉavum, avi iṉavoṟṟu mikāvāyiṉa eṉavum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		avitām
iyaṟkiya vākuñ ceyaṟkiya veṉpa			(tol. 197)
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiyatu.
(i-ḷ) aṅṅaṉam iṉa voṟṟu mikuvaṉatām iṉ cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyuñ ceyaṟkiyiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
muṉ cūttirattiṟ kāṭṭiyaṉa cāriyi peṟāmi kāṇka.
`ceyaṟkiya eṉṟataṉāl iṉavoṟṟu mikkaṉa yāṭṭiṉi, yāṭṭiṉoṭu, eṉac ciṟupāṉmi iṉ peṟutalum koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
`ceyaṟkiya eṉṟa mikiyāṉē neṭiṟ ṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkē yaṉṟi uyirt toṭark kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟkum muyiṟṟē, muyiṟṟōṭu eṉa ivviti koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum.			(tol. 198)
itu kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyar muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉatu kuṟṟukara vīṟu aṉ cāriyiyoṭu poruntum.
(u-m) oṉṟaṉi, oṉṟaṉoṭu, iraṇṭaṉi, iraṇṭa ṉoṭu, eṉa avvīṟṟu ellā eṇṇum ellā urupoṭum kūṭum.
		oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrntu varūum
ellā veṇṇuñ colluṅ kāli
āṉiṭi variṉum māṉa milli
aḵteṉ kiḷavi yāvayiṟ keṭumē
uytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.			(tol. 199)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭīṟāka niṉṟa eṇkaḷiṉ mēlē patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar ēṟi varukiṉṟa orupatu mutalāṉa ellā eṇkaḷiyum muṭipu colluṅ kālattu muṟkūṟiya aṉcāriyi yaṉṟi āṉ cāriyi iṭiyē variṉum kuṟṟamilli. āṉ peṟṟuḻip paḵtu eṉṉum eṇṇiṭattu aḵtu eṉṉum col keṭṭuppōm. av akarattiṉ ūrap paṭṭa mey keṭātu niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) oru paḵtu irupaḵtu mutalākak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ vitittavāṟē niṉṟu aḵteṉpatu keṭṭup pakara voṟṟuk keṭātu niṉṟu āṉcāriyi peṟṟu oru paḵtu + ai = orupāṉi. iru paḵtu + ai = irupāṉi eṉa ellāvurupoṭum kūṭi niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.
āciriyar `oṉpāṉ mutaṉili (463) `oṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē (475) eṉa oṉpaḵtu eṉpataṉ kaṇ pakarattuḷ akaram pirittu aḵteṉpatu keṭuttu āṉcāriyi koṭuttu oṉpatu eṉpataṟkum ivviti uṇṭeṉpataṉi uṭampoṭu puṇarttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. eṉavē oṉpaḵtu eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar urupōṭu puṇarumiṭattum oṉpāṉi oṉpāṉoṭu eṉa āṉ cāriyi peṟumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam ivveṇṇup peyarkaḷ āṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitali,
		oṉṟu mutal eṭṭīṟām eṇṇūr
pattiṉ muṉ āṉvariṟ pavvoṟ ṟoḻiyamēl
ellā mōṭum oṉpatu miṟṟē.			   (naṉ. 249)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār naṉṉūlār.
`āṉiṭi variṉum eṉṟa etirmaṟi ummiyāl, āṉ cāriyi peṟātu orupaḵtaṉi, irupaḵtaṉi eṉa ivvāṟu varutaluṅ koḷḷappaṭum eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karut tākum.
		yāteṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya
āyta iṟutiyum aṉṉoṭu civaṇum
āytaṅ keṭutal āvayi ṉāṉa.			(tol. 200)
iḵtu eṇṇup peyarallāta kuṟṟukara īṟṟuṭ cila vaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) yātu eṉa varum kuṟṟukara vīṟum cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara vīṟum aṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum; avviṭattu āytaṅ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaram āytaṅ keṭṭu aṉcāriyi peṟum eṉpataṉi,
		cuṭṭiṉmuṉ āytam aṉvariṟ keṭumē.			    (naṉ. 251)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
		ēḻa ṉurupiṟkut ticippeyar muṉṉarc
cāriyik kiḷavi iyaṟkiyu mākum
āvayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē.			(tol. 201)
ituvum kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻām urupoṭu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ticip peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta kaṇ ṇeṉṉuru piṟku muṭipu kūṟuṅkāl muṟkūṟiya iṉ cāriyi yākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalēyaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum. aṅṅaṉam iyal pāyavaḻit ticippeyar iṟutik kuṟṟukaram taṉṉālūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭum keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) vaṭakkiṉ kaṇ, kiḻakkiṉ kaṇ, teṟkiṉkaṇ, mēṟkiṉ kaṇ, eṉavum, vaṭakkaṇ, kiḻakkaṇ, teṟkaṇ, mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum. iṉpeṟumvaḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭātu niṟṟal `kuṟṟiyalukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa (eḻuttu 15) eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyuḷ viḷakkappaṭṭatu. āṇṭuk kāṇka.
kīḻ cār, kīḻpuṭi, mēl cār, mēlpuṭi, teṉcār, teṉpuṭi, vaṭa cār, vaṭapuṭi, eṉa ivvāṟu cāriyi peṟātu muṭivaṉa vellām `āvayiṉ eṉṟataṉāl iccūttirattil taḻuvik koḷḷappaṭum.
		puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
colliya alla ēṉiya vellām
tēruṅ kāli urupoṭu civaṇic
cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappā ṭilavē.			(tol. 202)
iḵtu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ) puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum eṉa muṭipu colliyavi yallāta oḻintavi yellām ārāyuṅ kālattu urupukaḷōṭu porunti cāriyi niṉṟu muṭiyum niyatiyi yuṭiyavalla eṉṟavāṟu.
`cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappāṭila eṉavē avi yellām cāriyi peṟṟum peṟātum varum eṉpatām.
puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ collātu oḻintaṉa ṇayaralaḷa eṉṉum aintumām. uyiruḷ ikaram collātoḻintatu.
(u-m) maṇ +iṉ+ai=maṇṇiṉi, maṇṇi, vēy + iṉ+ai=vēyiṉi; vēyi.
nār + iṉ + ai = nāriṉi; nāri, kal + iṉ + ai = kalliṉi; kalli.
muḷ + iṉ + ai = muḷḷiṉi; muḷḷi; kiḷi + iṉ + ai = kiḷiyiṉi, kiḷiyi eṉa varum.
iṉit tēruṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉē urupukaḷ nili moḻiyāka niṉṟu tam poruḷoṭu puṇarum vaḻi vēṟupaṭum urupīṟṟuc ceykiyellām īṇṭu muṭippar uriyāciriyar.
7. uyir mayaṅkiyal
uyirīṟu niṉṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇattōṭum mayaṅkip puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ uyir mayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mayaṅkutal - kalattal. uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya ellāviṭattum iyalpāmeṉavum, orōvaḻi aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar iyalpāvaṉavuḷaveṉavum  toki marapil vitantu kūṟiya āciriyar, aviyoḻinta uyirīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiyum, ēṉi viṉimuṟṟu viṉiyeccam peyareccaṅkaḷiyum īṇṭu uṇarttuvāṉ eṭuttukkoṇṭu, akaramutal aukāramvari avaṟṟi neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyāṉ vittuṇarttukiṉṟār. ivviyalil 93- cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyil amintaṉavākum. īṟṟuvakiyāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷellām valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iyalpāvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, uyirmika varuvaṉa, nīṭa varuvaṉa, kuṟukavaruvaṉa, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa, piṟavāṟu tirivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakip pakuppukkaḷuḷ aṭaṅkuvaṉavām. iv vakiyāl otta vitiyiṉavākiya cūttiraṅkaḷit tokuttu nōkkutal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. mutaṉ mutal a, i, u eṉṉuñ cuṭṭiṉmuṉ nāṟkaṇamum puṇartal īṇṭu nōkkattakkatākum.
mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉum nāṟkaṇamum puṇartal.
a, i, u eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi vanta valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyumeṉpataṉi muṟiyē iv vatikārattu 204, 235, 255 - ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ kūṟuvar āciriyar. akarattiṉ muṉṉar ña na ma veṉṉum melleḻuttukkaḷi mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷ varumiṭattu vanta melloṟṟu mikkumuṭitalum, yakaramum vakaramum uyirmutaṉ moḻikaḷum varumiṭattu vakara voṟṟu mikku muṭitalum, ceyyuḷuṭ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu muṭitalum uḷavām. ivvitikaḷi,
		cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ
oṭṭiya voṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
		yava muṉvariṉē vakara moṟṟum.
		uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
		nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.      	   (tol. 205, 206, 207, 208)
eṉa varuñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar uṇarttiyuḷḷār. akarac cuṭṭiṟkukkūṟiya ivvitikaḷi,
		cuṭṭiṉiyaṟki muṟkiḷan taṟṟē.			(tol. 238)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl ikarac cuṭṭiṟkum,
		ēṉavi variṉē mēṉili yiyala.			(tol. 256)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl ukarac cuṭṭiṟkum māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār.
eṉa mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum uyirum yakaramum variṉ vakara voṟṟum ka, ca, ta, pa, ña, na, ma, va eṉpaṉa variṉ vanta veḻuttukkaḷum mikumeṉpatām.
mēṟkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ivvitikaḷi,
		ekara viṉāmuc cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar
uyirum yakaramu meytiṉ vavvum
piṟavariṉ aviyun tūkkiṟ cuṭṭu
nīḷiṉ yakaramun tōṉṟutal neṟiyē.			   (naṉ. 163)
eṉa varum oru cūttirattāl tokutturittār naṉṉūlār.
1. valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa
		akara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ
tatta motta voṟṟiṭi mikumē.			(tol. 203)
iccūttiram akaravīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
(i-ḷ) akaramākiya iṟutiyiyuṭiya peyarc colliṉ muṉ vēṟṟumiyallāta viṭattuk ka ca ta pa mutalmoḻikaḷ varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟumāyiṉ tattamakkup poruntiṉa ak ka ca ta pa kkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) viḷakkuṟitu, nuṇakkuṟitu, atakkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu peritu eṉa varum.
ka ca ta pa mikutalākiya ivvitiyiṉi,
		vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ākāra viṟuti akara viyaṟṟē.
vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
īkāra viṟuti ākāra viyaṟṟē.
vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ukara viṟuti akara viyaṟṟē.
vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ūkāra viṟuti ākāra viyaṟṟē.
vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ēkāra viṟuti ūkāra viyaṟṟē.
vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ōkāra viṟuti ēkāra viyaṟṟē.
vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē.
ōkaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
                                  			   (tol. 206, 221, 225, 249, 252, 254, 259,
                                                   264, 266, 274, 276, 289, 292,)
eṉa varum cūttiraṅkaḷāl muṟiyē akara vīṟṟu vēṟṟumip peyarkkaṇṇum, ā ī u ū ō īṟṟu alvaḻi vēṟṟumip peyark kaṇṇum māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār.
mēl, toki marapiṉuḷ ikara aikāra vīṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu varumiṭattu mikātu iyalpātalum, mikutalum uṟaḻtalumākiya mūvaki niliyaveṉṟu kūṟiṉār. īṇṭu alvaḻiyi vilakki, ikara aikāramākiya avviru vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikap peṟu meṉpataṉi,
		ikara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
		aikāra viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.	       (tol. 235, 280)
eṉa varum iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum uṇarttiṉār. aukāra vīṟṟup peyar alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mikuta lōṭu avviru vaḻiyum ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyumeṉpataṉi,
	295.	aukāra viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟē
avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal
cevviteṉpa ciṟantaci ṉōrē.
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār. eṉavē ikara aikāram nīṅkalāka ēṉiya uyirīṟṟup peyarellām alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mikap peṟu meṉpatum ikara aikāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikappeṟu meṉpatum kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. `ekara okaram peyarkkīṟākā eṉap piṉṉark kūṟuvārākaliṉ avvīṟukaḷukku ivviti amiyā teṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.

alvaḻiyil valleḻuttu mikum uyirīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ:
		

viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum
eṉave ṉeccamum cuṭṭi ṉiṟutiyum
āṅka veṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum
ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.			(tol. 204)
eṉa akara vīṟṟilum,
		ceyyā veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
avviyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 222)
eṉa ākāra vīṟṟilum,
		iṉiyaṇiyeṉṉum kāliyu miṭaṉum
viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭu maṉṉa.			(tol. 236)
		patakkumuṉ variṉē tūṇik kiḷavi
mutaṟkiḷan teṭutta vēṟṟumi yiyaṟṟē.			(tol. 239)
eṉa ikara vīṟṟilum,
		iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum
uṭaṉili moḻipu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.			(tol. 231)
eṉa īkāra vīṟṟilum,
		cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.			(tol. 255)
eṉa ukara vīṟṟiṉum,
		viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikku muṉṉili moḻikkum
niṉiyuṅ kāli yavvaki variyār.			(tol. 265)
eṉa ūkāra vīṟṟiṉum alvaḻiyil valleḻuttumikum coṟkaḷi āciriyar vitantu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ic cūttiraṅkaḷāl akara vīṟṟuḷ, viṉiyeccamum avvīṟṟu uvamac collum `eṉa veṉṉum vāypāṭṭu eccamum akarac cuṭṭum āṅkaveṉṉum uriyacic collum, ākāra vīṟṟuḷ, ceyyāveṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉi yeccamum, ikaravīṟṟuḷ, iṉi aṇi eṉṟa iru coṟkaḷum, ikaravīṟṟu viṉiyeccamum, ikarac cuṭṭum, patakkeṉṉum varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum tūṇi yeṉṉum collum, īkāravīṟṟuḷ mēliṭatti yuṇarttum mīyeṉṉum collum, ukara vīṟṟuḷ ukarac cuṭṭum, ūkāra vīṟṟuḷ viṉiyeccamum, muṉṉili moḻiyum, alvaḻikkaṇ ka ca ta pa mutal moḻi varumiṭattu vanta valleḻuttukkaḷ mikap peṟum eṉṟa orēviti kūṟappaṭṭami kāṇka.
(u-m) akkoṟṟaṉ, ikkoṟṟaṉ, ukkoṟṟaṉ eṉa mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉum varumoḻi valleḻuttukkaḷ mikkaṉa.
uṇak koṇṭāṉ, uṇṇāk koṇṭāṉ, tēṭik koṇṭāṉ, uppiṉṟip puṟki yuṇṭāṉ, c©û¡ koṇṭāṉ eṉa a ā i ū eṉṉum nāṉku īṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṉ muṉṉum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
pulipōlap pāyntāṉ, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ, āṅkak kuyilum kāṭṭi, eṉa muṟiyē, uvamac collum, eṉaveṉ eccamum, āṅkaveṉṉum uriyaciyu mākiya akara vīṟṟiṭic colmuṉṉar valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
iṉik koṇṭāṉ, aṇikkoṇṭāṉ, tūṇippatakku eṉa ikara vīṟṟuḷ eṭuttōtiyaṉavaṟṟuḷ valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
mīkkōḷ, mīppōrvi eṉpaṉa valleḻuttu mikku uṭaṉ niṟkum moḻikaḷām. uṇṇukkoṟṟā, uṇṇuc cāttā eṉa ūkāra vīṟṟu muṉṉilikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam uyirīṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaliyuṇarnta naṉṉūlār, ivviti kaḷellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttu,
		iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉum niṉṟa vuyirmuṉ
kacatapa mikum vitavātaṉa maṉṉē.			   (naṉ. 165)
eṉa orē cūttirattiṟ potuvitiyāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉār.
iṉi alvaḻiyil akaravīṟṟu īreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya pala, cila eṉpaṉa tammuḷ tām varumiṭattu valleḻuttup peṟṟum iyalpāyum varuvaṉa. ivvuṟaḻcci muṭipiṉi,
		palleḻut tiyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟum.			(tol. 215)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār.
(u-m) pala pala, cila cila eṉavum palappala, cilac cila eṉavum varum. pala, cila eṉṉum ivviru collum tammuṉ tām varumāyiṉ iyalpākalum, mikutalum nili moḻiyīṟṟu akaram keṭa lakaram ṟakaramātalum, ivaṟṟiṉmuṉ piṟa moḻikaḷuḷ yātāṉu moṉṟu variṉ akaram niṟṟalum nīṅkalum ākiya iv vēṟupāṭukaḷ vaḻakkilum ceyyuḷilum perukiṉa. ivaṟṟi naṉṉūlār.
		pala cila veṉumivi tammuṉ tām variṉ
iyalpu mikalum akaram ēka
lakaram ṟakaramākalum piṟa variṉ
akaram vikaṟpa mākalu muḷapiṟa.			   (naṉ. 170)
eṉṟa cūttirattil tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
2. iyalpāvaṉa
alvaḻiyil valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpākum uyirīṟukaḷi yuṇarttap pōnta tolkāppiyaṉār.
		aṉṉa veṉṉum uvamak kiḷaviyum
aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum
ceymmaṉ veṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
ceyta veṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
amma veṉṉum uripporuṭ kiḷaviyum
palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi yuḷappaṭa
aṉṟi yaṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.			(tol. 210)
eṉavarum cūttirattāl akaravīṟṟuḷ iyalpāvaṉa vaṟṟi uṇarttiṉār.
(i-ḷ) aṉṉa veṉṟu collappaṭum uvama vurupākiya iṭic collum, aṇmiyiluḷḷāri aḻikkum viḷiyākiya nilimiyiyuṭiya akaravīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarccollum, ceymmaṉ eṉṉum viṉic collum, ēvalik karutiya viyaṅ kōḷākiya akaravīṟṟu viṉiccollum, ceyta veṉṉum peyareccamākiya viṉic collum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉi yeccamākiya viṉic collum, uriyacip poruṇmi yiṉiyuṭiya ammaveṉṉum iṭic col lum, paṉmip poruḷil varum akaravīṟṟup palavaṟi collu mākiya avvaṉittum (varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikātu) iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉpatām.
(u-m) poṉṉaṉṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi,
	ūra kēḷ, cel, tā, pō.
	uṇmaṉ kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
	celka kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
	uṇṭa	kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
(etirmaṟi)	uṇṇāta kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
(kuṟippu)	nalla kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
	uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ,
	(pōyiṉāṉ.)
	amma koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā pūtā.
	pala kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
eṉa varum. palavaṟṟiṟuti eṉpataṉāl uṇṭaṉa kutiri; kariyaṉa kutiri eṉavarum akaravīṟṟu viṉi muṟṟiyum viṉik kuṟippiyum taḻuviṉār. akara vīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipākiya itaṉi,
		ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉiyeccam palvakip
peyari ṉeccamuṟ ṟāṟaṉurupē
aḵṟiṇip paṉmi ammuṉ ṉiyalpē.			   (naṉ. 167)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		āvu māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum
yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum
ēval kuṟitta vuriyaci miyāvum
taṉṟoḻi lurikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō
ṭaṉṟi yaṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.			(tol. 224)
eṉavarum cūttirattāl ākāra vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ iyalpāmāṟu kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār.
(i-ḷ) ā veṉṉum peyarc collum, mā veṉṉum peyarc collum viḷittaliyuṭiya peyarākiya uyar tiṇic collum, yā veṉṉum viṉāppeyarum, aḵṟiṇip paṉmip poruḷi yuṇarttum ākāra vīṟṟu muṟṟu viṉic collum, muṉṉiliyil ēval viṉic collic cārntuvarum uriyaci miyāvākiya ākāravīṟṟuc collum, taṉatu toḻiliṉic collum, ākāravīṟṟu viṉāviṉi yuṭiya viṉic collum ākiya avvaṉittum (varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikātu) iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉpatām.
(u-m) ā kuṟitu ciṟitu, tītu peritu.
	mā kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu.
	ūrā koḷ, cel, tā, pō.
	yā kuṟiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya.
	uṇaṇā kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi.
	kēṇmiyā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā.
	uṇkā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā.
eṉavarum. ākāra vīṟṟiyalpākiya iv valvaḻi muṭipiṉi,
		alvaḻi āmā miyā muṉ mikā.			    (naṉ. 171)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
uṇṇāk kutiri, uṇṇāk kiṭantaṉa eṉa mikumēl uṇṇāta kutiri yeṉavum uṇṭu kiṭantaṉa eṉavum poruḷ paṭṭu, muṟiyē etirmaṟip peyareccamum. ceyyāveṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamum ām eṉpar caṅkara namaccivāyar.
		nīyeṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum
mīyeṉa marīiya iṭam vari kiḷaviyum
āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.			(tol. 250)
itaṉāl īkāra vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ iyalpāmiṭam kūṟukiṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
(i-ḷ) nī yeṉṉum peyarum, iṭakkarp peyarākiya pakara vīkāramum, mī eṉṟu marūuvāy ōriṭatti varintuṇarntuñ collum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)	nī kuṟiyi, ciṟiyi, tīyi, periyi.
	pī kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu.
	mī kaṇ, cevi, tali, puṟam.
eṉavaruma. ivvīkāra vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ alvaḻik kaṇ iyalpāy varumeṉpataṉi naṉṉūlār,
		pavvī nīmī muṉṉa ralvaḻi
iyalpām, valimeli mikalumā mīkkē.			   (naṉ. 178)
eṉpataṉkaṇ iyalpām eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.
		cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yiyalpā kummē.			(tol. 257)
itaṉāl ukara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara vīṟṟup peyar muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉpatām.
(u-m) atu kuṟitu, itu kuṟitu, utu kuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.
itaṉōṭu mūṉṟāmurupākiya oṭu, āṟāmurupākiya atu, oru, iru, aṟu, ēḻu eṉattirinta ukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, ukara vīṟṟuviṉit toki ākiyavikaḷiyuṅ kūṭṭi,
		mūṉṟā ṟurupeṇ viṉittoki cuṭṭī
ṟāku mukaram muṉṉa riyalpām.			   (naṉ. 179)
eṉac cūttiram ceytār naṉṉūlār.
(u-m) cāttaṉoṭu koṇṭāṉ, cāttaṉatu ki, oru kalam, iru kalam; aṟu kalam, ēḻu kalam, aṭu kaḷiṟu eṉavarum.
		tēṟṟa vekaramuñ ciṟappiṉ ovvum
mēṟkūṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā.			(tol. 273)
(i-ḷ) tēṟṟap poruṇmiyil varum ēkaramum ciṟappup poruṇmiyil varum okaramum valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyātu iyalpākum.
(u-m) yāṉēe koṇṭēṉ, nīyē e koṇṭāy
	yāṉōō koṭiyaṉ, nīyōō koṭiyi
eṉa iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. `yāṉē koṇṭēṉ eṉpuḻi aḷapeṭi ekaram tēṟṟam. `nīyōō koṭiyi eṉpuḻi aḷapeṭi okaram ciṟappup poruḷit tantu niṉṟatu.
ivvāṟu teḷivup poruṇmiyum ciṟappup poruṇmi yum allāta viṭattu ekaramum okaramum peyarc coṟku īṟākāveṉpatum, ivi viṉic colluḷ muṉṉili yiṭattaṉavāy varumeṉpatum,
		ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā
muṉṉili moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar
tēṟṟamuñ ciṟappum alvaḻiyāṉa.			(tol. 272)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. 273-m cūttirattu `mēṟkūṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā eṉa āciriyar kūṟutalāl, 272-m cūttira mākiya itaṉkaṇ kūṟappaṭṭa muṉṉili viṉiyīṟṟiṉkaṇ varum ekara okaraṅkaḷ.
ēek koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā.
ōokkoṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā.
eṉa valleḻuttu mikappeṟum eṉṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu.
		māṟukoḷ eccamum viṉāvu meṇṇum
kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.			(tol. 275)
itaṉāl ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭic coṟkaḷ iyalpāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) māṟupāṭu kōṭaliyuṭiya eccap poruṇ mikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟiṭiccollum, viṉāp poruṇ mikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟiṭic collum, eṇṇup poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟiṭic collum mēṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉpatām.
māṟu koḷeccamāvatu etirmaṟi yeccamām.
(u-m) yāṉē koṇṭēṉ, ceṉṟēṉ, tantēṉ, pōyiṉēṉ eṉa varum. yāṉē koṇṭēṉ eṉpuḻi ēkāram yāṉ koṇṭilēṉ eṉa māṟu koṇṭa oḻipupaṭa niṉṟatu. nīyē koṇṭāy, ceṉṟāy, tantāy, pōyiṉāp eṉpuḻi ēkāram viṉā. koṟṟaṉē cāttaṉē tēvaṉē pūtaṉē eṉpuḻi ēkāram eṇṇup poruḷit tantu niṟṟal kāṇka.
		māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvum aiyamum
kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.		            (tol. 290)
itaṉāl ōkāra vīṟṟu iṭiccol muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) māṟupāṭṭiṉik koṇṭa eccam poruṇmi yiṉiyuṭiya ōkāramum, aiyapporuṇmiyiṉiyuṭiya ōkāramum muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) yāṉō koṇṭēṉ (māṟukoḷeccam)
	nīyō koṇṭāy (viṉā)
	pattō patiṉoṉṟō (aiyam)
eṉavarum.
	 	oḻintata ṉiliyu moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.		(tol. 291)
itaṉāl oḻiyici ōkāramum mēṟkūṟiya ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyal piṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikātu muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
(u-m) koḷalō koṇṭāṉ - eṉavarum.
ittoṭar, `koṇṭuyyap pōmāṟaṟintilaṉ eṉa, oḻintu niṉṟa coṟporuṇmi yuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, iṅku vanta ōkāram oḻiyicip poruḷil vantatākum.
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷākiya iṭic coṟkaḷ valleḻuttu mikātiyalpāmeṉpatu,
		iṭic col ē ō muṉvari ṉiyalpē.			    (naṉ. 201)
eṉa naṉṉūṟ cūttirattuṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
3. melleḻuttu mikutal
vēṟṟumiyil  valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy varumiṭattu melleḻuttu mikappeṟum uyirīṟukaḷik kūṟavanta tolkāppiyar,
		marappeyarkkiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.		(tol. 217)
eṉa akaravīṟṟilum,
		yā marak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
āmup peyarum melleḻuttu mikumē.
		māmarak kiḷaviyum āvu māvum
āmup peyarum avaṟṟōraṉṉa
akaram valleḻut taviyava ṇiliyā
ṉakaram oṟṟum āvum māvum.		       (tol. 229, 231)
eṉa ākāra vīṟṟilum,
		utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.	(tol. 243)
eṉa ikara vīṟṟilum.
		oṭumarak kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē.			(tol. 262)
eṉa ukara vīṟṟilum,
		cēveṉ marappeyar oṭumara viyaṟṟē.			(tol. 278)
eṉa ēkāra vīṟṟilum,
		vici marakkiḷaviyu ñemiyu namiyum
āmup peyarum cēmara viyala.			(tol. 282)
eṉa aikāra vīṟṟilum varum cūttiraṅkaḷāl akara vīṟṟu marap peyarum, ā, i, u, ē, ai, ākiyavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟil eṭuttōtiya yā, piṭā, taḷā, mā, uti, oṭu, cē, vici, ñemi, nami eṉṉum marap peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa eṉak kuṟittuḷḷār.
(u-m)	viḷaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	yā aṅ kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	piṭāaṅ kōṭucetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	taḷāaṅ kōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	māaṅkōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	utiṅ kōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	oṭuṅ kōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	cēṅ kōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	viciṅ kōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	ñemiṅ kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
	namiṅ kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
eṉavarum. iṅṅaṉam vēṟṟumikkaṇ uyirīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ valleḻuttu varumiṭattu melleḻuttu mikku muṭivataṉi,
		marappeyar muṉṉa riṉamel leḻuttu
varap peṟuṉavumuḷa vēṟṟumi vaḻiyē.			   (naṉ. 166)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār. valleḻuttu varuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikkum valleḻuttu mikkum uṟaḻvaṉavaṟṟi uṇarttakkarutiya tolkāppiyar,
		yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
āmup peyarum melleḻuttu mikumē.			   (naṉ. 166)
		valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.		      (tol. 229, 230)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl ākāra vīṟṟup peyarākiya yā, piṭā, taḷā eṉpaṉa melleḻuttōṭu valleḻuttup peṟṟu uṟaḻum eṉṟār.
(u-m)	yāaṅkōṭu, yā akkōṭu
	piṭāaṅkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu
	taḷāaṅkōṭu, taḷāakkōṭu
eṉa melleḻuttum valleḻuttum peṟṟu vantaṉa.
		ēṉip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
		valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.
olvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.		      (tol. 245, 246)
eṉavarum iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷāl ikaravīṟṟuḷ puḷiyeṉṉum cuviyiyuṇarttum peyarvalleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varu miṭattu melleḻuttum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyu meṉṟār.
(u-m)	puḷiṅkūḻ, cāṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉavum
	puḷikkūḻ, cāṟu, tayir, pāḷitam eṉavum varum.
olvaḻi yaṟital eṉṟataṉāl valleḻuttu mikutal puḷiccāṟu pōla ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku vaḻakkup payiṟci illi yeṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
iṅṅaṉam cuvippuḷimuṉ valleḻuttōṭu mel leḻuttum mikku muṭitali,
		cuvippuḷi muṉṉiṉa meṉmiyun tōṉṟum.		(tol. 175)
eṉpataṉ ummiyāṟ ṟaḻīiṉār naṉṉūlār, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iccoṟku melleḻuttu mikutali mutaṉmiyākak kūṟi, valleḻuttu mikutalit taḻuviyatu pōlaṉṟi, naṉṉūlār valleḻuttu mikutali mutaṉmiyākak koṇṭu melleḻuttu mikutalic ciṟupāṉmiyākak kūṟiyatu piṟkāla vaḻakku nōkki yeṉpatu uṇarattakkatu.
ūkāravīṟṟuḷ `pū eṉṉum oru peyar ukaram peṟuta lākiya avviyalpiṉip peṟātu melleḻuttēṉum valleḻuttēṉum mikku muṭiyum. itaṉi,
		pūve ṉorupeya rāyiyal piṉṟē
āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.		(tol. 298)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉar tolkāppiyar.
(u-m)	pūṅkoṭi, cōli, tāmam, pantu
	pūṅkoṭi, ceyki, tāmam, pantu
eṉavarum. ivvitiyi,
		pūppeyar muṉṉiṉa meṉmiyun tōṉṟum.		   (naṉ. 200)
eṉṟa cūttirattil pavaṇanti muṉivar  kuṟittuḷḷār. `val leḻuttu mikutalum eṉṟa ummiyāl melleḻuttu mikutalit tolkāppiyaṉārum `meṉmiyun tōṉṟum eṉṟa ummiyāl vaṉmi mikutali naṉṉūlārun taḻuviṉami kāṇka.
4. uyirmika varutal.
uyirīṟṟiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉ vaḻi varumiṭattu uyirmika varutalik kūṟavanta tolkāppiyar,
		ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi
meymmiyāka akaram mikumē.			(tol. 223)
eṉpataṉāl ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ akaram mikumāṟu kūṟiṉār.
(i-ḷ) ummi tokkuniṉṟa irupeyarākiya tokic coṟkaḷ meyyāka nilimoḻi īṟṟil akaram mikku muṭiyum eṉpatām.
(u-m) uvāap patiṉāṉku, irāappakal eṉavarum.
`meymmiyāka eṉpataṉāṉ `valleḻuttuk koṭukka eṉavum, ummi tokka eṉṉātu ummi eñciya eṉṟataṉāl arāappāmpu eṉap paṇput tokikkum, irāakkoṭitu eṉa eḻuvāy muṭipiṟkum irāakkākki eṉap peyarecca maṟikkum akarappēṟu koḷka eṉavum, varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyāl iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum akarap pēṟu koḷka eṉavum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
(u-m) iṟāa vaḻutuṇaṅkāy eṉavarum.
		kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
aṟiyattōṉṟum akarakkiḷavi.			(tol. 226)
eṉpataṉāl vēṟṟumikkaṇ ākāravīṟṟup peyar akara mikumāṟu kūṟukiṟār.
(i-ḷ) kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ākāravīṟṟiṟkum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya ākāravīṟṟiṟkum akaramākiya eḻuttu aṟiyat tōṉṟum eṉpatām.
(u-m) palāakkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavum kāakkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam, eṉavum varum. itaṉāṟ kūṟiya akarappēṟu irā eṉak kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa ākāra vīṟṟiṟku iṉṟeṉpataṉi,
		irāveṉ kiḷavikku akaramilli.			(tol. 227)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ ivvakarappēṟu irā eṉpataṉi yoḻittu oḻintavaṟṟiṟkēyuriyatākum.
ivvāṟē vēṟṟumikkaṇ kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa ūkāra vīṟṟu moḻiyum, ōreḻuttorumoḻi yākiya ūkāra vīṟṟu moḻiyum, ukaramākiya eḻuttup peṟum eṉpataṉi,
		kuṟṟeḻut timparu mōreḻuttu moḻikkum
niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukarak kiḷavi.			(tol. 267)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
(u-m) uṭūukkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉavum, tūukkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum. aukāra vīṟṟuppeyar alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi ukaram peṟumeṉpataṉi,
		aukāra viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟē
avviru vīṟṟu mukaram varutal
cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.			(tol. 295)
eṉṟa cūttirattitāṟ kūṟiṉār.
(u-m) kuvukkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, eṉavum, kuvukkaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉavum varum.
ēkāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaramikku muṭiyu meṉpataṉi,
		ēye ṉiṟutik kekaram varumē.			(tol. 277)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār,
(u-m) ēekkoṭṭil, cāli, tuḷi, puḻi eṉavarum.
ōkāra vīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ okaram peṟu meṉpataṉi,
		vēṟṟumik kaṇṇu mataṉō raṟṟē
okaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.				(tol. 282)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār.
(u-m) kōokkaṭumi, ciṟumi, tīmi, perumi eṉa varum.
		illoṭu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟki yākum.			(tol. 293)
eṉpataṉāl okarap pēṟiṉṟik kōyil eṉa iyalpāy vantami yuṇarka.
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya uyirmikavarum puṇarcci piṟkālattu vaḻakkoḻintamiyāl ip puṇarcci muṟiyiṉi naṉṉūlār iṟantatu vilakkal eṉṉum uttiyāṟ kūṟātoḻintār.
5. nīṭavarutal
iṉi, kuṟil neṭilāka nīḷutalākiya tiripiṉip peṟṟup puṇarvaṉavaṟṟi mutal nīḷal, iṟuti nīḷal eṉa iruvakiyākap pirikkalām. avaṟṟuḷ mutal nīṇṭu puṇarvatu ceyyuḷiṭattu varum akarac cuṭṭākum itaṉi,
		nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.			(tol. 208)
eṉpataṉāl uṇaralām. iṟuti nīṇṭu puṇarvaṉa urip poruṭkaṇ varum amma eṉṉum iṭic collum, ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻikkaṇ varum akaravīṟṟup paṉmip poruḷuṇarttum pala cila eṉpaṉavum, ukaravīṟṟil ḻakara meyyi yūrnta ukara viṟutiyum ām. ivaṟṟi,
		uripporuṭ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār.
		palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa
		ḻakara vukara nīṭiṭa ṉuṭittē
ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.			  (tol. 212, 213, 261)
eṉa varum cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
(u-m) `āyirutiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē eṉavum, ammā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum, `palā añcilā ameṉmaṉār pulavar eṉavum, paḻūup pallaṉṉa paruvukirp pāvaṭi eṉavum varum.
6. kuṟukavaruvaṉa
āṉ eṉpataṉ muṉṉar varumoḻiyāka varum pakara vīkāram taṉṉoṟṟu mikat tōṉṟik kuṟukum itaṉi,
		āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakāram
tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalu murittē.		           (tol. 233)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār āciriyar.
(u-m) 
		āppi nīroṭu alakukik koṇṭilar
(tirukkuṟuntoki) eṉavarum ikkuṟukkattiṉi,
		āmuṉ pakaravī yaṉittumvarak kuṟukum
mēlaṉa alvaḻi yiyalpā kummē.			   (naṉ. 177)
eṉṟa cūttittāṟ kuṟittār naṉṉūlār.
kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa ākāra īṟṟuc coṟkaḷil ākārattiṉatu ciṉiyākiya oru māttiri keṭṭuk kuṟukiniṟka āṇṭu ukaram varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu uriyatākum. itaṉi,
		kuṟiyata ṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭa vukaram
aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.		           (tol. 234)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār.
(u-m) 
		iṟavup puṟattaṉṉa piṇarpaṭu taṭavumutal
eṉavarum. itaṉi naṉṉūlār,
		kuṟiyataṉ kīḻāk kuṟukalu mataṉō
ṭukara mēṟṟalu miyalpumān tūkkiṉ			(tol. 172)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
7. cāriyi peṟuvaṉa
muṉ urupiyalil urupukku vēṇṭum cāriyip pēṟu kūṟiya āciriyar, īṇṭu uyirīṟṟup poruṭ puṇarcciyuḷ taṉittaṉiyē cila coṟkaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku oṉṟum palavumākac cāriyikaḷi vitittum urupiyali leṭuttōtiya cilavaṟṟiṟku avviyal vitiyi māṭṭeṟin tum viti kūṟukiṟār.
		makappeyark kiḷavik kiṉṉē cāriyi.
		attavaṇ variṉum varinili yiṉṟē.		       (tol. 218, 219)
eṉavarum iraṇṭu cūttirattāṉum maka eṉṉum akaravīṟṟup peyar poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ iṉ cāriyi peṟum eṉṟum, orōvaḻi attuc cāriyi variṉum kaṭiyappaṭāteṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
(u-m) maka + ki = makaviṉ ki, cevi, tali, puṟam, eṉavum,
matattuk ki cevi, tali, puṟam, eṉavarum.
		nilāveṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum.			(tol. 228)
eṉpataṉāl, ākāvīṟṟuḷ nilāveṉpatu attuc cāriyi peṟumeṉṟār.
 (u-m) nilā + koṇṭāṉ = nilā attuk koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum. ā eṉṉum col muṉpeṟṟu ṉakara oṟṟu akarattōṭu kūṭi niṟkum eṉpataṉi,
		āṉoṟ ṟakaramoṭu niliyiṭa ṉuṭittē.	            (tol. 232)
eṉavarum cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār āciriyar.
(u-m) `āṉaney teḷittu eṉavarum.
		paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumik
kattu miṉṉuñ cāriyi yākum.			(tol. 241)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ paṉi eṉṉum nōyaṉṟip paṉikkālatti uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumi muṭipuṭiya peyarkku attum iṉṉum cāriyiyāka varumeṉak kūṟiṉār, ivvitiyi,
		vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
avviyal niliyal cevvi teṉpa			(tol. 242)
eṉavum,
		maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal niliyum.			(tol. 287)
eṉavum varum cūttiraṅkaḷāl muṟiyē vaḷiyeṉṉum collukkum maḻiyeṉṉuñ collukkum eytuvittār.
(u-m)	paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ.
	vaḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ.
	maḻiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, maḻiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ.
	ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum.
		puḷimarak kiḷavik kammē cāriyi.
		eruvuñ ceruvu mammoṭu civaṇit
tiripiṭaṉuṭiya teriyuṅ kāli
ammiṉ makaram ceruvayiṟ keṭumē
tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻut tiyaṟki.
		paṉiyu mariyum āvirik kiḷaviyum
niṉiyuṅkāli ammoṭu civaṇum
aiye ṉiṟuti yarivarintu keṭumē
meyya vaṇoḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.		(tol. 244, 260, 283)
eṉavaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl muṟiyē puḷi eṉavum eru, ceru, eṉavum, paṉi. ari, āviri yeṉavum varum ikara ukara aikāra vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ amcāriyi peṟum eṉṟār.
(u-m)	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū
	eruvaṅkuḻi, cēṟu, tātu, pūmi
	ceruvakkaḷam cēṉi, tāṉi, paṟi

	ariyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū
	āviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavarum.
		nāṇmuṟ ṟōṉṟun toḻiṉilik kiḷavik
kāṉiṭi varutal aiya miṉṟē.			(tol. 247)
eṉpataṉāl paraṇi mutaliya ikara vīṟṟu nāṭ peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum toḻiṟcoṟku āṉcāriyi yiṭiyē vantu muṭiyumeṉavum,
		tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyi.			(tol. 248)
eṉpataṉāl tiṅkaḷi (mātatti) yuṇara niṉṟa āṭi āvaṇi mutaliya ikara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉ (toḻiṉilik kiḷavivariṉ) ikkuccāriyi vantu muṭiyu meṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār āciriyar. ivvitiyi,
		tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa.			(tol. 286)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl ātiri cittiri mutaliya aikāra vīṟṟu nāṭ peyarkkum tiṅkaṭ peyarkkum māṭṭeṟintu koṇṭār.
(u-m)
paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ
ātiriyāṟ koṇṭāṉ,ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ

eṉavarum.

		ūve ṉorupeyar āvoṭu civaṇum.
		akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalu murittē
takkavaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.		       (tol. 26, 270)
eṉpavaṟṟāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār.
(u-m)

		peṟṟamāyiṉ muṟṟaviṉ vēṇṭum.		       (tol. 271, 279)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl āṭūu, makaṭūu, eṉṟa ūkāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, peṟṟattik kuṟikkum, cē eṉṉum ēkāravīṟṟup peyarum iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyumeṉak kūṟiṉār.
(u-m) āṭūu viṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam, eṉavum, cēviṉ, kōṭu, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
āciriyar urupiyal vitiyi a, ī, u, ai, ō eṉpavaṟṟiṉīṟṟil muṟiyē, 220, 253, 243, 281, 294 ākiya cūttiraṅkaḷil māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
akaravīṟu
		palavaṟ ṟiṟuti yurupiyal niliyum.			(tol. 220)
(i-ḷ) palla, pala, cila, uḷḷa, illa eṉavarum palavaṟṟi yuṇarttum, akara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti, urupupuṇarccik kaṇ vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarntāṟpōla urupiṉatu poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyumeṉpatām.
(u-m)	palvaṟṟuk kōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū
	palavaṟṟukkōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū
	cilavaṟṟukkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū
	uḷḷavaṟṟukkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū
	illavaṟṟuk kōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū
eṉavarum.
īkāravīṟu
		nīyeṉ orupeyar urupiyal niliyum
āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkiyākum.			(tol. 253)
(i-ḷ) nī eṉṉum ōreḻut torumoḻi, urupu puṇarccik kaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakara oṟṟuppeṟṟu niṉṟāṟ pōla īṇṭup poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum muṭiyum. avvāṟu muṭivuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu mikā teṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) niṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
`iyaṟkiyākumeṉavē nilimoḻit toḻil atikāra valleḻutti vilakkātāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ukaravīṟu
		cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiyal niliyum
oṟṟiṭi mikā valleḻut tiyaṟki.			(tol.263)

		cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiyal niliyum			(tol. 281)

eṉavarum.
ōkāravīṟu
		urupiyal niliyum moḻiyumā ruḷavē
āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.			(tol. 294)
(i-ḷ) ōkāra vīṟṟuṭcila poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ urupu puṇarcciyatu iyalpilē niṉṟu oṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa. avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉpatām.
(u-m) kōoṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavarum.
ēṉiyatiripukaḷ
uyirīṟṟup poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ varum ēṉiya tiripukaḷi iṉi nōkkuvām.
akara vīṟṟuḷ cāva eṉṉum ceyaveṉ eccattu iṟutiyil niṉṟa vakara vuyirmeyyum, neṭuṅkālam vāḻka eṉṉum poruḷil varum vāḻiya eṉṉuñ colliṉ iṟutiyil niṉṟa yakara vuyirmeyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. itaṉi,
		cāva veṉṉuñ ceyaveṉṉeccat
tiṟuti vakaraṅ keṭutalu murittē.			(tol. 209)
eṉavum,
		vāḻiya veṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ kiḷavi
yiṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalu murittē.			(tol. 211)
eṉavum varum cūttiraṅkaḷāluṇarttiṉar tolkāppiyaṉār. ivviru coṟkaḷilum iṟuti yuyirmey keṭutali,
		cāvaveṉ moḻiyīṟ ṟuyirmey cātalum viti.	(naṉ. 169)
		vāḻiya veṉpata ṉīṟṟi ṉuyirmey
ēkalu murittaḵ tēkiṉu miyalpē.			   (naṉ. 168)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāluṇarttiṉār naṉṉūlār.
ikara vīṟṟuḷ, nāḻi eṉṟa aḷavup peyar muṉṉar uriyeṉpatu varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nāḻi eṉṉuñ colliṟuti yikaram, tāṉ ēṟiya ḻakara meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu avviṭattu ṭakāra oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum. itaṉi,
		urivaru kāli nāḻik kiḷavi
iṟuti yikaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
ṭakaram oṟṟum āvayi ṉāṉa.			(tol. 240)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār.

		vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉikā ṉiṟumoḻi

eṉavarum iru cūttiraṅkaḷālun tokuttuk kūṟiṉār.
paṉimuṉ aṭṭuvarum poḻutu īṟṟu aikāram keṭṭu iṭiyē ōr ākāram varum eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiyataṉi viṭuttu, aṭṭu eṉṉum varumoḻiyiṉ akaram ākāramāka nīḷumeṉak kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār avar karuttuppaṭi,

pala cila eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ tammuṉ tām vantu puṇarumiṭattu lakaram ṟakara voṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyu meṉpataṉi,
		toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ

		palacila veṉumivi tammuṉ tāmvariṉ
iyalpu mikalum akaramēka

akaram vikaṟ pamākalum uḷapiṟa.			   (naṉ. 173)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
ikaravīṟṟuc collākiya iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉi yeccattu ikaram ceyyuḷuḷ ukaramāyt tiriyumeṉpataṉi,
		iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉiyeñciṟuti
niṉṟa ikaram ukaramātal
toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṟ ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.		(tol. 237)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār.
(u-m) uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇkamā koṟkiyōṉē eṉavarum. iṉṟi yeṉpatu pōla aṉṟi yeṉpatum ukaramāyt tirintu varumeṉak koṇṭa naṉṉūlār, attiripiṉiyum, avvaḻi varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikātiyal pātaliyum,
		aṉṟi yiṉṟiyeṉ viṉiyeñ cikaram
toṭarpiṉu ḷukara māyvari ṉiyalpē.			   (naṉ. 173)
eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār.
(u-m) `nāḷaṉṟu pōki eṉavarum.
ceyyuḷiṭattuc cuṭṭup peyarīṟṟu ukaram, aṉṟu eṉpa taṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattu ākāramākat tiritalum, aiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattuk keṭutalum ākiya iyalpiṟṟu eṉpataṉi,
		aṉṟu varukāli yāvākutalum
aivarukāli meyvarintu keṭutalum
ceyyuṇ maruṅki ṉuritteṉa moḻipa.			(tol. 258)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar.
(u-m) `atā aṉṟeṉpa veṇpāyāppē itara aṉṟamma, utā aṉṟamma, eṉavum, atimaṟṟamma iti maṟṟamma, utimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.
iṅṅaṉam aṉṟa varumiṭattuc cuṭṭup peyarīṟṟu ukaram ākāramākat tiriyumeṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiyapaṭi kūṟātu, aṉṟeṉpataṉ akaram nīṇṭu varumeṉa varumoḻit tiripu kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
		atu muṉvarum aṉṟāṉṟān tūkkiṉ.			   (naṉ. 180)
eṉpatu naṉṉūl.
(u-m) atāṉṟu, eṉavarum. naṉṉūlār, karuttuppaṭi atu eṉpata ṉukaram muṟṟumaṟṟorō vaḻi eṉpataṉāṟ keṭṭut takaravoṟṟiṉ mēl varumoḻiyuyirēṟi muṭiyum eṉpatām.
iṉic ceyyuḷiṭattuvarum vēṭki eṉṟa aikāra vīṟṟuccol, taṉakku muṉṉar avā eṉṟa col varu moḻiyāy varappeṟumāyiṉ, avvikāram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa meyyōṭuṅ keṭṭu ṭakāram ṇakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyu meṉpatu,
		ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum
aiye ṉiṟuti avāmuṉ variṉē
meyyoṭuṅ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar
ṭakāra ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.			(tol. 288)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m) `vēṇavā naliya veyyavuyirā eṉa varum. vēṭki yāvatu poruḷkaḷ mēṟṟōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam. avāvāvatu apporuḷkaḷip peṟavēṇṭumeṉṉum āci. eṉavē vēṇavā eṉpataṟku vēṭkiyāluṇṭākiya avāveṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu virittup poruḷ kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 
8. puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal
itu, meyyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum piṟakaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ puḷḷimayaṅkiyal eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. meykaḷ puḷḷi peṟutalāṟ puḷḷiyeṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvaṉa vāyiṉa. puḷḷiyīṟukaḷuḷ ukaram peṟuvaṉa, iṟutikeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iṟutikeṭātu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, ivviraṇṭum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉa, iyalpāy varuvaṉa, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa, urupiyal vitikaḷ, tirintu muṭivaṉa ākiyavaṟṟi ivviyalil virittu uṇarttukiṉṟār.
1. ukaram peṟuvaṉa
		ñakāri yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar
allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟamik kaṇṇum
valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē
ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.			(tol.296)
		ñanamava iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum.			(tol. 297)
ivviru cūttirattum ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṟṟāy niṉṟa toḻiṟ peyarmuṉṉar alvaḻi, vēṟṟumi ākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, avvalleḻuttu varumoḻik kaṇ mikumpaṭi, nilimoḻiyīṟu ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉavum, varumoḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ñanamava mutalāy varumiṭattum nilimoḻikkaṇ ñakarīṟu ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉavum kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār. ivvitikaḷi,
		nakara viṟutiyum ataṉō raṟṟē.
		toḻiṟ peyarellān toḻiṟ peyariyala.
		toḻiṟ peyarellān toḻiṟ peyariyala.
		toḻiṟ peyarellāt toḻiṟ peyariyala.
		toḻiṟ peyarellān toḻiṟ peyariyala.
                                               (tol. 298, 306, 327, 376, 401)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl muṟiyē na, ṇa, ma, la, ḷa eṉṉum aivakip puḷḷiyīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyark kaṇṇum māṭ ṭeṟintu muṭittār.
		vēṟṟumik kukkeṭa akaram niliyum.		           (tol. 299)
eṉpataṉāl, nakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukaram peṟātu akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟār.
(u-m)
alvaḻi
uriñ + kaṭitu = uriñukkaṭitu,
porun + kaṭitu = porunukkaṭitu,
maṇ + kaṭitu = maṇṇukkaṭitu,
tum + kaṭitu = tummukkaṭitu,
pul + kaṭitu = pullukkaṭitu,
tuḷ + kaṭitu = tuḷḷukkaṭitu,
ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu.
vēṟṟumi
uriñ + kaṭumi = uriñukkaṭumi,
porun + kaṭumi = porukkaṭumi,
maṇ + kaṭumi = maṇṇukkaṭumi,
tum + kaṭumi = tummukkaṭumi,
pul + kaṭumi = pullukkaṭumi,
tuḷ + kaṭumi = tuḷḷukkaṭumi,
ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, vaṉmi eṉavarum.
ñaṇanamalaḷa eṉṉum īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarkaḷukku āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ivvitikaḷi ivaṟṟuṭaṉ vakara ṉakara īṟṟuttoḻiṟ peyarkaḷukkum ivvīṟukaḷiṉ ēval viṉikaḷukkum iyitturippar naṉṉūlār.
		ñaṇa namalavaḷaṉa voṟṟiṟu toḻiṟpeyar
ēval viṉi naṉi yavval meyvariṉ
uvvuṟum, ēval uṟācila cilvaḻi.
navviṟu toḻiṟpeyark kavvumām vēṟṟumi.	          (naṉ. 207, 208)
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷ iṅku oppu nōkkat takkaṉavākum.
		īmuṅ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum
āmup peyarum avaṟṟō raṉṉa.
		miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉuṅ kaṉṉum
annāṟ collun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
		valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
		ēṉi vakaran toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē 
		puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
(tol. 328, 345, 373, 382, 403)
eṉavarum tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ, īm, kam, urum, miṉ, piṉ, paṉ, kaṉ, val, tev, puḷ, vaḷ, eṉavarum meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ toḻiṟ peyarpōla alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya iruvaḻiyum ukaram peṟṟu muṭivaṉa eṉa uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ kaṉ eṉṉuñ col vēṟṟumikkaṇ varumāyiṉ maramallāta ekiṉ eṉṉuñ col nilimoḻikkaṇ akaram peṟṟu, valleḻuttu mikku muṭital pōṉṟu akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum. immuṭipu,
		ēṉi ekiṉē akaram varumē
valleḻut tiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum.
		vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ ēṉi yekiṉoṭu
tōṟṟa mokkum kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.		      (tol. 337, 348)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
val eṉṟa colliṉmuṉ, nāy, palaki eṉpaṉa.
varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avviṭattu vitikkappaṭṭa ukaram keṭutalum uṇṭu eṉavum, avvaḻi akaram vantu nilipeṟum eṉavum kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar.
		nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
āvayiṉ ukaram keṭutalum urittē
ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi akara niliyum,			(tol. 374)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
(u-m)
īm + kaṭitu = īmukkaṭitu,
kam + kaṭitu = kammukkaṭitu,
urum + kaṭitu = urumukkaṭitu,
miṉ + kaṭitu = miṉṉukkaṭitu,
piṉ + kaṭitu = piṉṉukkaṭitu,
paṉ + kaṭitu = paṉṉukkaṭitu,
kaṉ + kaṭitu = kaṉṉukkaṭitu,
val + kaṭitu = vallukkaṭitu,
tev + kaṭitu = tevvukkaṭitu,
puḷ + kaṭitu =puḷḷukkaṭitu,
vaḷ + kaṭitu = vaḷḷukkaṭitu,
ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu. eṉa al vaḻiyilum,
īm + kaṭumi = īmukkaṭumi,
kam + kaṭumi =  kammukkaṭumi,
urum + kaṭumi =  urumukkaṭumi,
miṉ + kaṭumi =  miṉṉukkaṭumi,
piṉ + kaṭumi =  piṉṉukkaṭumi,
paṉ + kaṭumi =  paṉṉukkaṭumi,
kaṉ + kaṭumi =  kaṉṉukkaṭumi,
ekiṉ + kāl = ekiṉakkāl,
kaṉ + kuṭam = kaṉṉakkuṭam,
val +kaṭumi = vallukkaṭumi,
val + nāy = vallanāy,
tev + kaṭumi = tevvukkaṭumi,
puḷ + kaṭumi = puḷḷukkaṭumi,
vaḷ + kaṭumi = vaḷḷukkaṭumi,
ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, vaṉmi.
eṉa vēṟṟumiyilum vantaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam ukaram peṟṟum cila viṭattu akaram peṟṟum varum puḷḷiyīṟṟup peyarc coṟkaḷākiya ivaṟṟi āciriyar pavaṇantiyār,
		īmuṅ
kammu murumun toḻiṟpeyar māṉum
mutalaṉa vēṟṟumik kavvum peṟumē.
		maramal lekiṉmoḻi yiyalpum akaram
maruva valimeli mikalumākum.
		miṉ piṉ paṉkaṉ toḻiṟpeya raṉiya
kaṉṉav vēṟṟu meṉmiyō ṭuṟaḻum.
		vallē toḻiṟpeya raṟṟiru vaḻiyum
palaki nāyvariṉum vēṟṟumik kavvumām.
		tevveṉ moḻiyē toḻiṟ peyaraṟṟē
mavvariṉ vaḵkāṉ mavvu mākum.
		puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeyaru māṉum
                                               (naṉ. 223, 215, 217, 231, 236, 234)
eṉavarum iccūttiraṅkaḷāl eṭuttōti muṭittuḷḷār.
īṇṭu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttuppaṭi īmakkuṭam, kammakkuṭam eṉa akkuccāriyi peṟṟaṉavaṟṟip pavaṇantiyār tamatu koḷkik kēṟpa akarac cāriyi peṟṟaṉavākak kūṟiyatum, tev eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟuc col makaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu,
		`tevmuṉi yiṭattuc cēyarkol
eṉa makaramākat tirintuvarum ilakkiyam kaṇṭu `mavvariṉ vaḵkāṉ mavvumākum (236) eṉa ilakkaṇam vitittatum kūrntu nōkkat takkaṉavākum.
2. iṟutikeṭṭu muṭivaṉa.
		verine ṉiṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
varumiṭa ṉuṭittē melleḻut tiyaṟki,
		āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.	      (tol. 300, 301)
ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷ :- verin eṉṉum colliṉ iṟuti yākiya nakaravoṟṟu, tāṉ muṉpeṟṟa akarattoṭum keṭa, valleḻuttu varumiṭattu ataṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttāyiṉum valleḻuttāyiṉum mikku muṭiyum eṉpatām.
(u-m)
veriṅkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam.
veṟikkuṟi, ceyki, tali, puṟam eṉavarum.
		makara viṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ
tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.			(tol. 310)
itaṉporuḷ :- makaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumik kaṇ āyiṉ īṟṟu makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉpatām.
(u-m) marakkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉavarum.
		mellaḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa.			(tol. 312)
itaṉporuḷ:-(nilimoḻiyīṟṟu makaram vēṟṟumikkaṇ keṭṭaviṭattu) varumoḻi valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu mikkumuṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa eṉpatām.
(u-m)
kuḷaṅkari, cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi
kuḷakkari, cēṟu, tātu, pūḻi eṉavarum.
`num eṉṉum viravuppeyar poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇ makaraṅkeṭa melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉpatu.
		nummeṉoru peyar melleḻuttu mikumē.		(tol. 325)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m) nuṅki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavarum.
ivvāṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ nili moḻiyīṟṟu makaram keṭṭu muṭitali,
		vēṟṟumi mappōy valimeli yuṟaḻvum
alvaḻi uyir iṭivariṉ iyalpum uḷa.		(tol. 220)
eṉavaruñ cūttirattāl eṭutturittār naṉṉūlār.
`āyiram eṉṉum makaravīṟṟu eṇṇup peyar muṉ aḷavup peyarum niṟip peyarum vantu puṇarumiṭattu, makaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumiyōṭu ottu iṟuti makaramkeṭa, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉpatu,
		aḷavum niṟiyum vēṟṟumi yiyala.			(tol. 319)
eṉavarum cūttirattāṟ kuṟippiṭap peṟṟatu.
(u-m)
āyiram +kalam= āyirak kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum
āyiram + kaḻañcu = āyirakkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
mēl (tol-eḻuttu 310) `tuvara eṉṟa ilēciṉāl iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ eytiya makarakkēṭu īṇṭuṅ koḷḷap paṭum eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
(u-m)
āyiram + nāḻi = āyiranāḻi, vaṭṭi, akal eṉavarum.
`patiṉāyirakkalam eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu āyiram eṉṟa col aṭiyaṭuttu vantuḻiyum mēṟkūṟiya viti koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar uriyāciriyar.
tolkāppiyattuḷ makara vīṟṟukkuc collappaṭṭa ic ciṟappu vitikaḷi,
		mavvī ṟoṟṟaḻin tuyirī ṟoppavum
vaṉmik kiṉamāt tiripavu mākum.			   (naṉ. 219)
eṉṟa potu vitiyāl taḻuviṉār naṉṉūlār.
`illam eṉṟa marappeyar, īṟṟu makaraṅkeṭṭu, `vici eṉṉum marappeyar pōṉṟa valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ ataṉ kiḷiyoṟṟākiya melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉpatu,
		illa marappeyar vicimara viyaṟṟē.			(tol. 313)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m)
illam +kōṭu = illaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, pū eṉavarum.
		illam tōṟṟāmaram.
makaravīṟṟuc colmuṉ vakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu vakarattiṉ mēl niṉṟa makaram ataṉ toṭarpāl taṉ māttiriyiṟ kuṟukum eṉpatu,
		vakāramiciyu makāraṅ kuṟukum.		           	(tol.330)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl uṇarttappaṭṭatu. `ariyaḷavu kuṟukal makara muṭittē (nūṉmarapu 13) eṉa muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa oru moḻi makarak kuṟukkattuḷ `pōṉm eṉa īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyāy ṉakaram niṉṟu toṭara ataṉ muṉuḷḷa makaram taṉ māttiriyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttiri yātali, `ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum (moḻi marapu 52) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār. atuvēyaṉṟip puṇar moḻikkaṇ vakaram vantu toṭara ataṉmēl niṉṟa makaram kuṟukutal uṇṭeṉpār, `vakāramiciyum makāraṅ kuṟukum eṉa īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.
(u-m)
tarum vaḷavaṉ eṉavum `pōṉm eṉavum ivviruvaki makarak kuṟukkaṅkaḷiyum,
		ṇaṉamuṉṉum vaḵkāṉ miciyumak kuṟukum.		    (naṉ. 96)
eṉavarum cūttirattāl pavaṇantiyār tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṉi, ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ `aḻaṉ eṉṟa colliṉ iṟutiyi luḷḷa ṉakaram keṭa, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉpatu,
		aḻaṉe ṉiṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.		(tol. 354)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m)
aḻaṉ+kuṭam = aḻakkuṭam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavarum.
aḻaṉ - piṇam.
3. īṟu keṭātu mikku muṭivaṉa :
vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ varum ya, ra, ḻa meyyīṟṟup peyarkaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavākum. im muṭipiṉi,
		yakara viṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ
valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē.
		rakāra viṟuti yakāra viyaṟṟē.
		ḻakāra viṟuti rakāra viyaṟṟē.	   (tol. 357, 357, 362, 383)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
(u-m) 
nāy + kāl = nāykkāl, cevi, tali, puṟam
tēr + kāl = tērkkāl, celavu, tali, puṟam	
pūḻ + kāl = pūḻkkāl, cevi, tali, puṟam
mēṟkuṟitta yakāravīṟṟuḷ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉa vitanturikkappaṭṭa `tāy eṉṉuñ colmuṉ makaṉatu viṉiyik kiḷanturikkum valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻivariṉ muṟkūṟiyapaṭi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉpatu.
		makaṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ mutaṉili yiyaṟṟē.		(tol. 359)
eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m)
makaṉṟāykkalām, ceru, tuṟattal, pakittal eṉavarum.
makaṉṟāykkalām - makaṉ tāyoṭu nikaḻttiya kalakam eṉap poruḷ viritturikka. makaṉ viṉi eṉṟatu, īṇṭup pakimēṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
iṉi, rakāravīṟṟuḷ ār, vetir, cār, pīr eṉṟa coṟkaḷum, ḻakāravīṟṟuḷ marappeyarākiya kumiḻ eṉṟa collum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi avaṟṟiṉ kiḷiyeḻuttākiya melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavām. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi,
		ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum
melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
		kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṟ
pīreṉ kiḷaviyō ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.		      (tol. 363, 386)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl uṇarttuvar tolkāppiyar.
(u-m)
ār+kōṭu = ārṅkōṭu cetiḷ, tōl, pū

eṉavarum.
iṉi, yakaravīṟṟuḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ varumoḻi valleḻutti ṉōṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷ cila uḷa eṉpataṉi,
		melleḻut tuṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.		   	(tol.360)
eṉpataṉāl uṇarttiṉār.
(u-m) 
vēy +kuṟi = vēykkuṟi ceyki, tali, puṟam
vēy + kuṟi = vēyṅkuṟi ceyki, tali, puṟam
rakaravīṟṟuḷ melleḻuttu mikumeṉak kuṟippiṭṭa moḻikaḷuḷ `cār eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu `kāḻa eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉpatu,
		cāreṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum.			(tol. 364)
eṉpataṉāl uṇarttappaṭṭatu.
(u-m) cār + kāḻ = cārkkāḻ eṉavarum.

eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.

`kīḻ eṉṉum ḻakara vīṟṟuccol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu valleḻuttup peṟṟum peṟātum uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉpatu,
	 	kīḻeṉ kiḷaviyuṟaḻat tōṉṟum.			(tol. 365)
eṉpataṉāl vitantu kūṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m)

kīḻ + kuḷam = kīḻkuḷam, cēri tōṭṭam, pāṭi
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiya yaraḻa vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipukaḷāka mēṟkāṭṭiya aṉittiyum naṉṉū lāciriyarākiya pavaṇanti muṉivar,

4. iyalpāy varuvaṉa
mūṉṟu tiripum peṟutaliṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷi āciriyar tolkāppiyar patiṉāṟu cūttiraṅkaḷāl eṭutturippar.
meypiṟitātaṟku ēṟpuṭiya ṇaḷaṉala eṉṉum nāṉkīṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ, ṇakara vīṟṟuḷ `āṇ `peṇ eṉavarum viravup peyarum, ōr iṉatti yuṇara niṉṟa `umaṇ mutaliya kiḷip peyarkaḷum, `muraṇ eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarum; ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ `kuyiṉ eṉpatum, ōr iṉatti yuṇara niṉṟa `eyiṉ mutaliya kiḷip peyarkaḷum; `tāṉ, `pēṉ, `kōṉ eṉavarum iyaṟpeyarkaḷum; neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉar varum lakāra ḷakāra īṟṟuṭ cilavum tiriyātu iyalpāvaṉavām. ivi iṅṅaṉam iyalpātali,
		āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇi yiyaṟki.
		kiḷippeya rellām koḷattiri pilavē.
		muraṇeṉ ṟoḻiṟpeyar mutaliyal niliyum.
		                             			(tol. 303, 307, 309)
eṉavum,
		kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum.
		kiḷippeya ṟellāṅ kiḷippeya riyala.
		tāṉum pēṉum kōṉu meṉṉum
ātimuṟi iyaṟpeyar tiripiṭaṉ ilavē,     	(tol. 335, 338, 351)
eṉavum,
		neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumā ruḷavē.
		neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpā kunavum.........................................
pōṟṟal vēṇṭu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.		       (tol.370, 400)
eṉavum varum nūṟpākkaḷ muṟiyē uṇartti niṟṟal kāṇalām.

eṉavum,
pālkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu
kōḷkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu
eṉavum varum.

		tāyeṉkiḷavi iyaṟki yākum.
		alvaḻi yellām iyalpeṉa moḻipa.	      (tol. 358, 361)

		nāykaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu.
av, iv, uv, eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟu vakaram iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇariṉ iyalpām eṉpatu,
		 ēṉavi puṇariṉ iyalpeṉa moḻipa			(tol. 381)
eṉpataṉāṟ pulaṉām
(u-m) avyāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, eṉavarum.
iṉi, kuṟukalun tiritalum peṟutaṟku ēṟpuṭiyavaṟṟuḷ alvaḻiyil varum `ellīrum, `tām, `nām, `yām eṉavarum makaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum-, `tāṉ, `yāṉ eṉavarum ṉakaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum-, nūṟāyiram tāmari veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉa vaṟṟōṭum ēṉi uyirīṟṟuc coṟkaḷōṭum puṇarum `ēḻ eṉṉum ḻakaravīṟṟu eṇṇup peyarum-, uyir mutaṉ moḻi kaḷōṭum yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum evvakit tiripuyiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉa. ivi iyalpātali,
		allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki yākum.

tōṟṟamilli yeṉmaṉār pulavar.		      (tol. 321, 353)

		nūṟūrntu varūum kiḷavikkuk
kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.
		ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti
alpeya reṇṇiṉum āyiyal niliyum.
		ēṉavi puṇariṉ iyalpeṉa moḻipa		(tol. 392, 393, 383)
eṉavum varum cūttiraṅkaḷ viritturippaṉa.

tām+kuṟiya 	=	tāṅkuṟiya, ciṟitu, tīya, periya (vanta, yātta, aṭinta)
nām+kuṟiyam = nāṅkuṟiyam, ciṟiyam, tīyam, periyam

ēḻnūṟāyiram,
