5. cāriyi peṟuvaṉa
		āṇmarak kiḷavi arimara viyaṟṟē.
		ekiṉmara māyiṉ āṇmara viyaṟṟē.
		pīreṉ kiḷavi ammoṭuñ civaṇum.
		pūlvē leṉṟā āleṉ kiḷaviyō
ṭāmup peyarkkum ammiṭi varumē.
		kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṉ
pīreṉ kiḷaviyō ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.
                                             			             (tol. 304, 336, 365, 375, 385)
eṉa varum cūttiraṅkaḷāl muṟiyē āṇ, ekiṉ pīr, pūl vēl. āḻ, kumiḻ eṉa varum peyarccoṟkaḷ am cāriyi peṟum eṉa vitittār. ivaṟṟuḷ pīr - pīrkku eṉṉum koṭi. pūl - pūlā eṉa vaḻaṅkum ceṭi. ēṉiyavi maraṅkaḷākum.
(u-m)
āṇ+kōṭu= āṇaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
ekiṉ+kōṭu= ekiṉaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
pīr+kōṭu= pīraṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
pūl+kōṭu= pūlaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
vēl+kōṭu= vēlaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
āl+kōṭu= ālaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
kumiḻ+kōṭu= kumiḻaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū.
eṉavarum.
vēṟṟumikkaṇ varum `īm, `kam eṉṟa makara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ iraṇṭum, `kōl eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum `tāḻ eṉṉum collum, `tamiḻ eṉṉuñ collum ākiya ḻakaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ iraṇṭum akkuccāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉa. iccāriyip pēṟṟiṉi,
		vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ ēṉi yiraṇṭum
tōṟṟam vēṇṭum akkeṉ cāriyi.
		tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ
akkiṭi varutal urittu mākum.
		tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyum ataṉō raṟṟē.        		(tol. 329, 384, 385)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl vitippar tolkāppiyar.
(u-m)
īm+kuṭam = īmakkuṭam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi
kam+kuṭam = kammakkuṭam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi
tāḻ+kōl = tāḻakkōl, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi
tamiḻ+kūttu = tamiḻkkūttu, cēri, tōṭṭam, paḷḷi.
eṉavarum.
īm, kam, tamiḻ, tāḻ eṉavarum iccoṟkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivataṉi,
		īmum,
kammum urumum toḻiṟpeyar māṉum
mutalaṉa vēṟṟumikku avvum peṟumē.		   (naṉ. 223)
eṉavum,
		tamiḻav vuṟavum peṟum vēṟṟumikkē
tāḻuṅ kōl vantuṟumēl aṟṟē.			   (naṉ. 225)
eṉavum varuñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl naṉṉūlāciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭa `akku eṉṉum cāriyiyip pavaṇantiyār piṉṉuḷḷa oṉṟum uyirmeyyum keṭuttu akaraccāriyiyākak koṇṭami iṅku niṉikkat takuvatām.
veyil, iruḷ eṉṉum lakara ḷakaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ iraṇṭum attuc cāriyiyum iṉ cāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum, iccāriyip pēṟṟiṉi,
		veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum
iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliyal niliyum.		      (tol. 377, 402)
eṉavarum māṭṭēṟṟuc cūttiraṅkaḷ vitittal kāṇalām.
(u-m)
veyil+koṇṭāṉ=veyilattukkoṇṭāṉ.
veyil+koṇṭāṉ=veyiliṟkoṇṭāṉ.
iruḷ+koṇṭāṉ=iruḷattukkoṇṭāṉ.
iruḷ+koṇṭāṉ= iruḷiṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, 				         tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ.
eṉa varum.
makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi iṟuti makaravoṟṟuk keṭṭu, ikaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyar pōṉṟu āṉcāriyiyum av āṉcāriyi mēl attuc cāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum. itaṉik kūṟuvatu,
		nāṭpeyark kiḷavi mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa
attum āṉmici varinili yiṉṟē
oṟṟumey keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 331)
eṉavarum cūttiramākum.
(u-m)
makam+koṇṭāṉ	=	makattāṟkoṇṭāṉ,
		ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ.
eṉavarum,
itaṉkaṇ `attum eṉpatil ummiyi `attu āṉmici yum variniliyiṉṟu eṉa māṟik kūṭṭi, attuccāriyi mēlum piṟacāriyi mēlum varutal nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu eṉap poruḷ kūṟi,
makam+ñāṉṟukoṇṭāṉ  = makattu ñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ. ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ, eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ākāyatti yuṇarttum `viṇ eṉṟa ṇakara vīṟṟup peyar ceyyuḷil varuṅkāl ataṉmuṉ viṉiccol varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattum, taṉittum aṭiyaṭuttum vanta āyiram eṉṉum eṇṇup peyar piṟa eṇkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattum attuc cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉpatu,
		viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ
uṇmiyu murittē atteṉ cāriyi
ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṟ ṟoḻilvaru kāli.
		attoṭu civaṇum āyirat tiṟuti
otta veṇṇu muṉvaru kāli.
		aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum ataṉōraṟṟē.	 (tol. 305, 317, 318)
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ pulaṉām.
(u-m)
viṇṇattuk koṭkum vaṇṇattamarar eṉavum, āyirattoṉṟu, patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, nūṟāyirattoṉṟu eṉavum varum.
6. urupiyal vitikaḷ :
makaravīṟṟuḷ `ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarum, `ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarum, kiḷit toṭarccip poruḷi yuṭiyaṉavāy neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum tām, nām, yām eṉṉum peyarum, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum cāriyi peṟṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum urupu puṇarccikkaṇ muṭittavāṟē muṭiyum eṉpataṉi,
		paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum
toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilik kiḷaviyum
vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum
melleḻuttu mikutal āvayiṉāṉa.			(tol. 320)
eṉpataṉāl māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār.
(u-m)	ellārum + ki = ellār taṅkiyum,
	ellīrum+ki= ellīr nuṅkiyum, ceviyum, 		taliyum, puṟamum.
eṉavum,
tam+ki=taṅki, cevi, tali, puṟam.
nam+ki=naṅki cevi, tali, puṟam.
em+ki=eṅki cevi, tali, puṟam.
eṉavum varum.
`ellām eṉṉum viravup peyar, alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyiṉum urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu vaṟṟuc cāriyiyum iṟuti `um eṉṉum cāriyiyum peṟum. appeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcci yallāviṭattuc cāriyi peṟutal nillātatāy muṭiyum. ellām eṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭi yiṉum kuṟṟamilli. ivvitikaḷi,
		allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
ellā meṉumpeyar urupiyal niliyum
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻic cāriyi niliyātu.
		melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli.		      (tol. 322, 323)
eṉpavaṟṟāl āciriyar vitittuḷḷār.
urupiyal niliyum eṉṟa māṭṭēṟu, alvaḻik kaṇ ummuppeṟṟu niṟṟalum, poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uṇarttiṟṟu, eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
(u-m)	ellām+kuṟiya=ellākkuṟiyavum,
	          ciṟiyavum, tīyavum, periyavum.
eṉavum,
ellām + kōṭu = ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum, ceviyum taliyum, puṟamum (īṇṭuc cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi, makaram vaṟṟiṉmici oṟṟātalāṟ keṭṭatu)
ellām + kuṟiya= ellāṅ kuṟiyavum, ciṟiyavum, tīyavum, periyavum eṉavum varum.
`ellām eṉṉum peyar, uyartiṇiyāy niṟkumiṭattu iṭiyē `nam eṉṉum cāriyiyum iṟutiyil `um eṉṉum cāriyiyum peṟṟu urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy muṭiyum itaṉi,
	 	uyartiṇiyāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum.			(tol. 324)
eṉavarum cūttirattāl uṇarttiṉār.
av, iv, uv, eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ urupu puṇarcciyiṟpōlap poruṭ puṇarcciyilum vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉa. itaṉik kūṟuvatu,
		cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara viṟuti
muṟpaṭak kiḷanta urupiyal niliyum.			(tol. 378)

(u-m)

7. tirintu muṭiyum īṟukaḷ
ṇakāra ḷakāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu īṟṟiluḷḷa ṇakāra ḷakāraṅkaḷ ṭakaramāyt tirintu muṭivaṉa. ittiripiṉi,
		ṇakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
		ḷakāra viṟuti ṇakāra viyaṟṟē.		      (tol. 302, 396)
eṉavaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar kuṟittuḷḷār.
(u-m)
maṇ+kuṭam=maṭkuṭam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum
muḷ+kuṟi=muṭkuṟi, ciṟi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ṉakāra lakāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ, vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu varumoḻiyāy vantu iyipiṉ ṟakaramāyt tirintu muṭivaṉa. ittiripiṉi uṇarttuvaṉa,
		ṉakāra viṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
		lakāra viṟuti ṉakāra viyaṟṟē.		      (tol. 331, 356)
eṉavarum nūṟpākkaḷ.
(u-m) poṉ+kuṭam=poṟkuṭam,cāṭi,tūti, pāṉi eṉavum,

		`ṇaṉavalliṉam varaṭ ṭaṟavum

vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.		(tol. 308)
eṉṟa cūttirattāl āciriyar kuṟittuḷḷār.

(u-m) maraṅkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavarum.

ākum iruvaḻi yāṉumeṉpa.			   (naṉ. 227)

`akam eṉṟa makaravīṟṟuc colliṉ muṉ `ki eṉpatu varumoḻiyāy variṉ, nili moḻiyil muṉṉiṉṟa akaram maṭṭum keṭātu niṟpa, ataṉmuṉ uḷḷa kakara uyirmeyyum iṟuti makarameyyum keṭutal nīkkattakkatu aṉṟeṉavum, avi aṅṅaṉam keṭṭavaḻi avviṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttiṟku ēṟṟa melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉavum kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar.
		akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kimuṉ variṉē
mutaṉili yoḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum
varinili yiṉṟē āciri yarkku
melleḻuttu mikutal āvayiṉāṉa.			(tol. 315)
eṉavarum cūttiram ittiripiṉik kūṟuvatākum.
(u-m)
akam+ki=aṅki eṉa varum.
iccūttirattil `keṭutalum eṉṟa ummiyāl, kakara vuyirmeyyum makara voṟṟum keṭātu niṉṟu, īṟṟiluḷḷa ammakaram, alvaḻiyellām melleḻuttākum (tol. eḻut. 314) eṉṟa vitippaṭi ṅakaramākat tirintu `akaṅki eṉavum varum eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urikaḷāṟ pulaṉām.
`akam eṉṟa colmuṉṉark `ki eṉṟa collēyaṉṟic `cevi eṉpatu variṉum nilimoḻiyiṭiyiluḷḷa kakara vuyir meyyum makara voṟṟum keṭum eṉpar pavaṇantiyār.
		akamuṉarc ceviki variṉ iṭiyaṉa keṭum.		(tol. 222)
eṉpatu naṉṉūlār.
(u-m)
akam+cevi=añcevi eṉavarum.
`añcevi niṟiya mantira mōti eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli.
ivvāṟē `akam eṉpataṉmuṉ `ciṟi eṉpatu variṉum iṭiyaṉa keṭṭu `añciṟit tumpī (kuṟuntoki-2) eṉa varutaliyum iccūttira vitiyāl taḻuvikkoḷvar caṅkara namaccivāyar.
`num eṉṉum makaravīṟṟuc col, alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟuṅkāl, accollil nakara oṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaraṅkeṭa, avvoṟṟiṉ mēl īkāram ūrntu nīm eṉa niṉṟu, avaṟṟiṉ iṭiyilē ōr ikaram vantu nilipeṟṟu nīyim eṉa āki, iṟutiyiluḷḷa makara mey keṭa avviṭattu rakara meyyoṉṟu vantu nīyir eṉat tirintu muṭiyum eṉpar tolkāppiyar. ittiripiṉi,
		allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅ kāli
ukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ īvara
`i iṭi nilii īṟukeṭa rakaram
niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
appāṉ moḻivayiṉ iyaṟki yākum.			(tol. 326)
eṉavarum cūttirattil āciriyar teḷivākak kuṟittuḷḷār.
(u-m)
nīyir kuṟiyīr, ciṟiyīr, tīyīr, periyīr.
eṉavarum `num eṉṟa collē nīyir eṉat tirintatu eṉṉun tolkāppiyar karuttukku māṟāka `nīr eṉṟa collē `num eṉattirintatu eṉap pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyuḷḷami muṉṉark (naṉṉūl 247-ām cūttiram) kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
makara vīṟṟiṉmuṉ akara mutaṉmoḻiyum ākāra mutaṉ moḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu makara oṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaram nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu, nīṭāmiyum urittu eṉpatu,
		akara ākāram varūuṅ kāli
yīṟṟumici akaram nīṭalu murittē.			(tol. 311)

piṟavāṟu varuvaṉa

āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār `ēḻ eṉa itaṉi oṟṟīṟṟu moḻiyākakkoṇṭu puṇarcci viti kūṟiyuḷḷār. naṉṉūlā ciriyar pavaṇantiyār ivveṇṇiṉi ēḻu, eṉa uyirīṟṟu moḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷār. innuṭpam `ēḻaṉ uyirum ēkum ēṟpuḻi (naṉ - uyirīṟṟup - 38) eṉa avar viti kūṟumāṟṟāl naṉku viḷaṅkum. `ēḻ neṭumutal kuṟukum eṉṉum tolkāppiya vitiyi `ēḻ kuṟukum eṉṟa toṭariṟ pavaṇantiyār uṭaṉ paṭṭuḷḷami kāṇalām.
teriyac collappaṭṭa meyyīṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyalpiṉkaṇ, mēl muṭinta muṭipukaḷaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuṭ kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku uriyaṉa piṟavum uḷavāyiṉ avaṟṟiyum iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭaṉ karuti nōkkic cāriyi peṟuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyiyum eḻuttup peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttum koṭuttu ēṟṟavāṟu muṭittuk koḷka eṉpār,
		uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṟ
kaṇṭu ceyaṟkuriyavi kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.	            (tol. 465)
eṉa ivviyalukkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟi muṭittār āciriyar. ivvāṟē pavaṇantiyārum meyyīṟṟup puṇariyaliṉ iṟutiyil,
	 	iṭiyuri vaṭacoliṉ iyampiya koḷātavum
pōliyum marūuvum poruntiya vāṟṟiṟku
iyiyap puṇarttal yāvarkkum neṟiyē.		   (naṉ. 239)
eṉap puṇariyalkaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟi muṭittuḷḷami iṅku oppu nōkki yuṇarattakkatākum.

4. kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟuniṉṟu, poruṭpeyar, eṇṇup peyar ākiya varumoḻikaḷōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ, itu kuṟṟiya lukarap puṇariyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. īreḻuttoru moḻiyāyt taṉineṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum, iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa mūṉṟu mutaliya eḻuttukkaḷālākiya toṭar moḻikaḷiṉ īṟṟilum valliṉa meyyi ūrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram varum eṉa moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. iraṇṭiṟanticikkum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuk kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ ayalil toṭarntuḷḷa eḻuttuvakiyāl, uyirttoṭar, iṭit toṭar, āytat toṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar eṉa aintu toṭarākappakuttu avaṟṟuṭaṉ taṉi neṭiṟ piṉvarum kuṟṟukarattiyum kūṭṭi aṟuvakiyākki, avi uyir, vali, meli, iṭi ākiya nāṟkaṇattōṭum puṇarum iyalpiṉi ivviyalil viḷakku kiṉṟār.
		īreḻut torumoḻi, uyirttoṭar, iṭittoṭar
āytat toṭarmoḻi, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar
āyiru mūṉṟē ukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ.			(tol.406)
itaṉkaṇ, mēl `neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭar moḻi yīṟṟum varum eṉakkūṟiya kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ neṭṭe ḻuttiṉpiṉ varum kuṟṟiyalukaratti `īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉa oṉṟākavum, toṭar moḻiyīṟṟil varum kuṟṟukaratti uyirttoṭar, iṭittoṭar, āytattoṭar, vaṉ ṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar eṉa aintu vakiyākavum pakuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaram varumiṭam āṟu eṉa vakipeṟak kūṟiyuḷḷami kāṇalām.
(u-m)
nāku - īreḻuttorumoḻi
varaku - uyirttoṭar
teḷku - iṭittoṭar
eḵku - āytattoṭar
kokku - vaṉṟoṭar
kuraṅku - meṉṟoṭar
		īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi yiṭittoṭa rākā.		(tol.407)
mēṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷ ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟṟākivarum kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ oruvāṟṟāṉ iṭiyoṟṟuk kaḷōṭu toṭarntuvariṉum avaṟṟiṟku muṉṉē kacatapaṅañana makkaḷuḷ oṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukarattit toṭarntu niṟṟalāl avvāṟu muṉ toṭarnta vaṟṟiṉ toṭarāka avi koḷḷappaṭumallatu iṭittoṭarākak koḷḷappaṭā eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
(u-m)
ārkku, īrkku, noympu, moympu eṉavarum.
		allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
ellā viṟutiyum ukara niliyum.			(tol.408)
iḵtu `iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukumiṭaṉumāṟuṇṭē (eḻuttu 37) eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram arimāt tiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukumeṉa muṉ moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya taṉi vilakki `avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē eṉṟa vitippaṭi ari māttiri peṟṟu niṟṟalē ellāk kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷukkumuriya potuviyalpākum eṉa aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) alvaḻiyic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkaṇṇum āṟīṟṟiṉkaṇṇum kuṟṟiyalukaram ari māt tiriyip peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
iṉi `ellā iṟutiyum ukaram niṟiyum eṉap pāṭaṅ koṇṭu `alvaḻiyic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum niṟintē niṟkum eṉapporuḷ koḷvar iḷampūraṇar.
eṉavē irumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ varum kuṟṟiyalukaram ellāviṭattum oru māttiri peṟṟu niṟkumeṉpatu ivvuri yāciriyar karutteṉat terikiṟatu. ikkaruttup poruntuvataṉ ṟeṉpatu moḻimarapil `iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku meṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil viḷakkappaṭṭatu. aṉṟiyum irumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram orumāttiri peṟumeṉṟārkkuc ceyyuḷiyalil āciriyar kuṟṟukarattāṉ nērpaci niripaci kōṭalum avaṟṟāṉ aṟupatu vañciccīr kōṭalum pattoṉ patiṉāyirattu iru nūṟṟuttoṇṇuṟṟoru toṭi kōṭalu miṉṟāy muṟṟiya lukaramākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭumātaliṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟantaṅku meṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varu vaḻit
tolli yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē. 			(tol.409)
(i-ḷ) valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaram valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyāy varumiṭattu `ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi iccūttirattiṟkup paḻiya arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟku meṉṟu kūṟuvāru muḷar eṉap piṟaratu uriyiṉi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷamiyi nōkkuṅkāl avarkaḷatu karuttaṉṟeṉpatu iṉitu pulaṉām.
		yakaram varumvaḻi ikaraṅ kuṟukum
ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu.			(tol. 410)
itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattu varumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukara muṉṉarum yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu nilimoḻik kuṟṟukaraveḻuttu muṟṟattōṉṟātu āṇṭu ōr ikaram vantu ari māttiri peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokki yātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉavarum.
mēṟ potuvakiyāṟ kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟu iyalpiṉik kūṟiya āciriyar, āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum poruṭ peyarōṭu puṇarum puṇarcci kūṟattoṭaṅki 400 mutal 422 variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟār.
		īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum
vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ oṟṟiṭi iṉamikat
tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.		(tol. 411)
īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟukaravīṟum uyirt toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaravīṟum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ varumāyiṉ, iṉamākiya voṟṟu (taṉṉoṟṟu) iṭiyilē iraṭṭikka, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyu meṉpatām.
(u-m)
yāṭṭukkāl cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavum
muyiṟṟukkāl ciṉi, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.
`tōṟṟam eṉṟataṉāl yāṭu+ñāṟci = yāṭṭu ñāṟci, muyiṟu + ñāṟci = muyiṟṟu ñāṟci, māṭci, yāppu, vaṉmi, aṭivu eṉa ēṉikkaṇattum ivviru toṭariṉkaṇṇum oṟṟiraṭṭittal koḷka.
iṅṅaṉam ivvirumoḻikkaṇṇum niṉṟu iraṭṭuvaṉa ṭakara, ṟakara voṟṟukkaḷē yeṉpataṉi,
		neṭilō ṭuyirttoṭark kuṟṟuka raṅkaḷuṭ
ṭaṟa voṟ ṟiraṭṭum vēṟṟumi mikavē.			   (naṉ. 183)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kuṟippiṭṭār naṉṉūlār.
	 	oṟṟiṭi iṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
attiṟat tilli valleḻuttu mikalē.			(tol. 402)
iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
muṟkūṟiya virutoṭarkaḷuḷ iṉavoṟṟu iṭiyē mikkumuṭiyāta moḻikaḷumuḷa; appakutiyuḷ varumoḻi valloṟṟu mikku muṭitalilli eṉṟavāṟu.
muṟkūṟiya neṭiṟṟoṭar uyirttoṭark kuṟṟukaraṅ kaḷuḷ ṭakara ṟakara mallāta ku, cu, tu, pu eṉṉum nāṉkīṟṟu moḻi kaḷum iṉavoṟṟu mikātaṉavām; aḵtāvatu oṟṟiraṭṭā taṉavām.

varakukāl - ciṉi, tāḷ, patar eṉavum varum.

		iṭiyoṟṟut toṭarum āytat toṭarum
naṭiā iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 413)

eṉṟa cūttirattuṭ kūṟippōntār.
		vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
vanta valleḻut toṟṟiṭi mikumē
melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi melloṟ ṟellām
valloṟ ṟiṟuti kiḷiyoṟ ṟākum.			(tol. 414)
itu piṉṉiṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu. vaṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukaravīṟum meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaravīṟum varumoḻi valleḻuttu iṭiyilē mikku muṭiyum. melloṟṟut toṭar moḻikkaṇiṉṟa melloṟ ṟellām iṟutikkaṭ kuṟṟiyalukaramēṟi niṉṟa valloṟṟākavum ammelloṟṟiṉ kiḷiyākiya valloṟṟākavum tirintu muṭiyum.
iṟuti valloṟṟu - kuṟṟiyalukaramēṟiya oṟṟu. kiḷi valloṟṟu - ṇakarattiṟkuṭakaramum, ṉakarattiṟku ṟakaramum eṉa ivvāṟu puṇarcciyum piṟappum nōkkik kiḷiyāy varumeḻuttu.
(u-m)
kokkukkāl ciṟaku, tali, puṟam eṉavum

eṟpukkāṭu - curam, tali, puṟam eṉavum varum.

		meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuṭcila vēṟṟumiyiṟ

		marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyi.			(tol. 415)
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu. kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟu marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟum.
(u-m)

kamuku+kāy= kamukaṅkāy, teṅku+kāy=teṅkaṅkāy, kam+pul = kampampul, payaṟu+kāy=payaṟṟaṅkāy eṉavaruvaṉa maramallāta pullik kuṟittaṉavāyiṉum ic cūttirattu marap peyarkkiḷavik kuṟṟiyalakarameṉa oruvāṟṟāṉ aṭakkappeṟṟu amcāriyi peṟutal koḷka.
		melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyaru muḷavē.		(tol. 416)
itu meṉṟoṭar moḻikku eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu. mēl 414-m cūttirattiṟ kūṟiyavāṟu melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu marappeyarum uḷa eṉpatām.

	 	īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum
amiṭi varaṟkum uriyavi uḷavē

ēṟ+kōḷ=ēṟṟaṅkōḷ, cūtu+pōr=cūtampōr, vaṭṭu + pōr = vaṭṭampōr, puṟṟu+ paḻañcōṟu =puṟṟampaḻañcōṟu eṉavarum. `ammiṭi varaṟkum eṉpuḻi ummi etirmaṟi yākaliṉ nākukāl, kokkukkāl eṉa amcāriyi peṟātaṉavē perum pālaṉa eṉka. `ammara poḻukum eṉṟataṉāl aracakkaṉṉi, muracakkaṭippu eṉa akkuccāriyiyum valleḻut tum peṟṟu muṭitalum araca vāḻkki eṉa akkuccāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalum, iruṭṭattuk koṇṭāṉ viḷakkattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa attuccāriyiyum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭitalum koḷka. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār `īreḻuttu moḻi eṉṟataṉi naṉṉūlār `neṭiṟṟoṭar eṉa vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.

ituvum avvāṟīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭākiya ciṉiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukaramum yā veṉṉum viṉāvi mutalākavuṭiya meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaramum valleḻuttup peṟutalākiya avviyalpiṟṟiriyātu mikkumuṭiyum.
(u-m)
āṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, īṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkuk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum.
iyaṟki eṉṟataṉāl iruntu koṇṭāṉ, āṇṭu coṉṟāṉ eṉa meṉṟoṭark  kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟu viṉiyeccam iyalpāyum, cettukkiṭantāṉ ceṟṟuc ceytāṉ eṉa vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟu viṉiyeccam mikkum muṭital koḷḷappaṭum.
		yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum.			(tol. 428)
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ yā viṉāvi mutalāka vuṭiya col muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)

		annāṉ moḻiyun tannili tiriyā.			(tol.429)

muṉṉar yā moḻiyeṉṉātu viṉāveṉṟataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṉ iṭapporuṭṭāy muntu, paṇṭu, iṉṟu, aṉṟu, eṉṟu eṉavarum piṟavum iyalpātal koḷka eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.

(i-ḷ) āṟeṉṉumeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram, neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakaravoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) aṟāyiram eṉavarum.
muṉṉar `neṭumutal kuṟukum (eḻuttu - 440) eṉṟa vitippaṭi āṟu aṟu eṉat tirintu niṟkiṉṟa muṟṟukarattiyē īṇṭu `āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟār āciriyar. iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatu tirintataṉ tiripu atuveṉṉuṅ karuttiṉā leṉpar uriyāciriyar. eṉavē īṇṭuk keṭumeṉac collappaṭṭatu aṟu eṉpataṉ kaṇ muṟṟiyalukaramē yeṉpatum āṟu eṉpataṉīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramē yeṉpatum āṟu eṉpataṉīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ meyyīṟu pōlak keṭātu niṉṟu uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukku meṉpatum pulaṉām. itu puṇariyalil `kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉamoḻipa eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil viḷakkap peṟṟami nōkkattakkatākum.
		oṉpā ṉiṟuti urupu tiriyā
tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi marapē.			(tol. 470)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉiṟutik kuṟṟukaram taṉ vaṭivu keṭātu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉavarum.
		nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ  kāli
nūṟa ṉiyaṟki mutaṉilik kiḷavi.			(tol. 471)
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉṉu meṇ aṭiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nūṟāyirameṉṉum aṭiyaṭutta moḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷmuṉ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu oṉṟeṉṉu mutaṉilikkiḷavi nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇōṭu puṇarntāṟpōla vikārameyti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) oru nūṟāyiram eṉavarum.
		nūṟeṉ kiḷavi oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟku
īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikumē.		(tol. 472)
itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇup peyar oṉṟumutaloṉ pāṉkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu īṟāki niṉṟa kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṟ paṟṟap paṭṭa meyyōṭuṅ keṭātu niṟpa acciṉikku iṉamākiya ṟakaravoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nūṟṟoṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatu eṉavarum.
		aviyūr pattiṉum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.			(tol. 473)
(i-ḷ) nūṟeṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟu mutaloṉ pāṉkaḷi yūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattum muṟ kūṟiyavāṟu īṟuciṉi yoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
nūṟṟorupaḵtu, irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu eṉavarum.
`ākum eṉṟataṉāl oru nūṟṟārupaḵtu, iru nūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipuṅ koḷḷappaṭum.
	 	aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyātu
kuṟṟiyalukara mum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyum 
muṟkiḷan taṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 474)
(i-ḷ) nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṟ ṟiriyātu iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum. avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭāmiyum iṉavoṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum `valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē (226) eṉa vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk kūṟiya taṉmiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nūṟṟukkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
`tiriyā eṉṟataṉāṉ oru nūṟṟukkalam, iru nūṟṟukkalam eṉa nūṟeṉpatu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷḷappaṭum.
		oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē
niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.			(tol. 475)
iḵtu oṉṟumutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭum oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟu mutaleṭṭīṟākap patteṉṉumeṇ ēṟi oru collāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇṇup peyarkaḷ oṉṟu mutalākiya oṉpateṇkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattup paḵtu eṉpataṉ āytaṅkeṭṭu āṇṭu iṉavoṟṟākiya oru takaravoṟṟu iṭiyilē mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum varum.
itaṉi naṉṉūlār,
		orupaḵtāti muṉ ṉoṉṟu mutaloṉpāṉ
eṇṇum aviyūr piṟavu meytiṉ
āyta maḻiyavāṇ ṭākun tavvē.			   (naṉ. 196)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār.
		āyiram variṉē iṉṉeṉ cāriyi
āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭi mikuta lilli.			(tol. 476)
(i-ḷ) orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa āyirattōṭu puṇarumiṭattu iṉcāriyipeṟum. avviṭattut takara voṟṟu iṭi mikātu eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orupatiṉāyiram, irupatiṉāyiram, eṇpatiṉāyiram eṉavarum.
		aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā			(tol. 477)
iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭimikātu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orupatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum, kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
`tiriyā eṉṟataṉāl orupatiṟṟukkalam, irupatiṟṟuk kalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram iraṭṭiya ṟakara mātalum, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṉum muṭipiṉkaṇ varumoḻi nakaram tirintavaḻi nilimoḻiyiṉ ṉakarakkēṭumkoḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
		mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
ñanamat tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyiyiṉum
mutaṉili yiyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 478)
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyarip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟeṉṉumeṇṇiṉ muṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻivariṉum ña, na, ma, kkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum yavakkaḷākiya iṭiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉum avvoṉṟumuta loṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ eytiya muṭipu nilimi eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orukal, cuṉi, tuṭi, paṟi, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉavarum.
		ataṉili uyirkkum yāvaru kāliyum
mutaṉili okaram ōvākummē
rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.			(tol. 479)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutal moḻi muṭiyumāṟuṅ mēṟkūṟiya yakaram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉpataṉ muṉṉar uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻi yāy varuṅkālattu am mutaṉiliyiṉ taṉmiyāvatu okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum, rakarattu mēl niṉṟa ukaram muṟṟakkeṭṭu ōr eṉa muṭiyumeṉpatām.
(u-m)
ōraṭi, ōrāṭi, ili, uram, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oḻuṅku, ōli, auviyam, ōryāṉi eṉavarum.
`tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟu eṉṉum vaṇṇum ceyyuḷakattu muṟiyē īr, īraci, īryāṉi eṉavum mū, mūvaci, mūyāṉi yeṉavum mutal nīṇṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭital koḷḷappaṭum.
		iraṇṭumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
vaḻaṅkiyal māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
makara aḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.			(tol. 480)
(i-ḷ) iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇ mutalāka oṉpatīṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vaḻakkiṉ kaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuriya māveṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avveṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭi yeṉṉum makaramutal aḷavuppeyarōṭottu vikārap paṭutalu muṭittu eṉpatām.
ummiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalu muritteṉak koḷka.
(u-m)
irumā, mummā, nāṉmā, aimmā, aṟumā, eḻumā, eṇmā, oṉpatiṉmā eṉavarum. iraṇṭumā, mūṉṟumā eṉavum varum.
vilaṅkumāvi nīkkutaṟku vaḻaṅkiyaṉ mā eṉṟār. ic cūttirattu iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ eṉṟu eṭuttamiyiṉ oṉṟeṉ pataṉ muṉṉar orumā eṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi oṉṟumā eṉṉum muṭipu illiyāyiṟṟu. mikka eṇṇōṭu kuṟinta eṇ varuṅkāl ummit tokiyākavum kuṟinta eṇṇōṭu mikka eṇvaruṅ kāl paṇputtoki yākavum muṭittal āciriyar karuttākum.
		laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic
ceyyuṭ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum
vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.			(tol. 481)
itu, lakāra vīṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟuvar.
(i-ḷ) lakāra ṉakāramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉmuṉ um, keḻu eṉṉuñ cāriyikaḷum piṟa cāriyikaḷum appeṟṟip paṭṭa moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷit toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟumiṭattu vēṟṟumiyik kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ poruḷpeṟa niṟkum eṉpatām.
(u-m)
vāṉavarivillun tiṅkaḷum, kalkeḻukāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē eṉavum, `mānitikkiḻavaṉum pōṉm eṉavum kāṉ keḻunāṭu eṉavum varum.
tuṟikeḻumānti, vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar eṉap piṟa īṟuḷḷum iccāriyi varutal `moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟi eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
aṉṉa marapiṉ eṉṟataṉāl cāriyi kāraṇamākap pūkkēḻūraṉ, vaḷaṅkēḻ tirunakar eṉa valleḻuttup peṟutalum nilimoḻiyīṟu tiritalum cāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekaranīṭciyum koḷḷappeṟum.
`cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṭ piṟacāriyi peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittuk koḷka eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākik
kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉun tōṉṟi
neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccoṟ kiḷaviyum
uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi āyiru maruṅkiṉ
aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyuñ
ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiltoku moḻiyum
tammiyal kiḷappiṉ tammuṉ tāmvarūum
eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
aṉṉavi yellām maruviṉ pāttiya
puṇariyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā.	 	(tol. 482)
mēl nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumākac coṟkaḷip puṇarkkum viti kūṟiya āciriyar, iccūttirattāl aṅṅaṉam niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumākap puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivi yeṉpataṉi eṭutturikkiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) uyireḻuttum puḷḷiyeḻuttum īṟāka niṟpatōr collākik kuṟippiṉkaṇṇum iciyiṉ kaṇṇum paṇpiṉ kaṇṇuntōṉṟi oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmi kuṟinta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, oruvaṉ, orutti, palar, oṉṟu, pala eṉṉum aintupāliyum aṟiyavarum paṇput toki moḻikaḷum, ceyyum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉuṭiya kālaṅ kāṭṭum ummum akaramum orucēra naṭakkum puṭi peyarcci tokkuniṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, tamatu taṉmi kūṟumiṭattu niṟuttacollum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumāy vārātu taṉmuṉṉart tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇuppeyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa attaṉmiyavākiya piṟavumellām ulakattu maruvinaṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyit tam ilakkaṇa mākavuṭiyaṉavām; avi oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmi iṭam viḷaṅkat tōṉṟaveṉṟavāṟu.
uriccol kuṟiccoṟkiḷaviyātalāṉum, karuñ cāṉṟāṉ eṉap paṇputtokiyāyavaḻik kariyāṉ cāṉṟāṉ eṉa marūuvāyt tirintu niṉṟatākalāṉum, kolyāṉiyeṉa viṉittokiyāyavaḻik koṉṟa, kolkiṉṟa, kollum eṉak kālaṅkāṭṭuvaṉa kol eṉat tirintaṉavākalāṉum, tammuṉ tām varumeṇṇup peyarkaḷ niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum aṉmiyāṉum iṅṅaṉam vaḻakkiṭattu marūuvāy vantaṉa niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap puṇarkkappaṭāveṉa āciriyar vilakkiṉār.
(u-m)
viṇviṇittatu, kārkaṟuttatu, ollolittatu eṉavum karumpārppaṉi eṉavum, kolyāṉi eṉavum pappattu eṉavum varum .
		kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 483)
iḵtu ivvatikārap puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) muṉṉar eṭuttōtātaṉavāyc ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu varuvaṉavaṟṟiyum, vaḻakkiṭattē marūuvāyt tirintu varuvaṉavaṟṟiyum, muṟkūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇa muṭipiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ nallaṟiviṉatu ārāycciyāṉ vaḻakki yaṟkaṇ avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭṭi aṟintu naṭattukaveṉak kūṟuvar pulavar.
(u-m)
taṭa eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuriccol `taṭavuttiri eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum `taṭavu nili eṉa ukaram peṟṟum vantatu atu eṉṉum akara vīṟu `atavattukkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ attuy peṟṟatu. kacatapa - tōṉṟiṉ eṉpatu `kacatapattōṉṟiṉ eṉa valleḻuttup peṟṟatu.
naṟā, kurā eṉavarum ākāra vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ `naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi `kuravanīṭiya eṉak `kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉi keṭṭu iru vaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa. ākāra vīṟu piṇā+nāy=piṇavu nāy eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟātum, irā+vaḻaṅku= iravaḻaṅku eṉa ukaram peṟātum vantaṉa. kaḷḷi+kāṭu= kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu eṉa ikaravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ am cāriyi peṟṟatu. tī+aṉṉa = tīyiṉaṉṉa (malipaṭu-145) eṉa īkāravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉcāriyipeṟṟatu. tiru+avar= tiruvat tavar (nālaṭi 57) e ukaravīṟu vēṟṟumik kaṇ attuc cāriyi peṟṟatu. ē+peṟṟa = ēppeṟṟa (cīvaka-2965) eṉa ēkāravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu. ki+uṇṭām pōḻtē= kittuṇṭām pōḻtē (nālaṭi-19) eṉavum, ki+ illar=kittillar (nāṉmaṇi - 69) eṉavum, puṉṉi+kāṉal = puṉṉiyaṅkāṉal eṉavum, mulli+toṭiyal = mulliyantoṭiyal eṉavum aikāravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. kō+il= kōyil eṉa ōkāravīṟu yakaravuṭampaṭu mey peṟṟatu. `kāretir kāṉampāṭiṉēmāka (puṟam 144) `poṉṉan tikiri eṉpuḻik kāṉ, poṉ eṉṉum ṉakara vīṟukaḷ iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa. `vēr piṇi vetirattuk kāl poru naralici (naṟṟiṇi-62) eṉpuḻi vetir eṉṉum rakaravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu. `nāvalan taṇpoḻil (perumpāṇ-465) eṉa lakaravīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. neytal+ciṟupaṟi= neytalañ ciṟupaṟi eṉa lakara vīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu, āyiṭi yirupērāṇmi ceytapūcal (kuṟuntoki - 43) eṉpuḻi `av eṉṉum vakara vīṟu `iṭi eṉṉum urupoṭu puṇarntu `āyiṭi eṉa nīṇṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. tev+muṉi=temmuṉi eṉpuḻi vakaraṅ keṭṭu makara voṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭintatu. a+aṉittum = aṉṟi yaṉittum eṉa akarac cuṭṭu aṉṟi eṉat tirintatu. kōṅkiṉ muki, teṅkiṉpaḻam eṉak kuṟṟukara vīṟu iṉpeṟṟaṉa. ivvāṟē ceyyuḷil tirintu varuvaṉavuḷavēl avaṟṟi yellām ivvatikārap puṟaṉaṭiyāl muṭittuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
`arumaruntaṉṉāṉ eṉaṟpālatu arumaruntāṉ eṉa marūvāy muṭintatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, `aru maruntāṉ eṉpatu rakara vukaraṅ keṭṭu arumantāṉ eṉa muṭintatu  eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟuvar. cōḻaṉ + nāṭu = cōṇāṭu eṉavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ + nāṭu = pāṇṭiyanāṭu eṉavum aṉ keṭṭu muṭintaṉa. toṇṭimāṉ + nāṭu = toṇṭi nāṭu eṉa īṟṟeḻuttuc cila keṭṭum, maliyamāṉ +nāṭu = malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻut toḻintaṉa palavuṅ keṭṭum muṭintaṉa eṉavum, potu + il = potiyil eṉa  ukarantirintu ikaramāy yakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu eṉavum, piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa vellām ippuṟaṉaṭi yāṉ amittuk koḷka eṉavum viti kūṟuvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.
naṉṉūlāciriyarākiya pavaṇanti muṉivar tām vakuttuk koṇṭa vikutip puṇarcci, patap puṇarcci, cāriyip puṇarcci, urupu puṇarcci ākiya nāṉkiṉ puṇarccik kaṇṇum vēṟupaṭa vantavaṟṟi yellām uṇarntu oppunōkki amittuk koḷḷum muṟiyil puṟaṉaṭic cūttiraṅkaḷākac cila nūṟpākkaḷi eḻuttatikārattiṉ iṟutiyil amittuḷḷaṉar.
		itaṟkitu cāriyi yeṉiṉ aḷaviṉmiyiṉ
vikutiyum patamum urupum pakuttu, iṭi
niṉṟa eḻuttum patamum iyaṟkiyum
oṉṟa uṇarttal uravōr neṟiyē.			   (naṉ. 253)
eṉpatu cāriyi paṟṟiya puṟaṉaṭiyākum.
vikuti mutaliya puṇarccikkaṇ iṉṉataṟku iṉṉa cāriyi eṉṟu variyaṟuttu vitittaṉa vaṟṟuḷḷum oḻintaṉa vaṟṟuḷḷum taṉittaṉi yeṭuttuk kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉ avi aḷaviṉṟi viriyumākaliṉ vikutip puṇarcciyiṉiyum patappuṇarcci yiṉiyum urupupuṇarcciyiṉiyum kaṇṭaviṭattu avaṟṟip pakuttu avaṟṟiṉiṭiyē niṉṟa ēkāram akaram mutaliya eḻuttuc cāriyiyiṉiyum aṉ āṉ mutaliya pataccāriyi yiṉiyum ivvirucāriyiyum peṟātu iyalpāy niṉṟa taṉmiyiṉiyum teḷiya aṟivittal aṟiñaratu neṟi muṟi yākum eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ poruḷākum.
urupu puṇarccikkaṇ vititta cāriyi mutaliya muṭipukaḷ av vuruputokka poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum uriyavi yuriyaṉa eṉpatu,
		urupiṉ muṭiyavi okkumap poruḷiṉum
eṉpataṉāl uṇarttappaṭṭatu.			   (naṉ. 238)
pavaṇanti muṉivar tām iyaṟṟiya naṉṉūl karukka nūlātaliṉ ataṉkaṇ vikuti, patam, cāriyi, urupu eṉṉum nāṉkiṉ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vitikaḷ potuppaṭak kūṟap paṭṭaṉavāyiṉum avvitikaḷuḷ ivviti itaṟkup poruntum ivviti itaṟkup  poruntātu eṉa uyttuṇarntu ivviti itaṟkup poruntātu eṉa uyttuṇarntu evviti etaṟkup poruntumō avvitiyi ataṟkuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum eṉa aṟivuṟuttum muṟiyil,
		vikutipatañ cāriyi yurupaṉittiṉum
uritta vitiyiṉōrn toppaṉa koḷalē.		   (naṉ. 265)
eṉap puṟaṉaṭi kūṟiyuḷḷami āḻntuṇarat takkatākum. ic cūttiravuriyil naṉṉū luriyāciriyarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kūrntuṇarttakkaṉavākum.
		iyalpiṉ vikāramum vikārat tiyalpum
uyartiṇi yiṭattu virintun tokkum
viravup peyariṉ virintu niṉṟum
aṉṉa piṟavu mākum aiyurupē.
eṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumikkum,
		puḷḷiyum uyiru māyiṟu coṉmuṉ
tammiṉākiya toḻiṉmoḻi variṉē
valliṉam vikaṟpamum iyalpum ākum.			   (naṉ. 256)
eṉa mūṉṟām vēṟṟumikkum aminta puṟaṉaṭic cūttiraṅkaḷum ivvatikārattuḷ itaṟku itu muṭipeṉṟu vitikkat takuvaṉa vaṟṟiyellām taṉittaṉi variyaṟuttu vitikūṟat toṭaṅkiṉ avi varam piṉṟi viriyumātalāl vakuttu viti kūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi ētuvākak koṇṭu vakuttu viti kūṟāta vaṟṟiyum karutalaḷaviyāl vakuttu viti yurittukkoḷka eṉa aṟivuṟuttum muṟiyil
		itaṟkitu muṭipeṉ ṟeñcā tiyāvum
vitippaḷa viṉmiyiṉ vitittavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ
vakutturi yātavum vakuttaṉar koḷalē.		   (naṉ. 257)
eṉap pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟiya atikārap puṟaṉaṭiyum mēṟkāṭṭiya nūṟpākkaḷukku uriyāciriyarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷum viḷakkaṅkaḷum eḻuttatikārattiṟku aminta puṟaṉaṭi vitikaḷāka iṅku oppunōkki yuṇarntu koḷḷat takkaṉavākum.
		kūṟiya kuṉṟiṉum mutaṉūl kūṭṭit
tōmiṉ ṟuṇartal tollāp piyaṉṟaṉ
āṇiyiṟ ṟamiḻaṟin tōrkkuk kaṭaṉē.
eṉṟār palkāppiyaṉārum.
	piṟcērkki
uyireḻuttukkaḷil kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷukku neṭṭeḻut tukkaḷ piṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟāl otta oliyamippuṭiyaṉa eṉpatum, otta kuṟṟeḻuttillāta aikāra aukāraṅkaḷukku muṟiyē ikara ukaraṅkaḷ iciyāl ottaṉa eṉpatum,
		kuṉṟici moḻivayi ṉiṉṟici niṟikkum
neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
		ai au veṉṉum āyī reḻuttiṟku
ikara ukaram iciniṟi vākum.		         (tol. 41, 42)
eṉavarum nūṟpākkaḷāl uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟē kacaṭatapaṟa eṉṉum valleḻuttāṟukkum ṅañaṇanamaṉa eṉṉum melleḻut tāṟum muṟiyē ottup poruntum oci yuṭiyaṉa ātalāl avaṟṟik kiḷiyoṟṟu eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar tolkāppiyaṉār. ivviru cūttiraṅkaḷiyum aṭiyoṟṟi,
		ai au iuc ceṟiya mutaleḻuttu
ivviraṇṭu ōriṉamāy varal muṟiyē.		     (naṉ. 71)
eṉa aā, iī, kaṅa, caña eṉṟāṅku eḻuttukkaḷi iṉappaṭut tiṉār naṉṉūlār,
		tāṉam, muyaṟci, aḷavu, poruḷ, vaṭivu
āṉa voṉṟāti ōr puṭiyoppu iṉamē.		     (naṉ. 72)
eṉa iṉameṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇamuṅ kūṟiṉār.
eḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappuṇarttum niliyil uyireḻut tukkaḷuḷḷum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷḷum pala eḻuttuk kaḷukkuc cērttup piṟappu oṉṟākac collappaṭṭaṉa vēṉum avi taṉittaṉi eḻuttātaṟkēṟṟa olivēṟupāṭuṭiyaṉa eṉa nuṇuki yuṇartal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉi,
		tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.				 (tol. 88)
eṉavarum piṟappiyaṟ cūttirattil āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār kuṟittuḷḷār. avar kuṟittavaṇṇa eḻuttuk kaḷiṭiyē yaminta ciṟiya vēṟṟumikkuriya oli muyaṟciyākiya kāraṇaṅkaḷi,
		eṭuttal paṭuttal nalita luḻappiṟ
ṟiriyum tattamiṟ ciṟituḷa vākum.			     (naṉ. 88)
eṉavarum cūttirattil paṇavantiyār teḷivākak kuṟit tuḷḷami kāṇalām.
moḻikaḷ tammuṭpuṇaruṅkāl ōreḻuttu niṟṟaṟkuriya niliyil maṟṟō reḻuttup pōliyāy niṟṟaluṇṭu eṉpataṉi,
		ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvu ñāvum
viṉiyō raṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 146)
eṉpataṉāl uṇarttiṉār tolkāppiyar. ivviti yiṉi iṟantatuvilakkal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟip pavaṇantiyār kūṟātu viṭṭaṉarāyiṉum, ivvāṟu moḻiyiṉ mutalilum iṭiyilum payiṉṟuvarum eḻuttup pōlikaḷi etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṉum uttiyāl naṉṉūl 123, 124-ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ kuṟittuḷḷār.
cila viṭaṅkaḷil moḻi mutalilum iṭiyilum akaram niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu aikāram pōliyāy varumeṉpataṉiyum yuṇarttuvatu,
		a ai mutaliṭi yokkuñ cañayamuṉ.			   (naṉ. 123)
eṉṟa cūttiramākum. u-m. mayal miyal eṉavum aracu aricu eṉavum varum.
cila iṭaṅkaḷil aikārattiṉ piṉṉum yakaravoṟṟiṉ piṉṉum iyalpāy nakaram niṉṟa nilikkaḷattu ñakaram pōliyāy varutaluṇṭeṉpatu,
ceyññiṉṟa nīla malarkiṉṟa tillicciṟ ṟampalavaṉ
miññiṉṟa voṇkaṇ malimakaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻntuniṟka
neyññiṉ ṟeriyum viḷakkotta nīla maṇimiṭaṟṟāṉ
kiññiṉṟa āṭalkaṇ ṭāṟpiṉṉik kaṇkoṇṭu kāṇpateṉṉē.
eṉat tirunāvukkaracar aruḷiya tiruviruttattāl aṟiyappaṭum. ivvāṟu cey + niṉṟa = ceynniṉṟa, ceyñ ñiṉṟa eṉavum, mi+niṉṟa=minniṉṟa miññiṉṟa eṉavum puṇar moḻiyiṭi varum pōliyip piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi,
		aikāṉ yavvaḻi navvoṭu cilvaḻi
ñaḵkā ṉuṟaḻu meṉmarum uḷarē.			   (naṉ. 124)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kuṟittār pavaṇanti muṉivar.
taṉatu toḻilic collavarum ākāravīṟṟu viṉā viṉi yuṭiya viṉiccollum, yāveṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, viḷittaliyuṭiya peyarākiya ākāravīṟṟu uyar tiṇic collum valleḻuttu mutaliya vantu puṇaruṅkāl iyalpāy muṭivaṉa eṉa `āvumāvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum eṉavarum 224 ām cūttirattiṟ kuṟittār tolkāppiyaṉār. itaṉi,
		īṟṟiyā viṉāviḷip peyarmuṉ valipiyalpē.		   (naṉ. 160)
eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ pavaṇanti muṉivar kuṟittuḷḷār.
illāmi eṉṉum kuṟippuṇarttum ilam eṉṉum uriccoṟkumuṉ uṇṭātal eṉṉum poruḷuṭiya `paṭu eṉṉum viṉikkuṟippup peyar varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattuc ceyyuḷiṭattu makarak kēṭum tiripumiṉṟi `ilampaṭu eṉa iyalpāy niṟkum. itaṉi yuṇarttuvatu,
		ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kāli
niliyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.			(tol. 316)
eṉṟa nūṟpā. itaṉi innūliṉ 256-ām pakkattuc cērkka.
makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccolliṟuti ṉakaram iyal pātalēyaṉṟit takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟākat tirintu makkaṭki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉavarutalum uritteṉak kūṟuvatu,
		makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.			(tol.404)
eṉṟa nūṟpāvākum itaṉi 253-ām pakkattil tirintu muṭiyum īṟukaḷ eṉṟa talippiṉkīḻc cērkka.
av, iv, uv eṉṉum vakara īṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷiṉ vakaram alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻivariṉ (av+kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya eṉṟāṅku) āytamākat tirintu varum eṉpataṉi yuṇarttum.
		vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.			(tol. 379)
eṉṟa cūttirattiṉiyum, vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ tiripuṇarttiya,
		melleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻut tākum.		(tol. 380)
eṉṟa cūttirattiyum 256-ām pakkat toṭakkattiṟ cērttuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
